<div class="col-sm order-1">
      <h2 class="chapter_title">CHAPTER 171. DRIVERS' LICENSES AND TRAINING SCHOOLS</h2>
      
      <div class="sr" id="stat.171.001"><b>171.001</b> MS 2006 [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/15.001">15.001</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.01">
        <h1 class="shn">171.01 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope.</span></h2>
          <p>Unless the language or context clearly indicates that a different meaning is intended, the following words, terms, and phrases, for the purposes of this chapter, shall be given the meanings subjoined to them.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 50]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 39]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 32]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 45]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 31]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/644/#laws.0.17.0">1971 c 644 s 17</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 43]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 42]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 48]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 28]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 30]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 29]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.14">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 37]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.15">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 47]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.16">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 34]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.17">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 40]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.18">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.</h2>
          <p>(a) (b) [Renumbered subd 49]</p>
          <p class="r">(c) [Renumbered subd 38]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.18a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18a.<span class="headnote">Autocycle.</span></h2>
          <p>"Autocycle" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.011">169.011</a>, subdivision 3a.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.19">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 19.<span class="headnote">Bus.</span></h2>
          <p>"Bus" means every motor vehicle designed for carrying more than 15 passengers, including the driver, and used for the transportation of persons.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.20">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 20.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 41]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.21">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 21.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 46]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.22">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 22.<span class="headnote">Commercial motor vehicle.</span></h2>
          <p>"Commercial motor vehicle" means a motor vehicle or combination of motor vehicles used to transport passengers or property if the motor vehicle:</p>
          <p>(1) has a gross vehicle weight of more than 26,000 pounds;</p>
          <p>(2) has a towed unit with a gross vehicle weight of more than 10,000 pounds and the combination of vehicles has a combined gross vehicle weight of more than 26,000 pounds;</p>
          <p>(3) is a bus;</p>
          <p>(4) is of any size and is used in the transportation of hazardous materials that are required to be placarded under Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, parts 100-185; or</p>
          <p>(5) is outwardly equipped and identified as a school bus, except for type III vehicles defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.011#stat.169.011.71">169.011, subdivision 71</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.23">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 23.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 44]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.24">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 24.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/142/#laws.0.4.0">1993 c 142 s 4</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.25">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 25.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 33]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.26">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 26.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 35]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.27">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 27.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 36]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.28">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 28.<span class="headnote">Commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p>"Commissioner" means the commissioner of public safety of the state of Minnesota, acting directly or through duly authorized agents.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.29">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 29.<span class="headnote">Conviction.</span></h2>
          <p> "Conviction" means a final conviction either after trial or upon a plea of guilty. Conviction also includes: a forfeiture of cash or collateral deposited to guarantee a defendant's appearance in court, which forfeiture has not been vacated; the failure to comply with a written notice to appear in court; or a breach of a condition of release without bail, is equivalent to a conviction.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.29a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 29a.<span class="headnote">Current medical waiver.</span></h2>
          <p>"Current medical waiver" means:</p>
          <p>(1) a medical variance, as defined in Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, section 390.5, that has been granted to the applicant or licensee by the Federal Motor Carrier Safety Administration and that is not expired, removed, or rescinded;</p>
          <p>(2) a waiver of physical qualifications that has been granted to the applicant or licensee by the commissioner under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.321#stat.171.321.2">171.321, subdivision 2</a>, and rules adopted under that section, and that is not expired or revoked; or</p>
          <p>(3) a waiver of physical qualifications that has been granted to the applicant or licensee by the commissioner of transportation under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/221.0314#stat.221.0314.3">221.0314, subdivision 3</a> or 3a, or rules adopted under that section, and that is not expired or revoked.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.30">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 30.<span class="headnote">Department.</span></h2>
          <p>"Department" means the Department of Public Safety of the state, acting directly or through its duly authorized officers and agents.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.31">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 31.<span class="headnote">Driver.</span></h2>
          <p>"Driver" means every person, who drives or is in actual physical control of a motor vehicle.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.31a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 31a.<span class="headnote">Enhanced driver's license.</span></h2>
          <p>"Enhanced driver's license" means a license, instruction permit, or provisional license to operate a motor vehicle issued or issuable under the laws of this state by the commissioner of public safety that denotes citizenship and identity and contains technology and security features approved by the secretary of the United States Department of Homeland Security. An enhanced driver's license may be used in the same manner as a driver's license, instruction permit, or provisional license, and is approved by the secretary of the United States Department of Homeland Security for purposes of entering the United States. All provisions in this chapter relating to drivers' licenses, instruction permits, and provisional licenses, including cancellation, suspension, revocation, reinstatement, examination, restriction, expiration, renewal, and unlawful acts and violations, apply to an enhanced driver's license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.31b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 31b.<span class="headnote">Enhanced identification card.</span></h2>
          <p>"Enhanced identification card" means an identification card issued or issuable under the laws of this state by the commissioner of public safety that denotes citizenship and identity and contains technology and security features approved by the secretary of the United States Department of Homeland Security. An enhanced identification card may be used in the same manner as an identification card and is approved by the secretary of the United States Department of Homeland Security for purposes of entering the United States.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.32">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 32.<span class="headnote">Farm tractor.</span></h2>
          <p>"Farm tractor" means every motor vehicle designed and used primarily as a farm implement for drawing plows, mowing machines and other implements of husbandry.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.33">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 33.<span class="headnote">Farm truck.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of this chapter only, "farm truck" means a single-unit truck, including a pickup truck as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/168.002">168.002</a>; truck-tractor; tractor; semitrailer; or trailer, used by its owner:</p>
          <p>(1) to transport from the farm to the market agricultural, horticultural, dairy, or other farm products, including livestock, produced or finished by the owner of the farm truck;</p>
          <p>(2) to transport the owner's other personal property from the farm to market; or</p>
          <p>(3) to transport property and supplies to the farm of the owner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.33a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 33a.<span class="headnote">Full-service provider.</span></h2>
          <p>"Full-service provider" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/168.002#stat.168.002.12a">168.002, subdivision 12a</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.34">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 34.<span class="headnote">Gross vehicle weight.</span></h2>
          <p>"Gross vehicle weight" means the greater of:</p>
          <p>(1) the unloaded weight of a vehicle or the unloaded weight of a truck-tractor and semitrailer combination, plus the weight of the load; or</p>
          <p>(2) the value specified by the manufacturer as the maximum gross weight or gross vehicle weight rating.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.35">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 35.<span class="headnote">Hazardous materials.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Hazardous materials" means any material that has been designated as hazardous under United States Code, title 49, section 5103, and is required to be placarded under Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, part 172, subpart F, or any quantity of a material listed as a select agent or toxin in Code of Federal Regulations, title 42, part 73.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.36">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 36.<span class="headnote">Head Start bus.</span></h2>
          <p>"Head Start bus" means a motor vehicle used to transport children or parents to or from a Head Start facility, or to or from Head Start-related activities, by the Head Start grantee, or by someone under an agreement with the Head Start grantee. A Head Start bus does not include a motor vehicle transporting children or parents to or from a Head Start facility for which parents or guardians receive direct compensation from a Head Start grantee, a motor coach operating under charter carrier authority, or a transit bus providing services as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/174.22#stat.174.22.7">174.22, subdivision 7</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.36a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 36a.<span class="headnote">Interstate or foreign commerce.</span></h2>
          <p>"Interstate or foreign commerce" means (1) any trade, traffic, or transportation within the jurisdiction of the United States between a place in a state and a place outside of that state, including a place outside of the United States, and (2) trade, traffic, and transportation in the United States that affects any trade, traffic, and transportation described in clause (1).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.36b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 36b.<span class="headnote">Intrastate commerce.</span></h2>
          <p>"Intrastate commerce" means any trade, traffic, or transportation that occurs entirely within the state of Minnesota and that is not interstate or foreign commerce.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.37">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 37.<span class="headnote">License.</span></h2>
          <p>"License" means any operator's license or any other license or permit to operate a motor vehicle issued or issuable under the laws of this state by the commissioner of public safety including:</p>
          <p>(1) any temporary license, instruction permit, or provisional license;</p>
          <p>(2) the privilege of any person to drive a motor vehicle whether or not the person holds a valid license; and</p>
          <p>(3) any nonresident's operating privilege.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.37a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 37a.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 31a]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.37b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 37b.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 31b]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.38">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 38.<span class="headnote">Manufactured home.</span></h2>
          <p>"Manufactured home" means any trailer or semitrailer which is designed, constructed, and equipped for use as a human dwelling place, living abode, or living quarters except travel trailers.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.38a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 38a.<span class="headnote">Medical examiner.</span></h2>
          <p>"Medical examiner" has the meaning given in Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, section 390.5.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.39">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 39.<span class="headnote">Motor vehicle.</span></h2>
          <p>"Motor vehicle" means every vehicle that is self-propelled, other than an electric personal assistive mobility device as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.011#stat.169.011.26">169.011, subdivision 26</a>, and any vehicle propelled or drawn by a self-propelled vehicle, and not deriving its power from overhead wires except snowmobiles.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.40">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 40.<span class="headnote">Motorcycle.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Motorcycle" means every motor vehicle having a seat or saddle for the use of the rider and designed to travel on not more than three wheels in contact with the ground. Motorcycle includes motor scooters and bicycles with motor attached.</p>
          <p>(b) Motorcycle excludes tractors, motorized bicycles, and roadable aircraft, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.011#stat.169.011.67a">169.011, subdivision 67a</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.41">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 41.<span class="headnote">Motorized bicycle.</span></h2>
          <p>"Motorized bicycle" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.011#stat.169.011.45">169.011, subdivision 45</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.41a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 41a.<span class="headnote">Noncompliant license; noncompliant identification card.</span></h2>
          <p>"Noncompliant license," "noncompliant identification card," or "noncompliant license or identification card," means a driver's license or a Minnesota identification card issued under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.019#stat.171.019.2">171.019, subdivision 2,</a> paragraph (b). Unless provided otherwise, noncompliant license includes an appropriate instruction permit, provisional license, limited license, and restricted license.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.42">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 42.<span class="headnote">Nonresident.</span></h2>
          <p>"Nonresident" means every person who is not a resident of this state.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.43">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 43.<span class="headnote">Owner.</span></h2>
          <p>"Owner" means any person, firm, copartnership, association, or corporation who holds the legal title to a vehicle, or in the event a vehicle is the subject of an agreement for the conditional sale or lease thereof with the right of purchase upon performance of the conditions stated in the agreement and with an immediate right of possession vested in the conditional vendee or lessee, or in the event a mortgagor of a vehicle is entitled to possession, then such conditional vendee or lessee or mortgagor shall be deemed the owner for the purposes of this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.44">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 44.<span class="headnote">Passenger endorsement.</span></h2>
          <p>"Passenger endorsement" means the driver's license endorsement required of a person who operates a vehicle designed to transport more than 15 passengers, including the driver.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.45">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 45.<span class="headnote">Person.</span></h2>
          <p>"Person" means every natural person, firm, copartnership, association, or corporation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.45a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 45a.<span class="headnote">Primary driving supervisor.</span></h2>
          <p>"Primary driving supervisor" means a person who: (1) supervises, for a majority of the supervised driving time, motor vehicle operation by an instruction permit holder who is under age 18; and (2) meets the applicable supervision requirements under this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.45b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 45b.<span class="headnote">REAL ID Act.</span></h2>
          <p>"REAL ID Act" means the REAL ID Act of 2005, Public Law 109-13, Division B.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.45c">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 45c.<span class="headnote">REAL ID compliant license; REAL ID compliant identification card.</span></h2>
          <p>"REAL ID compliant license," "REAL ID compliant identification card," or "REAL ID compliant license or identification card" means a driver's license or a Minnesota identification card issued under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.019#stat.171.019.2">171.019, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (a). Unless provided otherwise, REAL ID compliant license includes an appropriate instruction permit, provisional license, limited license, and restricted license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.45d">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 45d.<span class="headnote">Residence address and permanent mailing address.</span></h2>
          <p>"Residence address" and "permanent mailing address" mean, for purposes of a driver's license or Minnesota identification card, the postal address of the permanent domicile within this state where an individual:</p>
          <p>(1) resides;</p>
          <p>(2) intends to reside within 30 calendar days after the date of application; or</p>
          <p>(3) intends to return whenever absent.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.46">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 46.<span class="headnote">School bus.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"School bus" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.011#stat.169.011.71">169.011, subdivision 71</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.47">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 47.<span class="headnote">State.</span></h2>
          <p>"State" means a state of the United States, the District of Columbia, Puerto Rico, the United States Virgin Islands, or any territory or insular possession subject to the jurisdiction of the United States, including a United States military base located on foreign soil.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.48">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 48.<span class="headnote">Street or highway.</span></h2>
          <p>"Street" or "highway" means the entire width between property lines of every way or place of whatever nature when any part thereof is open to the use of the public, as a matter of right, for purpose of vehicular traffic.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.48a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 48a.<span class="headnote">Tank vehicle.</span></h2>
          <p>"Tank vehicle" means any commercial motor vehicle that is designed to transport any liquid or gaseous materials within a tank, as defined in Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, section 178.320, including a cargo tank or a portable tank as defined in Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, section 171.8, that is either permanently or temporarily attached to the vehicle or the chassis, except portable tanks having a rated capacity under 1,000 gallons.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.48b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 48b.<span class="headnote">Temporary lawful status.</span></h2>
          <p>"Temporary lawful status" has the meaning given in Code of Federal Regulations, title 6, section 37.3.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.48c">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 48c.<span class="headnote">Third-party tester.</span></h2>
          <p>"Third-party tester" means an individual who is an employee of a third-party testing program who has qualified for a third-party tester certificate issued by the commissioner granting the individual authorization to conduct road tests or skills tests.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.48d">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 48d.<span class="headnote">Third-party testing program.</span></h2>
          <p>"Third-party testing program" means a program authorized by the commissioner to administer to an individual the road test or skills test.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.48e">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 48e.<span class="headnote">Temporary mailing address.</span></h2>
          <p>"Temporary mailing address" means the mailing address of any place where a person regularly or occasionally stays and may receive mail in their name other than the person's residence address. A temporary mailing address does not include the designated address under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/5B.05">5B.05</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.49">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 49.<span class="headnote">Travel trailer.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Travel trailer" means any trailer or semitrailer designed and used for human living quarters that meets all of the following qualifications:</p>
          <p>(1) is not used as the residence of the owner or occupant;</p>
          <p>(2) is used for temporary living quarters by the owner or occupant while engaged in recreational or vacation activities; and</p>
          <p>(3) is towed on the public streets or highways incidental to such recreational or vacation activities.</p>
          <p>(b) The term "travel trailer" shall not include bunkhouses, so called, temporarily mounted on trailers, and manufactured homes. Such bunkhouses, exclusive of the trailer and manufactured homes, shall be listed and taxed as personal property as provided by law.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.49a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 49a.<span class="headnote">Valid license; valid driver's license.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Valid license," "valid driver's license," "valid Minnesota driver's license," "valid standard driver's license," or other similar term, means any operator's license, provisional license, temporary license, limited license, permit, or other license to operate a motor vehicle issued or issuable under the laws of this state by the commissioner, or by another state or jurisdiction if specified, that is:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) not expired, suspended, revoked, or canceled; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) not disqualified for the class of vehicle being operated.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.49b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 49b.<span class="headnote">Valid medical examiner's certificate.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Valid medical examiner's certificate" means a record, on a form prescribed by the department:</p>
          <p>(1) of a medical examiner's examination of a person who holds or is applying for a class A, class B, or class C commercial driver's license;</p>
          <p>(2) upon which the medical examiner attests that the applicant or license holder is physically qualified to drive a commercial motor vehicle; and</p>
          <p>(3) that is not expired.</p>
          <p>(b) A valid medical examiner's certificate must be issued by a medical examiner who is certified by the Federal Motor Carrier Administration and listed on the National Registry of Certified Medical Examiners.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.50">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 50.<span class="headnote">Vehicle.</span></h2>
          <p>"Vehicle" means every device in, upon, or by which any person or property is or may be transported or drawn upon any highway, excepting devices moved by human power or used exclusively upon stationary rails or tracks.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.01.51">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 51.<span class="headnote">Veteran with a total service-connected disability.</span></h2>
          <p>"Veteran with a total service-connected disability" means a veteran, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/197.447">197.447</a>, who provides to the commissioner satisfactory evidence that: (1) is issued by the Department of Veterans Affairs, the United States Veterans Administration, or the retirement board of one of the several branches of the armed forces; and (2) demonstrates that the veteran has received a 100 percent total and permanent service-connected disability rating.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.01.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/13">2720-142</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/13">2720-145a</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/401/#laws.0.1.0">1939 c 401 s 1</a>,16, par (c);       <p><b>NOTE: </b>(a) Subdivisions 41a, 45b, and 48b, as added by Laws 2017, chapter 76, sections 1 to 3, were effective May 19, 2017, and apply to driver's licenses and Minnesota identification cards applied for and issued on or after October 1, 2018. Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 29.</p>
      <p>(b) If the Federal REAL ID Act, Public Law 109-13, Division B, is repealed, or if the definition of "official purpose" in Code of Federal Regulations, title 6, part 37, is amended, subdivisions 41a, 45b, and 48b, as added by Laws 2017, chapter 76, sections 1 to 3, are repealed on June 30 of the following year. Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 27.</p>
      <h1 class="fgh">DRIVERS' LICENSES</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.015">
        <h1 class="shn">171.015 DRIVER'S LICENSE DIVISION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.015.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Created; director.</span></h2>
          <p>A division in the Department of Public Safety to be known as the Driver's License Division is hereby created, under the supervision and control of a director. The commissioner may place the director's position in the unclassified service if the position meets the criteria established in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/43A.08#stat.43A.08.1a">43A.08, subdivision 1a</a>. The director shall be assigned the duties and responsibilities prescribed in this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.015.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Powers and duties transferred.</span></h2>
          <p>All the powers and duties now vested in or imposed upon the Department of Transportation and the commissioner of transportation in regard to drivers' licensing and safety responsibility as prescribed by this chapter and chapters 169 and 170, are hereby transferred to, vested in, and imposed upon the commissioner of public safety. The duties and responsibilities of the Department of Transportation and the commissioner of transportation, in relation to such matters as heretofore constituted, are hereby abolished.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.015.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Licensing chauffeur and school bus driver.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of public safety, with the approval of the governor, may transfer and assign to the Driver's License Division duties and responsibilities in relation to chauffeurs' licensing and school bus drivers' licensing as vested in and imposed upon the Division of Motor Vehicles.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.015.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/199/#laws.1.59.0">1991 c 199 art 1 s 59</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.015.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Powers and duties transferred.</span></h2>
          <p>All the powers and duties now vested in or imposed upon the Department of Education and the Department of Transportation relating to drivers' training as prescribed by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.04">171.04</a>, are hereby transferred to, vested in, and imposed upon the commissioner of public safety. The duties of the Department of Education and the Department of Transportation with reference to such training as heretofore constituted are hereby abolished.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.015.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Facility for licensing activity.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of transportation shall provide space as required for driver and chauffeur license activities at such locations and under such contractual conditions as may be determined with the commissioner of public safety.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.015.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/13/#laws.1.12.0">2023 c 13 art 1 s 12</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.015.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/1129/#laws.1.18.0">1969 c 1129 art 1 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/166/#laws.0.7.0">1976 c 166 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/560/#laws.0.50.0">1982 c 560 s 50</a>; 1986 c 444; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/95/#laws.3.5.0">2017 c 95 art 3 s 5</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.017">
        <h1 class="shn">171.017 BACKGROUND INVESTIGATIONS; DRIVER'S LICENSE AGENTS AND CERTAIN EMPLOYEES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.017.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Background checks authorized.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner must investigate the criminal history background of any driver's license agent and any current or prospective employees of the department or driver's license agent being considered for any position that has or will have the ability to:</p>
          <p>(1) create or modify records of applicants for identification cards and driver's licenses, including enhanced driver's licenses and enhanced identification cards;</p>
          <p>(2) issue enhanced driver's licenses or enhanced identification cards; or</p>
          <p>(3) administer knowledge or skills tests under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.13">171.13</a> to an applicant for a commercial driver's license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.017.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Procedure.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner must request a criminal history background check from the superintendent of the Bureau of Criminal Apprehension on all individuals specified in subdivision 1. A request under this section must be accompanied by an executed criminal history consent form, including fingerprints, signed by the agent or the current or prospective employee being investigated.</p>
          <p>(b) After receiving a request under paragraph (a), the superintendent of the Bureau of Criminal Apprehension shall perform the background check required under subdivision 1. The superintendent shall retrieve criminal history data as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.87">13.87, </a>conduct a search of the national criminal records repository, and provide wants and warrant information from federal and state repositories. The superintendent is authorized to exchange fingerprints with the Federal Bureau of Investigation for purposes of the criminal history check. The superintendent shall return the results of the background checks to the commissioner to determine whether:</p>
          <p>(1) the agent, employee, or applicant for employment specified in subdivision 1, clause (1) or (2), has committed a disqualifying crime under Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, section 1572.103; or</p>
          <p>(2) the employee or applicant for employment specified in subdivision 1, clause (3), has a conviction of the type specified by Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, section 384.228(j).</p>
          <p>(c) The superintendent shall recover the bureau's background check cost for the person on whom the background check is performed through a fee charged to the commissioner or the driver's license agent who requested the background check.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.017.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Notification by other criminal justice agencies.</span></h2>
          <p>Criminal justice agencies, as defined by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.02#stat.13.02.3a">13.02, subdivision 3a</a>, shall provide the commissioner with information they possess and that the commissioner requires for the purposes of determining the employment suitability of current or prospective employees subject to this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.017.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Annual background checks in certain instances.</span></h2>
          <p>Consistent with Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, section 384.228, the commissioner shall request and the superintendent shall conduct annual background checks for the department employees specified in subdivision 1, clause (3). Annual background checks under this subdivision must be performed in a manner consistent with subdivisions 2 and 3.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.017.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/127/#laws.0.44.0">2013 c 127 s 44</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/76/#laws.0.4.0">2017 c 76 s 4</a></p>
      </div>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>(a) The amendment to this section by Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 4, was effective May 19, 2017, and applies to driver's licenses and Minnesota identification cards applied for and issued on or after October 1, 2018. Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 29.</p>
      <p>(b) If the Federal REAL ID Act, Public Law 109-13, Division B, is repealed, or if the definition of "official purpose" in Code of Federal Regulations, title 6, part 37, is amended, the amendment to this section by Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 4, is repealed on June 30 of the following year. Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 27.</p>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.019">
        <h1 class="shn">171.019 REAL ID ACT CONFORMITY; LIMITATIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.019.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definition.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of this section, "federal change" means a modification or addition to REAL ID Act requirements, made by the federal government after May 19, 2017, with respect to: legal requirements; processes; policies and procedures; or data collection, storage, and dissemination. Federal change includes but is not limited to a modification:</p>
          <p>(1) in what constitutes an official purpose under Code of Federal Regulations, title 6, part 37;</p>
          <p>(2) in the machine-readable technology standards for a license or Minnesota identification card;</p>
          <p>(3) in the information provided on the face of the license or Minnesota identification card;</p>
          <p>(4) that relates to dissemination of state-provided data to or among federal agencies, other states, organizations operating under agreement among the states, or private entities; or</p>
          <p>(5) that imposes an identifiable cost for the state of Minnesota.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.019.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">License and Minnesota identification card options.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner must establish a license and a Minnesota identification card that meets the requirements of the REAL ID Act, including but not limited to documentation requirements, administrative processes, electronic validation or verification of data, and card design and marking, as provided under this chapter.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner must establish a license and Minnesota identification card that:</p>
          <p>(1) is not acceptable for federal identification under the REAL ID Act, but complies with Code of Federal Regulations, title 6, section 37.71; and</p>
          <p>(2) meets the requirements under this chapter, including but not limited to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.12">171.12</a>, subdivision 7b.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner must establish an enhanced driver's license or enhanced identification card as provided in this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.019.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Limitations.</span></h2>
          <p>Compliance under subdivision 2, paragraph (a), is limited to those requirements of the REAL ID Act and any rules or regulations promulgated pursuant to the REAL ID Act in effect as of May 19, 2017. The commissioner may not take any action to implement or meet the requirements of a federal change.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.019.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Legislative notification.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Upon identification of an impending or completed federal change, the commissioner must notify the chairs and ranking minority members of the legislative committees having jurisdiction over transportation policy and finance, public safety, and data practices, and the Legislative Commission on Data Practices and Personal Data Privacy. Notification must be submitted as required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/3.195">3.195</a>, except that printed copies are not required.</p>
          <p>(b) Notification under this subdivision must include a review of the federal change, an initial analysis of data practices impacts, and any preliminary estimates of implementation costs, including the availability of additional federal funds.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.019.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Statutory construction.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Unless specifically provided otherwise, a driver's license, instruction permit, or provisional license includes any noncompliant license. Unless specifically provided otherwise, a Minnesota identification card includes any noncompliant identification card.</p>
          <p>(b) A noncompliant license does not include an enhanced driver's license, and a noncompliant identification card does not include an enhanced identification card.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.019.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/76/#laws.0.5.0">2017 c 76 s 5</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>(a) This section, as added by Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 5, was effective May 19, 2017, and applies to driver's licenses and Minnesota identification cards applied for and issued on or after October 1, 2018. Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 29.</p>
      <p>(b) If the Federal REAL ID Act, Public Law 109-13, Division B, is repealed, or if the definition of "official purpose" in Code of Federal Regulations, title 6, part 37, is amended, this section, as added by Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 5, is repealed on June 30 of the following year. Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 27.</p>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.02">
        <h1 class="shn">171.02 LICENSES; TYPES, ENDORSEMENTS, RESTRICTIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.02.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">License required; duplicate identification restricted.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Except when expressly exempted, a person shall not drive a motor vehicle upon a street or highway in this state unless the person has a valid license under this chapter for the type or class of vehicle being driven.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The department shall not issue a driver's license to a person unless and until the person's license from any jurisdiction has been invalidated. The department shall provide to the issuing department of any jurisdiction, information that the licensee is now licensed in Minnesota. A person is not permitted to have more than one valid driver's license at any time. The department shall not issue to a person to whom a current Minnesota identification card has been issued a driver's license, other than a limited license, unless the person's Minnesota identification card has been invalidated. This subdivision does not require invalidation of a tribal identification card as a condition of receiving a driver's license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.02.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Driver's license classifications, endorsements, exemptions.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Drivers' licenses are classified according to the types of vehicles that may be driven by the holder of each type or class of license. The commissioner may, as appropriate, subdivide the classes listed in this subdivision and issue licenses classified accordingly.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Except as provided in this subdivision and subdivision 2a, no class of license is valid to operate a motorcycle, school bus, tank vehicle, double-trailer or triple-trailer combination, vehicle transporting hazardous materials, or bus, unless so endorsed. There are four general classes of licenses as described in paragraphs (c) through (f).</p>
          <p class="in">(c) Class D drivers' licenses are valid for:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) operating all farm trucks if the farm truck is:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) controlled and operated by a farmer, including operation by an immediate family member or an employee of the farmer;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) used to transport agricultural products, farm machinery, or farm supplies, including hazardous materials, to or from a farm;</p>
          <p class="in">(iii) not used in the operations of a common or contract motor carrier as governed by Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, part 365; and</p>
          <p class="in">(iv) used within 150 miles of the farm;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) notwithstanding paragraph (b), operating an authorized emergency vehicle, as defined in section 169.011, subdivision 3, whether or not in excess of 26,000 pounds gross vehicle weight;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) operating a recreational vehicle as defined in section 168.002, subdivision 27, that is operated for personal use;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) operating all single-unit vehicles except vehicles with a gross vehicle weight of more than 26,000 pounds, vehicles designed to carry more than 15 passengers including the driver, and vehicles that carry hazardous materials;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) notwithstanding paragraph (d), operating a type A school bus or a multifunction school activity bus without a school bus endorsement if the requirements of subdivision 2a are satisfied, as determined by the commissioner;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) operating any vehicle or combination of vehicles when operated by a licensed peace officer while on duty;</p>
          <p class="in">(7) towing vehicles if:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) the towed vehicles have a gross vehicle weight of 10,000 pounds or less; or</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) the towed vehicles have a gross vehicle weight of more than 10,000 pounds and the combination of vehicles has a gross vehicle weight of 26,000 pounds or less;</p>
          <p class="in">(8) operating a covered farm vehicle as defined in Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, section 390.5, that is not carrying hazardous materials of a type or quantity that requires the vehicle to be placarded in accordance with Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, section 172.504; and</p>
          <p class="in">(9) operating an autocycle.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) Class C drivers' licenses are valid for:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) operating class D motor vehicles;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) with a hazardous materials endorsement, operating class D vehicles to transport hazardous materials;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) with a passenger endorsement, operating buses; and</p>
          <p class="in">(4) with a passenger endorsement and school bus endorsement, operating school buses.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) Class B drivers' licenses are valid for:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) operating all class C motor vehicles, class D motor vehicles, and all other single-unit motor vehicles including, with a passenger endorsement, buses; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) towing only vehicles with a gross vehicle weight of 10,000 pounds or less.</p>
          <p class="in">(f) Class A drivers' licenses are valid for operating any vehicle or combination of vehicles.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.02.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Exception for certain school bus drivers.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Notwithstanding subdivision 2, paragraph (b), the holder of a class D driver's license, without a school bus endorsement, may operate a type A school bus or a multifunction school activity bus under the following conditions:</p>
          <p class="in">(a) The operator is an employee of the entity that owns, leases, or contracts for the school bus and is not solely hired to provide transportation services under this subdivision.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The operator drives the school bus only from points of origin to points of destination, not including home-to-school trips to pick up or drop off students.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The operator is prohibited from using the eight-light system. Violation of this paragraph is a misdemeanor.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) The operator's employer has adopted and implemented a policy that provides for annual training and certification of the operator in:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) safe operation of the type of school bus the operator will be driving;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) understanding student behavior, including issues relating to students with disabilities;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) encouraging orderly conduct of students on the bus and handling incidents of misconduct appropriately;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) knowing and understanding relevant laws, rules of the road, and local school bus safety policies;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) handling emergency situations; and</p>
          <p class="in">(6) safe loading and unloading of students.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) A background check or background investigation of the operator has been conducted that meets the requirements under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.18#stat.122A.18.8">122A.18, subdivision 8</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.03">123B.03 </a>for teachers; section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.057">144.057</a> or chapter 245C for day care employees; or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.321#stat.171.321.3">171.321, subdivision 3</a>, for all other persons operating a school bus under this subdivision.</p>
          <p class="in">(f) Operators shall submit to a physical examination as required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.321#stat.171.321.2">171.321, subdivision 2</a>, and must have in their possession the original or a copy of a current medical examiner's certificate, or otherwise provide proof of being medically qualified, to operate a school bus under this subdivision.</p>
          <p class="in">(g) The operator's driver's license is verified annually by the entity that owns, leases, or contracts for the school bus.</p>
          <p class="in">(h) A person who sustains a conviction, as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.02">609.02</a>, of violating section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.25">169A.25</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.26">169A.26</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.27">169A.27</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.31">169A.31</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.51">169A.51</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.52">169A.52</a>, or a similar statute or ordinance of another state is precluded from operating a school bus for five years from the date of conviction.</p>
          <p class="in">(i) A person who has ever been convicted of a disqualifying offense as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.3215#stat.171.3215.1">171.3215, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (c), may not operate a school bus under this subdivision.</p>
          <p class="in">(j) A person who sustains a conviction, as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.02">609.02</a>, of a fourth moving offense in violation of chapter 169 is precluded from operating a school bus for one year from the date of the last conviction.</p>
          <p class="in">(k) Students riding the school bus must have training required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.90#stat.123B.90.2">123B.90, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(l) An operator must be trained in the proper use of child safety restraints as set forth in the National Highway Traffic Safety Administration's "Guideline for the Safe Transportation of Pre-school Age Children in School Buses," if child safety restraints are used by the passengers.</p>
          <p class="in">(m) Annual certification of the requirements listed in this subdivision must be maintained under separate file at the business location for each operator licensed under this subdivision and subdivision 2, paragraph (c), clause (5). The business manager, school board, governing body of a nonpublic school, or any other entity that owns, leases, or contracts for the school bus operating under this subdivision is responsible for maintaining these files for inspection.</p>
          <p class="in">(n) The school bus must bear a current certificate of inspection issued under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.451">169.451</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(o) If the word "School" appears on the front and rear of the bus, the word "School" must be covered by a sign that reads "Activities" when the bus is being operated under authority of this subdivision.</p>
          <p>(p) The type A-I school bus or multifunction school activity bus is designed to transport 15 or fewer passengers, including the driver.</p>
          <p>(q) The school bus or multifunction school activity bus has a gross vehicle weight rating of 14,500 pounds or less.</p>
          <p>(r) An operator who sustains a conviction as described in paragraph (h), (i), or (j) while employed by the entity that owns, leases, or contracts for the school bus must report the conviction to the employer within ten days of the date of the conviction.</p>
          <p>(s) An operator whose driver's license is suspended, revoked, canceled, or disqualified by Minnesota, another state, or another jurisdiction must notify the operator's employer in writing of the suspension, revocation, cancellation, lost privilege, or disqualification. The operator must notify the operator's employer before the end of the business day immediately following the day the operator received notice of the suspension, revocation, cancellation, lost privilege, or disqualification.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.02.2b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2b.<span class="headnote">Exception for type III vehicle drivers.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Notwithstanding subdivision 2, the holder of a class A, B, C, or D driver's license, without a school bus endorsement, may operate a type III vehicle described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.011#stat.169.011.71">169.011, subdivision 71</a>, paragraph (h), under the conditions in this subdivision.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The operator is an employee of the entity that owns, leases, or contracts for the school bus.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The operator's employer has adopted and implemented a policy that provides for annual training and certification of the operator in:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) safe operation of a type III vehicle;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) understanding student behavior, including issues relating to students with disabilities;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) encouraging orderly conduct of students on the bus and handling incidents of misconduct appropriately;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) knowing and understanding relevant laws, rules of the road, and local school bus safety policies;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) handling emergency situations;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) proper use of seat belts and child safety restraints;</p>
          <p class="in">(7) performance of pretrip vehicle inspections;</p>
          <p class="in">(8) safe loading and unloading of students, including, but not limited to:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) utilizing a safe location for loading and unloading students at the curb, on the nontraffic side of the roadway, or at off-street loading areas, driveways, yards, and other areas to enable the student to avoid hazardous conditions;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) refraining from loading and unloading students in a vehicular traffic lane, on the shoulder, in a designated turn lane, or a lane adjacent to a designated turn lane;</p>
          <p class="in">(iii) avoiding a loading or unloading location that would require a pupil to cross a road, or ensuring that the driver or an aide personally escort the pupil across the road if it is not reasonably feasible to avoid such a location;</p>
          <p class="in">(iv) placing the type III vehicle in "park" during loading and unloading; and</p>
          <p class="in">(v) escorting a pupil across the road under item (iii) only after the motor is stopped, the ignition key is removed, the brakes are set, and the vehicle is otherwise rendered immobile; and</p>
          <p class="in">(9) compliance with paragraph (k), concerning reporting certain convictions to the employer within ten days of the date of conviction.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) A background check or background investigation of the operator has been conducted that meets the requirements under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.18#stat.122A.18.8">122A.18, subdivision 8</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.03">123B.03 </a>for school district employees; section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.057">144.057</a> or chapter 245C for day care employees; or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.321#stat.171.321.3">171.321, subdivision 3</a>, for all other persons operating a type III vehicle under this subdivision.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) Operators shall submit to a physical examination as required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.321#stat.171.321.2">171.321, subdivision 2</a>, and must have in their possession the original or a copy of a current medical examiner's certificate, or otherwise provide proof of being medically qualified, to operate a school bus under this subdivision.</p>
          <p class="in">(f) The operator's employer requires preemployment drug testing of applicants for operator positions. Current operators must comply with the employer's policy under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/181.951">181.951, subdivisions 2</a>, 4, and 5. Notwithstanding any law to the contrary, the operator's employer may use a Breathalyzer or similar device to fulfill random alcohol testing requirements.</p>
          <p class="in">(g) The operator's driver's license is verified annually by the entity that owns, leases, or contracts for the type III vehicle as required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.321">171.321</a>, subdivision 5.</p>
          <p class="in">(h) A person who sustains a conviction, as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.02">609.02</a>, of violating section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.25">169A.25</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.26">169A.26</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.27">169A.27</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.31">169A.31</a>, or whose driver's license is revoked under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.50">169A.50</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.53">169A.53</a> of the implied consent law or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.177">171.177</a>, or who is convicted of violating or whose driver's license is revoked under a similar statute or ordinance of another state, is precluded from operating a type III vehicle for five years from the date of conviction.</p>
          <p class="in">(i) A person who has ever been convicted of a disqualifying offense as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.3215#stat.171.3215.1">171.3215, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (c), may not operate a type III vehicle under this subdivision.</p>
          <p class="in">(j) A person who sustains a conviction, as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.02">609.02</a>, of a moving offense in violation of chapter 169 within three years of the first of three other moving offenses is precluded from operating a type III vehicle for one year from the date of the last conviction.</p>
          <p class="in">(k) An operator who sustains a conviction as described in paragraph (h), (i), or (j) while employed by the entity that owns, leases, or contracts for the school bus, shall report the conviction to the employer within ten days of the date of the conviction.</p>
          <p class="in">(l) An operator of a type III vehicle whose driver's license is suspended, revoked, canceled, or disqualified by Minnesota, another state, or another jurisdiction must notify the operator's employer in writing of the suspension, revocation, cancellation, lost privilege, or disqualification. The operator must notify the operator's employer before the end of the business day immediately following the day the operator received notice of the suspension, revocation, cancellation, lost privilege, or disqualification.</p>
          <p class="in">(m) Students riding the type III vehicle must have training required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.90#stat.123B.90.2">123B.90, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(n) Documentation of meeting the requirements listed in this subdivision must be maintained under separate file at the business location for each type III vehicle operator. The business manager, school board, governing body of a nonpublic school, or any other entity that owns, leases, or contracts for the type III vehicle operating under this subdivision is responsible for maintaining these files for inspection.</p>
          <p class="in">(o) The type III vehicle must bear a current certificate of inspection issued under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.451">169.451</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(p) An employee of a school or of a school district, who is not employed for the sole purpose of operating a type III vehicle, is exempt from paragraphs (e) and (f).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.02.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Motorized bicycle.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A motorized bicycle may not be operated on any public roadway by any person who does not possess a valid driver's license, unless the person has obtained a motorized bicycle operator's permit or motorized bicycle instruction permit from the commissioner of public safety. The operator's permit may be issued to any person who has attained the age of 15 years and who has passed the examination prescribed by the commissioner. The instruction permit may be issued to any person who has attained the age of 15 years and who has successfully completed an approved safety course and passed the written portion of the examination prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) This course must consist of, but is not limited to, a basic understanding of:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) motorized bicycles and their limitations;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) motorized bicycle laws and rules;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) safe operating practices and basic operating techniques;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) helmets and protective clothing;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) motorized bicycle traffic strategies; and</p>
          <p class="in">(6) effects of alcohol and drugs on motorized bicycle operators.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The commissioner may adopt rules prescribing the content of the safety course, examination, and the information to be contained on the permits. A person operating a motorized bicycle under a motorized bicycle permit is subject to the restrictions imposed by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.974#stat.169.974.2">169.974, subdivision 2</a>, on operation of a motorcycle under a two-wheel instruction permit.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) The fees for motorized bicycle operator's permits are as follows:</p>
          <table class="border_none">
            <col style="width:11.5384615384615%">
            <col style="width:73.0769230769231%">
            <col style="width:6.153846153846154%">
            <col style="width:9.23076923076923%">
            <tr>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: right;">(1) </td>
              <td class="valign_top">Motorized bicycle operator's permit before age 21 and valid until age 21</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: right;">$</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: right;">9.75 </td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: right;">(2)</td>
              <td class="valign_top">Renewal permit age 21 or older and valid for four years</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: right;">$</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: right;">15.75 </td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: right;">(3)</td>
              <td class="valign_top">Duplicate of any renewal permit</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: right;">$</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: right;">5.25 </td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: right;">(4)</td>
              <td class="valign_top">Written examination and instruction permit, valid for 30 days</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: right;">$</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: right;">6.75</td>
            </tr>
          </table>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.02.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Restricted commercial driver's license.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner may issue restricted commercial drivers' licenses and take the following actions to the extent that the actions are authorized by regulation of the United States Department of Transportation in Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, section 383.3, paragraph (f):</p>
          <p>(1) prescribe examination requirements and other qualifications for the license;</p>
          <p>(2) prescribe classes of vehicles that may be operated by holders of the license;</p>
          <p>(3) specify commercial motor vehicle operation that is authorized by the license, and prohibit other commercial motor vehicle operation by holders of the license; and</p>
          <p>(4) prescribe the period of time during which the license is valid.</p>
          <p>(b) Restricted commercial drivers' licenses are subject to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.165">171.165</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.166">171.166</a> in the same manner as other commercial drivers' licenses.</p>
          <p>(c) Actions of the commissioner under this subdivision are not subject to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.05">14.05</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.47">14.47</a> of the Administrative Procedure Act.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.02.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Exemption for certain backup snowplow drivers.</span></h2>
          <p>Pursuant to the waiver authorization set forth in Public Law 104-59, section 345, subsection (a), paragraph (5), a person who operates a commercial motor vehicle for the purpose of removing snow or ice from a roadway by plowing, salting, or sanding is not required to hold a commercial driver's license if the person:</p>
          <p>(1) is an employee of a local unit of government with a population of 3,000 or less;</p>
          <p>(2) is operating within the boundaries of the local unit of government;</p>
          <p>(3) holds a valid class D driver's license; and</p>
          <p>(4) except in the event of a lawful strike, is temporarily replacing the employee who normally operates the vehicle but either is unable to operate the vehicle or is in need of additional assistance due to a snow emergency as determined by the local unit of government.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.02.history
			">
