<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/146/#laws.8.11.0">2007 c 146 art 8 s 11</a>,12; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/271/#laws.0.3.0">2008 c 271 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/287/#laws.1.64.0">2008 c 287 art 1 s 64</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/350/#laws.1.54.0">2008 c 350 art 1 s 54</a>,96; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/24/#laws.0.1.0">2009 c 24 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/96/#laws.8.10.0">2009 c 96 art 8 s 10</a>,11; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/351/#laws.0.36.0">2010 c 351 s 36</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/287/#laws.0.16.0">2014 c 287 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/312/#laws.11.19.0">2014 c 312 art 11 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2016/0/114/#laws.0.9.0">2016 c 114 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/83/#laws.3.18.0">2017 c 83 art 3 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/3/#laws.3.73.0">1Sp2017 c 3 art 3 s 73</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/83/#laws.1.58.0">2020 c 83 art 1 s 58</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/100/#laws.0.12.0">2020 c 100 s 12</a>,13</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.03">
        <h1 class="shn">171.03 PERSONS EXEMPT.</h1>
        <p class="in">The following persons are exempt from license hereunder:</p>
        <p class="in">(a) A person in the employ or service of the United States federal government is exempt while driving or operating a motor vehicle owned by or leased to the United States federal government.</p>
        <p class="in">(b) A person in the employ or service of the United States federal government is exempt from the requirement to possess a valid class A, class B, or class C commercial driver's license while driving or operating for military purposes a commercial motor vehicle for the United States federal government if the person is:</p>
        <p class="in">(1) on active duty in the U. S. Coast Guard;</p>
        <p class="in">(2) on active duty in a branch of the U. S. armed forces, which includes the Army, Air Force, Navy, and Marine Corps;</p>
        <p class="in">(3) a member of a reserve component of the U. S. armed forces; or</p>
        <p class="in">(4) on active duty in the Army National Guard or Air National Guard, which includes (i) a member on full-time National Guard duty, (ii) a member undergoing part-time National Guard training, and (iii) a National Guard military technician, who is a civilian required to wear a military uniform.</p>
        <p class="bl">The exemption provided under this paragraph does not apply to a U. S. armed forces reserve technician.</p>
        <p class="in">(c) A person operating a covered farm vehicle as defined in Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/390.05">390.05</a>, that is not carrying hazardous materials of a type or quantity that requires the vehicle to be placarded in accordance with Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/172.504">172.504</a>, is exempt from the requirement to possess a valid class A, class B, or class C commercial driver's license.</p>
        <p class="in">(d) Any person while driving or operating any farm tractor or implement of husbandry temporarily on a highway is exempt. For purposes of this section, an all-terrain vehicle, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/84.92#stat.84.92.8">84.92, subdivision 8</a>, an off-highway motorcycle, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/84.787#stat.84.787.7">84.787, subdivision 7</a>, and an off-road vehicle, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/84.797#stat.84.797.7">84.797, subdivision 7</a>, are not implements of husbandry.</p>
        <p class="in">(e) A nonresident who is at least 15 years of age and who has in immediate possession a valid driver's license issued to the nonresident in the home state or country may operate a motor vehicle in this state only as a driver.</p>
        <p class="in">(f) A nonresident who has in immediate possession a valid commercial driver's license issued by a state or jurisdiction in accordance with the standards of Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, part 383, and who is operating in Minnesota the class of commercial motor vehicle authorized by the issuing state or jurisdiction is exempt.</p>
        <p class="in">(g) Any nonresident who is at least 18 years of age, whose home state or country does not require the licensing of drivers may operate a motor vehicle as a driver, but only for a period of not more than 90 days in any calendar year, if the motor vehicle so operated is duly registered for the current calendar year in the home state or country of the nonresident.</p>
        <p class="in">(h) Any person who becomes a resident of the state of Minnesota and who has in possession a valid driver's license issued to the person under and pursuant to the laws of some other state or jurisdiction or by military authorities of the United States may operate a motor vehicle as a driver, but only for a period of not more than 60 days after becoming a resident of this state, without being required to have a Minnesota driver's license as provided in this chapter.</p>
        <p class="in">(i) Any person who becomes a resident of the state of Minnesota and who has in possession a valid commercial driver's license issued by another state or jurisdiction in accordance with the standards of Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, part 383, is exempt for not more than 30 days after becoming a resident of this state.</p>
        <p class="in">(j) Any person operating a snowmobile, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/84.81">84.81</a>, is exempt.</p>
        <p>(k) A railroad operator, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.035#stat.169.035.4">169.035, subdivision 4</a>, paragraph (a), is exempt while operating a railroad locomotive or train, or on-track equipment while being operated upon rails. This exemption includes operation while crossing a street or highway, whether public or private.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.03.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/13">2720-144</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/401/#laws.0.3.0">1939 c 401 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1943/0/331/#laws.0.2.0">1943 c 331 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/496/#laws.0.1.0">1955 c 496 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/695/#laws.0.13.0">1969 c 695 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/644/#laws.0.5.0">1971 c 644 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/307/#laws.0.18.0">1989 c 307 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/331/#laws.0.23.0">1989 c 331 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/333/#laws.0.27.0">1991 c 333 s 27</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/311/#laws.1.14.0">1993 c 311 art 1 s 14</a>; art 2 s 14; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/6/#laws.3.60.0">1Sp2005 c 6 art 3 s 60</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/350/#laws.1.55.0">2008 c 350 art 1 s 55</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/287/#laws.4.33.0">2012 c 287 art 4 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/287/#laws.0.17.0">2014 c 287 s 17</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.04">
        <h1 class="shn">171.04 PERSONS NOT ELIGIBLE FOR DRIVERS' LICENSES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.04.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Persons not eligible.</span></h2>
          <p>The department shall not issue a driver's license:</p>
          <p>(1) to any person under 18 years unless:</p>
          <p>(i) the applicant is 16 or 17 years of age and has a previously issued valid license from another state or country or the applicant has, for the 12 consecutive months preceding application, held a provisional license and during that time has incurred (A) no conviction for a violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.20">169A.20</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.33">169A.33</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.35">169A.35</a>, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.50">169A.50 </a>to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.53">169A.53</a>, or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.177">171.177</a>, (B) no conviction for a crash-related moving violation, and (C) not more than one conviction for a moving violation that is not crash related. "Moving violation" means a violation of a traffic regulation but does not include a parking violation, vehicle equipment violation, or warning citation;</p>
          <p>(ii) the application for a license is approved by (A) either parent when both reside in the same household as the minor applicant or, if otherwise, then (B) the parent or spouse of the parent having custody or, in the event there is no court order for custody, then (C) the parent or spouse of the parent with whom the minor is living or, if subitems (A) to (C) do not apply, then (D) the guardian having custody of the minor, (E) the foster parent or director of the transitional living program in which the child resides or, in the event a person under the age of 18 has no living father, mother, or guardian, or is married or otherwise legally emancipated, then (F) the minor's adult spouse, adult close family member, or adult employer; provided, that the approval required by this item contains a verification of the age of the applicant and the identity of the parent, guardian, adult spouse, adult close family member, or adult employer; and</p>
          <p>(iii) the applicant presents a certification by the person who approves the application under item (ii), stating that the applicant has driven a motor vehicle accompanied by and under supervision of a licensed driver at least 21 years of age for at least ten hours during the period of provisional licensure;</p>
          <p>(2) to any person who is 18 years of age or younger, unless the person has applied for, been issued, and possessed the appropriate instruction permit for a minimum of six months, and, with respect to a person under 18 years of age, a provisional license for a minimum of 12 months;</p>
          <p>(3) to any person who is 19 years of age or older, unless that person has applied for, been issued, and possessed the appropriate instruction permit for a minimum of three months;</p>
          <p>(4) to any person whose license has been suspended during the period of suspension except that a suspended license may be reinstated during the period of suspension upon the licensee furnishing proof of financial responsibility in the same manner as provided in the Minnesota No-Fault Automobile Insurance Act;</p>
          <p>(5) to any person whose license has been revoked except upon furnishing proof of financial responsibility in the same manner as provided in the Minnesota No-Fault Automobile Insurance Act and if otherwise qualified;</p>
          <p>(6) to any drug-dependent person, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/254A.02#stat.254A.02.5">254A.02, subdivision 5</a>;</p>
          <p>(7) to any person who has been adjudged legally incompetent by reason of mental illness, mental deficiency, or inebriation, and has not been restored to capacity, unless the department is satisfied that the person is competent to operate a motor vehicle with safety to persons or property;</p>
          <p>(8) to any person who is required by this chapter to take a vision, knowledge, or road examination, unless the person has successfully passed the examination. An applicant who fails four road tests must complete a minimum of six hours of behind-the-wheel instruction with an approved instructor before taking the road test again;</p>
          <p>(9) to any person who is required under the Minnesota No-Fault Automobile Insurance Act to deposit proof of financial responsibility and who has not deposited the proof;</p>
          <p>(10) to any person when the commissioner has good cause to believe that the operation of a motor vehicle on the highways by the person would be inimical to public safety or welfare;</p>
          <p>(11) to any person when, in the opinion of the commissioner, the person is afflicted with or suffering from a physical or mental disability or disease that will affect the person in a manner as to prevent the person from exercising reasonable and ordinary control over a motor vehicle while operating it upon the highways;</p>
          <p>(12) to a person who is unable to read and understand official signs regulating, warning, and directing traffic;</p>
          <p>(13) to a child for whom a court has ordered denial of driving privileges under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260C.201#stat.260C.201.1">260C.201, subdivision 1</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260B.235#stat.260B.235.5">260B.235, subdivision 5</a>, until the period of denial is completed; or</p>
          <p>(14) to any person whose license has been canceled, during the period of cancellation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.04.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Disqualified operators of commercial motor vehicles.</span></h2>
          <p>During the period of disqualification, the department shall not issue a class C, class B, or class A commercial driver's license, including a limited license, to a person who has been disqualified from operating a commercial motor vehicle under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.165">171.165</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.04.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Medical qualification for commercial driver's license.</span></h2>
          <p>The department shall not issue a class A, class B, or class C commercial driver's license to a person who:</p>
          <p>(1) has not submitted the self-certification required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.162">171.162</a>, subdivision 2; or</p>
          <p>(2) has not submitted either a valid medical examiner's certificate or a current medical waiver as required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.162#stat.171.162.3">171.162, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.04.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Persons not eligible for enhanced driver's license.</span></h2>
          <p>The department shall not issue an enhanced driver's license to any person who is:</p>
          <p>(1) under 16 years of age;</p>
          <p>(2) not a resident of this state;</p>
          <p>(3) not a citizen of the United States of America; or</p>
          <p>(4) described in subdivision 1, clauses (4) to (12), or (14).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.04.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Temporary lawful admission.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner is prohibited from issuing a driver's license or Minnesota identification card to an applicant who has a lawful temporary admission period, as demonstrated under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.06#stat.171.06.3">171.06, subdivision 3</a>, paragraph (b), clause (2), that expires within 30 days of the date of the application.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.04.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/13">2720-144a</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/401/#laws.0.4.0">1939 c 401 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1941/0/517/#laws.0.0.0">1941 c 517</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/0/564/#laws.0.1.0">1957 c 564 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/382/#laws.0.1.0">1963 c 382 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1965/0/78/#laws.0.1.0">1965 c 78 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1965/0/795/#laws.0.1.0">1965 c 795 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/0/61/#laws.0.1.0">1967 c 61 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/0/839/#laws.0.1.0">1967 c 839 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/363/#laws.0.30.0">1981 c 363 s 30</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/548/#laws.4.14.0">1982 c 548 art 4 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/226/#laws.3.13.0">1995 c 226 art 3 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/275/#laws.0.3.0">1996 c 275 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/455/#laws.3.24.0">1996 c 455 art 3 s 24</a>;       <p><b>NOTE: </b>(a) Subdivision 5, as added by Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 6, was effective May 19, 2017, and applies to driver's licenses and Minnesota identification cards applied for and issued on or after October 1, 2018. Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 29.</p>
      <p>(b) If the Federal REAL ID Act, Public Law 109-13, Division B, is repealed, or if the definition of "official purpose" in Code of Federal Regulations, title 6, part 37, is amended, subdivision 5, as added by Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 6, is repealed on June 30 of the following year. Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 27.</p>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.041">
        <h1 class="shn">171.041 RESTRICTED LICENSE FOR FARM WORK.</h1>
        <p>(a) Notwithstanding any provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.04">171.04</a> relating to the age of an applicant to the contrary, the commissioner may issue a restricted farm work license to operate a motor vehicle to a person who has attained the age of 15 years and who, except for age, is qualified to hold a driver's license. The applicant is not required to comply with the six-month instruction permit possession provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.04#stat.171.04.1">171.04, subdivision 1</a>, clause (2), and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.05#stat.171.05.2a">171.05, subdivision 2a</a>, or with the 12-month provisional license possession provision of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.04#stat.171.04.1">171.04, subdivision 1</a>, clause (1), item (i).</p>
        <p>(b) The restricted license must be issued solely for the purpose of authorizing the person to whom the restricted license is issued to assist with farm work. An individual may perform farm work under the restricted license for any entity authorized to farm under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/500.24">500.24</a>. A person holding this restricted license may operate a motor vehicle only during daylight hours and only within a radius of 40 miles of the farmhouse on the farm where the person is working.</p>
        <p>(c) An applicant for a restricted license must apply to the commissioner for the license on forms prescribed by the commissioner. The application must be accompanied by:</p>
        <p>(1) a copy of a property tax statement showing that the applicant's parent, guardian, or employer owns land that is classified as agricultural land or a copy of a rental statement or agreement showing that the applicant's parent or guardian rents land classified as agricultural land;</p>
        <p>(2) a written verified statement by the applicant's parent or guardian setting forth the necessity for the license; and</p>
        <p>(3) if the applicant is not working for a parent or guardian, a written verified statement by the farm owner setting forth the necessity for the license.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.041.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/382/#laws.0.3.0">1963 c 382 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/0/87/#laws.0.1.0">1967 c 87 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/48/#laws.0.1.0">1997 c 48 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/230/#laws.0.6.0">1997 c 230 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/251/#laws.0.24.0">1997 c 251 s 24</a>,25; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/388/#laws.0.9.0">1998 c 388 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/3/#laws.3.73.0">1Sp2019 c 3 art 3 s 73</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/68/#laws.5.34.0">2023 c 68 art 5 s 34</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.042">
        <h1 class="shn">171.042 DRIVER'S LICENSE FOR MEDICAL REASON.</h1>
        <p>(a) For purposes of this section, "relative" means the applicant's grandparent, parent, sibling, or legal guardian, including adoptive, half, step, and in-law relationships.</p>
        <p>(b) Notwithstanding any provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.04">171.04</a>, relating to the age of an applicant, the commissioner may issue a driver's license to a person who has attained the age of 15 years but is under the age of 16 years, who, except for age, is qualified to hold a driver's license and who needs to operate a motor vehicle because of:</p>
        <p>(1) personal medical reasons;</p>
        <p>(2) medical reasons of a relative; or</p>
        <p>(3) a disabled relative who has a disability that makes it difficult to drive or who does not have a driver's license due to a disability.</p>
        <p>(c) The applicant is not required to comply with the six-month instruction permit possession provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.04#stat.171.04.1">171.04, subdivision 1</a>, clause (2), and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.05">171.05, subdivision </a>2a, or with the 12-month provisional license possession provision of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.04">171.04</a>, subdivision 1, clause (1), item (i).</p>
        <p>(d) Applicants must apply to the commissioner for the license on forms prescribed by the commissioner. The application must be accompanied by written verified statements from the applicant's relative or a doctor setting forth the reason the applicant is qualified for the license. The commissioner in issuing such license may impose such conditions and limitations as in the commissioner's judgment are necessary to the interests of the public safety and welfare.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.042.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/23/#laws.0.1.0">1975 c 23 s 1</a>; 1986 c 444; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/68/#laws.4.55.0">2023 c 68 art 4 s 55</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.043">
        <h1 class="shn">171.043 NOTICE OF PERSONS UNDER DRIVER'S LICENSE CANCELLATION.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner of public safety shall develop a program under which the commissioner provides a monthly notice to local law enforcement agencies of the names and addresses of persons residing within the local agency's jurisdiction whose drivers' licenses or driving privileges have been canceled under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.04#stat.171.04.1">171.04, subdivision 1</a>, clause (10). At the commissioner's discretion, the commissioner may adopt necessary procedures so that the information is current and accurate. Data in the notice are private data on individuals and are available to law enforcement agencies.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.043.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/570/#laws.1.28.0">1992 c 570 art 1 s 28</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/12/#laws.3.7.0">1997 c 12 art 3 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/238/#laws.2.91.0">1999 c 238 art 2 s 91</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.05">
        <h1 class="shn">171.05 INSTRUCTION PERMIT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.05.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Person 18 or more years of age.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Any person who is 18 or more years of age and who, except for a lack of instruction in operating a motor vehicle, would otherwise be qualified to obtain a class D driver's license under this chapter, may apply for an instruction permit and the department shall issue the permit. The instruction permit entitles the applicant to drive a motor vehicle for which a class D license is valid upon the highways for a period of two years if the permit holder:</p>
          <p>(1) has the permit in immediate possession; and</p>
          <p>(2) is driving the vehicle while accompanied by an adult licensed driver who is actually occupying a seat beside the driver.</p>
          <p>(b) Any license of a lower class may be used as an instruction permit to operate a vehicle requiring a higher class license for a period of six months after passage of the written test or tests required for the higher class and when the licensee is accompanied by and receiving instruction from a holder of the appropriate higher class license. A copy of the record of examination taken for the higher class license must be carried by the driver while using the lower class license as an instruction permit.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.05.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Minimum period to possess instruction permit.</span></h2>
          <p>An applicant who has received an instruction permit under subdivision 1 and has not previously been licensed to drive in Minnesota or in another jurisdiction must possess the instruction permit for not less than six months for an applicant who is 18 years of age, and not less than three months for all other applicants, before qualifying for a driver's license. An applicant with an instruction permit from another jurisdiction must be credited with the amount of time that permit has been held.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.05.1b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1b.<span class="headnote">Instruction permit not issued.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding subdivision 1, the commissioner shall not issue an instruction permit to a person under age 18 if the person has ever been convicted of a violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.20">169A.20</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.33">169A.33</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.35">169A.35</a>; a violation of a provision of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.50">169A.50</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.53">169A.53</a>; a violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.177">171.177</a>; or a crash-related moving violation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.05.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Person less than 18 years of age.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The department may issue an instruction permit to an applicant who is 15, 16, or 17 years of age and who:</p>
          <p>(1) has completed a course of driver education in another state, has a previously issued valid license from another state, or:</p>
          <p>(i) is enrolled in behind-the-wheel training in a driver education program; and</p>
          <p>(ii) has completed:</p>
          <p>(A) the classroom phase of instruction in a driver education program that includes classroom and behind-the-wheel training;</p>
          <p>(B) 15 hours of classroom instruction in a driver education program that presents classroom and behind-the-wheel instruction concurrently;</p>
          <p>(C) home-classroom driver training, when the student is receiving full-time instruction in a home school within the meaning of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/120A.22">120A.22 </a>and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/120A.24">120A.24</a>, the student is working toward a home school diploma, the home-classroom driver training materials are approved by the commissioner of public safety, and the student's parent has certified the student's home school and home-classroom driver training status on the form approved by the commissioner;</p>
          <p>(D) a teleconference driver education program authorized by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.395">171.395</a>; or</p>
          <p>(E) an online driver education program authorized by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.396">171.396</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) has passed a test of the applicant's eyesight;</p>
          <p>(3) has passed a department-administered test of the applicant's knowledge of traffic laws;</p>
          <p>(4) has completed the required application, which must be approved by (i) either parent when both reside in the same household as the minor applicant or, if otherwise, then (ii) the parent or spouse of the parent having custody or, in the event there is no court order for custody, then (iii) the parent or spouse of the parent with whom the minor is living or, if items (i) to (iii) do not apply, then (iv) the guardian having custody of the minor, (v) the foster parent or the director of the transitional living program in which the child resides or, in the event a person under the age of 18 has no living father, mother, or guardian, or is married or otherwise legally emancipated, then (vi) the applicant's adult spouse, adult close family member, or adult employer; provided, that the approval required by this clause contains a verification of the age of the applicant and the identity of the parent, guardian, adult spouse, adult close family member, or adult employer; and</p>
          <p>(5) has paid all fees required in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.06#stat.171.06.2">171.06, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) In addition, the applicant may submit a certification stating that a primary driving supervisor has completed the supplemental parental curriculum under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.0701">171.0701</a>, subdivision 1a, for the purposes of provisional license requirements under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.055">171.055</a>, subdivision 1, paragraph (a), clause (6). The certification must be completed by a driver education instructor, as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.0701#stat.171.0701.1a">171.0701, subdivision 1a</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) For the purposes of determining compliance with the certification of paragraph (a), clause (1), item (ii), subitem (C), the commissioner may request verification of a student's home school status from the superintendent of the school district in which the student resides and the superintendent shall provide that verification.</p>
          <p>(d) A driver education program under this subdivision includes a public, private, or commercial program and must be approved by the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(e) The instruction permit is valid for two years from the date of application and may be renewed upon payment of a fee equal to the fee for issuance of an instruction permit under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.06#stat.171.06.2">171.06, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.05.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Permit for six months.</span></h2>
          <p> An applicant who has applied for and received an instruction permit pursuant to subdivision 2 must possess the instruction permit for not less than six months before qualifying for a driver's license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.05.2b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2b.<span class="headnote">Instruction permit use by person under age 18.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) This subdivision applies to persons who have applied for and received an instruction permit under subdivision 2.</p>
          <p>(b) The permit holder may, with the permit in possession, operate a motor vehicle, but must be accompanied by and be under the supervision of a certified driver education instructor, the permit holder's parent or guardian, or another licensed driver age 21 or older. The supervisor must occupy the seat beside the permit holder.</p>
          <p>(c) The permit holder may not operate a vehicle while communicating over, or otherwise operating, a cellular or wireless telephone, whether handheld or hands free, when the vehicle is in motion. The permit holder may assert as an affirmative defense that the violation was made for the sole purpose of obtaining emergency assistance to prevent a crime about to be committed, or in the reasonable belief that a person's life or safety was in danger. Violation of this paragraph is a petty misdemeanor subject to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.89">169.89</a>, subdivision 2.</p>
          <p>(d) The permit holder must maintain a driving record free of convictions for moving violations, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.04#stat.171.04.1">171.04, subdivision 1</a>, and free of convictions for violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.20">169A.20</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.33">169A.33</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.35">169A.35</a>, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.50">169A.50</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.53">169A.53</a>, or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.177">171.177</a>. If the permit holder drives a motor vehicle in violation of the law, the commissioner shall suspend, cancel, or revoke the permit in accordance with the statutory section violated.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.05.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Motorized bicycle.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding any provision in subdivision 1 to the contrary, the department, upon application and payment of the fee prescribed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.02#stat.171.02.3">171.02, subdivision 3</a>, may issue a motorized bicycle instruction permit to an applicant who is 15 years of age and who has successfully completed the written portion of the examination prescribed by the commissioner. The holder of this instruction permit who has the permit in possession may operate a motorized bicycle within one mile of the holder's residence for the purpose of practicing to take the operator portion of the examination prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.05.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Rulemaking.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of public safety shall adopt rules to carry out the provisions of this section. The rules adopted under this section are exempt from the rulemaking provisions of chapter 14. The rules are subject to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.386">14.386</a>, except that section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.386">14.386</a>, paragraph (b), does not apply.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.05.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/13">2720-144b</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/401/#laws.0.5.0">1939 c 401 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1949/0/91/#laws.0.1.0">1949 c 91 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1961/0/33/#laws.0.1.0">1961 c 33 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/382/#laws.0.2.0">1963 c 382 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/644/#laws.0.7.0">1971 c 644 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/95/#laws.0.1.0">1973 c 95 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/446/#laws.0.1.0">1980 c 446 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/454/#laws.0.22.0">1986 c 454 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/384/#laws.1.19.0">1987 c 384 art 1 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/529/#laws.0.6.0">1990 c 529 s 6</a>,7;       <div class="section" id="stat.171.055">
        <h1 class="shn">171.055 PROVISIONAL LICENSE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.055.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Requirements for provisional license.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The department may issue a provisional license, which must be distinctive in appearance from a driver's license, to an applicant who:</p>
          <p>(1) has reached the age of 16 years;</p>
          <p>(2) during the six months immediately preceding the application for the provisional license has possessed an instruction permit and has incurred (i) no convictions for a violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.20">169A.20</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.33">169A.33</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.35">169A.35</a>, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.50">169A.50</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.53">169A.53</a>, or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.177">171.177</a>, (ii) no convictions for a crash-related moving violation, and (iii) no convictions for a moving violation that is not crash related;</p>
          <p>(3) has successfully completed a course of driver education in accordance with department rules;</p>
          <p>(4) completes the required application, which must be approved by (i) either parent when both reside in the same household as the minor applicant or, if otherwise, then (ii) the parent or spouse of the parent having custody or, in the event there is no court order for custody, then (iii) the parent or spouse of the parent with whom the minor is living or, if items (i) to (iii) do not apply, then (iv) the guardian having custody of the minor, (v) the foster parent or the director of the transitional living program in which the child resides or, in the event a person under the age of 18 has no living father, mother, or guardian, or is married or otherwise legally emancipated, then (vi) the applicant's adult spouse, adult close family member, or adult employer; provided, that the approval required by this clause contains a verification of the age of the applicant and the identity of the parent, guardian, adult spouse, adult close family member, or adult employer;</p>
          <p>(5) submits a supervised driving log, in a format approved by the commissioner, that:</p>
          <p>(i) states that the applicant has driven a motor vehicle accompanied by and under the supervision of a licensed driver at least 21 years of age, for no less than 40 total hours or as provided in clause (6), item (i), at least 15 of which were nighttime hours;</p>
          <p>(ii) identifies dates and lengths of driving time for each supervised driving trip; and</p>
          <p>(iii) is signed by the primary driving supervisor, attesting that the applicant has met the requirements under item (i);</p>
          <p>(6) has:</p>
          <p>(i) driven a motor vehicle in the manner required under clause (5) for no less than ten hours in addition to the total hours specified in clause (5), item (i); or</p>
          <p>(ii) submitted a certification of supplemental parental curriculum under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.05#stat.171.05.2">171.05, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (b), for the primary driving supervisor under clause (5), as part of an application for an instruction permit; and</p>
          <p>(7) pays the fee required in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.06#stat.171.06.2">171.06, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) For purposes of this section, "moving violation" has the meaning given it in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.04#stat.171.04.1">171.04, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) Notwithstanding paragraph (a), clause (2), the commissioner shall not issue a provisional license to a person who has ever incurred a conviction for violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.20">169A.20</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.33">169A.33</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.35">169A.35</a>; a violation of a provision of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.50">169A.50</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.53">169A.53</a>; a violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.177">171.177</a>; or a crash-related moving violation, and at the time of the conviction the person did not possess an instruction permit.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.055.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Use of provisional license.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A provisional license holder may not operate a vehicle while communicating over, or otherwise operating, a cellular or wireless telephone, whether handheld or hands free, when the vehicle is in motion. The provisional license holder may assert as an affirmative defense that the violation was made for the sole purpose of obtaining emergency assistance to prevent a crime about to be committed, or in the reasonable belief that a person's life or safety was in danger. Violation of this paragraph is a petty misdemeanor subject to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.89">169.89</a>, subdivision 2.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) If the holder of a provisional license during the period of provisional licensing incurs (1) a conviction for a violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.20">169A.20</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.33">169A.33</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.35">169A.35</a>, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.50">169A.50</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.53">169A.53</a>, or a violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.177">171.177</a>, (2) a conviction for a crash-related moving violation, or (3) more than one conviction for a moving violation that is not crash related, the person may not be issued a driver's license until 12 consecutive months have expired since the date of the conviction or until the person reaches the age of 18 years, whichever occurs first.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) For the first six months of provisional licensure, a provisional license holder may not operate a motor vehicle carrying more than one passenger under the age of 20 years who is not a member of the holder's immediate family. For the second six months, the holder of the license may not operate a motor vehicle that is carrying more than three passengers who are under the age of 20 years and who are not members of the holder's immediate family. This paragraph does not apply if the provisional license holder is accompanied by a parent or guardian.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) For the first six months of provisional licensure, a provisional license holder may operate a motor vehicle between the hours of midnight and 5:00 a.m. only when the license holder is:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) driving between the license holder's home and place of employment;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) driving between the license holder's home and a school event for which the school has not provided transportation;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) driving for employment purposes; or</p>
          <p class="in">(4) accompanied by a licensed driver at least 25 years of age.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.055.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/388/#laws.0.13.0">1998 c 388 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/478/#laws.2.7.0">2000 c 478 art 2 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/371/#laws.1.54.0">2002 c 371 art 1 s 54</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/177/#laws.0.3.0">2004 c 177 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/6/#laws.3.65.0">1Sp2005 c 6 art 3 s 65</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/260/#laws.2.16.0">2006 c 260 art 2 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/350/#laws.1.56.0">2008 c 350 art 1 s 56</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/165/#laws.0.6.0">2009 c 165 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/269/#laws.1.3.0">2010 c 269 art 1 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/261/#laws.0.4.0">2014 c 261 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/83/#laws.3.18.0">2017 c 83 art 3 s 18</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.06">
        <h1 class="shn">171.06 APPLICATION FOR LICENSE, PERMIT, IDENTIFICATION CARD; FEES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.06.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Application format and requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>Every application for a Minnesota identification card, including an enhanced identification card, or for a driver's license, including an instruction permit, a provisional license, a noncompliant driver's license, a REAL ID driver's license, and an enhanced driver's license, must be made in a format approved by the department. Every application must be accompanied by payment of the proper fee. All applicants must sign the application and declare, under penalty of perjury, that the information and documentation presented in the application is true and correct. All applications requiring evidence of legal presence in the United States or United States citizenship must be signed in the presence of the person authorized to accept the application, or the signature on the application may be verified by a notary public.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.06.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Fees.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The fees for a license and Minnesota identification card are as follows:</p>
          <table>
            <col style="width:30.7219662058372%">
            <col style="width:7.680491551459294%">
            <col style="width:15.0537634408602%">
            <col style="width:15.5145929339478%">
            <col style="width:15.5145929339478%">
            <col style="width:15.5145929339478%">
            <tr>
              <td>REAL ID Compliant or Noncompliant Classified Driver's License</td>
              <td></td>
              <td style="text-align: right;">D-$27.75</td>
              <td style="text-align: right;">C-$31.75</td>
              <td style="text-align: right;">B-$38.75</td>
              <td style="text-align: right;">A-$46.75</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>REAL ID Compliant or Noncompliant Classified Under-21 D.L.</td>
              <td></td>
              <td style="text-align: right;">D-$27.75</td>
              <td style="text-align: right;">C-$31.75</td>
              <td style="text-align: right;">B-$38.75</td>
              <td style="text-align: right;">A-$26.75</td>
            </tr>

              <td>Commercial Learner's Permit</td>

              <td style="text-align: right;"></td>

              <td>REAL ID Compliant or Noncompliant Provisional License</td>

              <td style="text-align: right;"></td>
              <td style="text-align: right;">$14.25</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>Enhanced Provisional License</td>
              <td></td>
              <td style="text-align: right;"></td>
              <td style="text-align: right;"></td>

              <td>Duplicate REAL ID Compliant or Noncompliant License or duplicate REAL ID Compliant or Noncompliant identification card</td>
              <td></td>

            <tr>
              <td>REAL ID Compliant or Noncompliant Minnesota identification card or REAL ID Compliant or Noncompliant Under-21 Minnesota identification card, other than duplicate, except as otherwise provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.07">171.07, subdivisions 3</a> and 3a</td>
              <td></td>
              <td style="text-align: right;"></td>

              <td style="text-align: right;"></td>

              <td>Enhanced Minnesota identification card</td>
              <td></td>
              <td style="text-align: right;"></td>

              <td style="text-align: right;">$32.25</td>

          </table>
          <p class="in">(b) In addition to each fee required in paragraph (a), the commissioner must collect a surcharge of $2.25. Surcharges collected under this paragraph must be credited to the driver and vehicle services technology account under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/299A.705">299A.705</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) Notwithstanding paragraph (a), an individual who holds a provisional license and has a driving record free of (1) convictions for a violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.20">169A.20</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.33">169A.33</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.35">169A.35</a>, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.50">169A.50</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.53">169A.53</a>, or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.177">171.177</a>, (2) convictions for crash-related moving violations, and (3) convictions for moving violations that are not crash related, has a $3.50 credit toward the fee for any classified under-21 driver's license. "Moving violation" has the meaning given it in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.04#stat.171.04.1">171.04, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) In addition to the driver's license fee required under paragraph (a), the commissioner must collect an additional $4 processing fee from each new applicant or individual renewing a license with a school bus endorsement to cover the costs for processing an applicant's initial and biennial physical examination certificate. The department must not charge these applicants any other fee to receive or renew the endorsement.</p>
          <p>(e) In addition to the fee required under paragraph (a), a driver's license agent may charge and retain a filing fee as provided under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.061#stat.171.061.4">171.061, subdivision 4</a>.</p>
          <p>(f) In addition to the fee required under paragraph (a), the commissioner must charge a filing fee at the same amount as a driver's license agent under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.061">171.061</a>, subdivision 4. Revenue collected under this paragraph must be deposited in the driver and vehicle services operating account under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/299A.705">299A.705</a>.</p>
          <p>(g) An application for a Minnesota identification card, instruction permit, provisional license, or driver's license, including an application for renewal, must contain a provision that allows the applicant to add to the fee under paragraph (a), a $2 donation for the purposes of public information and education on anatomical gifts under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.075">171.075</a>.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.06.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Motorcycle endorsement fee.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) In addition to the appropriate fee under subdivision 2, the fee for a motorcycle endorsement on a driver's license is:</p>
          <p>(1) $26.50 for an initial endorsement or a duplicate license obtained for the purpose of adding the endorsement; and</p>
          <p>(2) $17 for each license renewal with the endorsement.</p>
          <p>(b) The additional fee must be paid into the state treasury and credited as follows:</p>
          <p>(1) $19 of the additional fee under paragraph (a), clause (1), and $11 of the additional fee under paragraph (a), clause (2), to the motorcycle safety account, which is hereby created in the special revenue fund; and</p>
          <p>(2) the remainder to the general fund.</p>
          <p>(c) All application forms prepared by the commissioner for motorcycle endorsements must clearly state the amount of the total fee that is dedicated to the motorcycle safety account.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.06.2b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2b.</h2>
          <p>Never effective. See Laws 1991 c 291 art 19 s 11</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.06.2c">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2c.<span class="headnote">Exemption; certain veterans.</span></h2>
          <p>For an applicant who is a veteran with a total service-connected disability, the commissioner must not impose:</p>
          <p>(1) a license or endorsement fee, including fees and surcharges specified under:</p>
          <p>(i) subdivisions 2 and 2a; and</p>
          <p>(ii) section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.02#stat.171.02.3">171.02, subdivision 3</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) a filing fee under subdivision 2 or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.061#stat.171.061.4">171.061, subdivision 4</a>; or</p>
          <p>(3) a fee for an identification card under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.07#stat.171.07.3">171.07, subdivision 3</a> or 3a.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.06.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Contents of application; other information.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) An application must:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) state the full name, date of birth, sex, and either (i) the residence address of the applicant, or (ii) designated address under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/5B.05">5B.05</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) contain a description of the applicant, including the applicant's height in feet and inches, weight in pounds, eye color, and sex; the applicant's driving privileges; and the applicant's ability to operate a motor vehicle with safety;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) state:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) the applicant's Social Security number; or</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) if the applicant does not have a Social Security number and is applying for a Minnesota identification card, instruction permit, or class D provisional or driver's license, that the applicant elects not to specify a Social Security number;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) contain a notification to the applicant of the availability of a living will/health care directive designation on the license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.07#stat.171.07.7">171.07, subdivision 7</a>;</p>
          <p>(5) include a method for the applicant to:</p>
          <p>(i) request a veteran designation on the license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.07#stat.171.07.15">171.07, subdivision 15</a>, and the driving record under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.12">171.12</a>, subdivision 5a;</p>
          <p>(ii) indicate a desire to make an anatomical gift under subdivision 3b, paragraph (e);</p>
          <p>(iii) as applicable, designate document retention as provided under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.12">171.12</a>, subdivision 3c;</p>
          <p>(iv) indicate emergency contacts as provided under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.12#stat.171.12.5b">171.12, subdivision 5b</a>;</p>
          <p>(v) indicate the applicant's race and ethnicity;</p>
          <p>(vi) indicate caretaker information as provided under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.12#stat.171.12.5c">171.12, subdivision 5c</a>; and</p>
          <p>(vii) indicate a temporary mailing address separate from the applicant's residence address listed on the identification card or license; and</p>
          <p>(6) meet the requirements under section 201.161, subdivision 3.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Applications must be accompanied by satisfactory evidence demonstrating:</p>
          <p>(1) identity, date of birth, and any legal name change if applicable; and</p>
          <p>(2) for drivers' licenses and Minnesota identification cards that meet all requirements of the REAL ID Act:</p>
          <p>(i) principal residence address in Minnesota, including application for a change of address, unless the applicant provides a designated address under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/5B.05">5B.05</a>;</p>
          <p>(ii) Social Security number, or related documentation as applicable; and</p>
          <p>(iii) lawful status, as defined in Code of Federal Regulations, title 6, section 37.3.</p>
          <p>(c) An application for an enhanced driver's license or enhanced identification card must be accompanied by:</p>
          <p>(1) satisfactory evidence demonstrating the applicant's full legal name and United States citizenship; and</p>
          <p>(2) a photographic identity document.</p>
          <p>(d) An application form must not provide for identification of (1) the accompanying documents used by an applicant to demonstrate identity, or (2) except as provided in paragraphs (b) and (c), the applicant's citizenship, immigration status, or lawful presence in the United States. The commissioner and a driver's license agent must not inquire about an applicant's citizenship, immigration status, or lawful presence in the United States, except as provided in paragraphs (b) and (c).</p>
          <p>(e) A Minnesota driver's license or identification card must be issued only to an individual who has a residence address in the state at the time of the application. Applications for an enhanced driver's license or enhanced identification card must include proof of residency in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.063#stat.171.063.6">171.063, subdivision 6</a>. An individual may only have one residence address where the individual is domiciled at any particular time. The residence address of the individual is presumed to continue until the contrary is shown. The applicant must provide the following information about the residence address: residence number, street name, street type, directional, city or town, state, and zip code.</p>
          <p>(f) If an applicant designates a temporary mailing address under paragraph (a), clause (5), item (vii), the commissioner must use the temporary mailing address in lieu of the applicant's residence address for delivery of the driver's license or identification card. The commissioner must send all other correspondence to the applicant's residence address. Nothing in this paragraph or paragraph (a), clause (5), item (vii), may be construed to modify or remove proof of residency requirements at the time of application for an initial driver's permit, driver's license, or identification card.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.06.3a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/68/#laws.5.55.0">2023 c 68 art 5 s 55</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.06.3b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3b.<span class="headnote">Information for applicants.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner must develop summary information on identity document options. The summary information must be available on the department's website and at every location where a person may apply for an enhanced, REAL ID compliant, or noncompliant driver's license or identification card.</p>
          <p>(b) The summary information must, at a minimum, include:</p>
          <p>(1) each available type of driver's license and Minnesota identification card, including a noncompliant license or identification card, an enhanced driver's license, and an enhanced identification card;</p>
          <p>(2) the official purposes of and limitations on use for each type of driver's license and Minnesota identification card; and</p>
          <p>(3) an overview of data shared outside the state, including through electronic validation or verification systems, as part of the application and issuance of each type.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner must ensure that the summary information is available to driver's license and identification card applicants. Renewal notifications mailed to driver's license and identification card holders must include the website address that displays the summary information.</p>
          <p>(d) An applicant for an enhanced or noncompliant license or identification card must sign an acknowledgment that the applicant understands the limitations on use of the license or card.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) If the applicant does not indicate a desire to make an anatomical gift when the application is made, the applicant must be offered a donor document in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.07#stat.171.07.5">171.07, subdivision 5</a>. The application must contain statements sufficient to comply with the requirements of the Darlene Luther Revised Uniform Anatomical Gift Act, chapter 525A, so that execution of the application or donor document will make the anatomical gift as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.07#stat.171.07.5">171.07, subdivision 5</a>, for those indicating a desire to make an anatomical gift. The application must be accompanied by information describing Minnesota laws regarding anatomical gifts and the need for and benefits of anatomical gifts, and the legal implications of making an anatomical gift, including the law governing revocation of anatomical gifts. The commissioner shall distribute a notice that must accompany all applications for and renewals of a driver's license or Minnesota identification card. The notice must be prepared in conjunction with a Minnesota organ procurement organization that is certified by the federal Department of Health and Human Services and must include:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) a statement that provides a fair and reasonable description of the organ donation process, the care of the donor body after death, and the importance of informing family members of the donation decision; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) a telephone number in a certified Minnesota organ procurement organization that may be called with respect to questions regarding anatomical gifts.</p>
          <p class="in">(f) The application must be accompanied also by information containing relevant facts relating to:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the effect of alcohol on driving ability;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the effect of mixing alcohol with drugs;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the laws of Minnesota relating to operation of a motor vehicle while under the influence of alcohol or a controlled substance; and</p>
          <p class="in">(4) the levels of alcohol-related fatalities and accidents in Minnesota and of arrests for alcohol-related violations.</p>
          <p>(g) The commissioner must provide information on the department's website about the option for an applicant to designate a temporary mailing address. The information on the department's website must:</p>
          <p>(1) be easily accessible and address frequently asked questions;</p>
          <p>(2) detail the department's requirements for the use of a temporary mailing address;</p>
          <p>(3) compare the use of a temporary mailing address to the use of an applicant's residence address; and</p>
          <p>(4) clarify that a driver's license or identification card will not be delivered to a forwarded mail address.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.06.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/250/#laws.0.17.0">1997 c 250 s 17</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.06.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1979/0/150/#laws.0.2.0">1979 c 150 s 2</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.06.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Compliance with selective service system registration requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) By applying for an original, duplicate, or renewal instruction permit, provisional driver's license, driver's license, enhanced driver's license, commercial driver's license, state identification card, or enhanced identification card, an applicant under the age of 26, who is a United States citizen or resident, consents to registration in compliance with the requirements of the Military Selective Service Act, United States Code, title 50, appendix, section 453. The application form must state that submission of the application constitutes consent to registration with the selective service system, if required by federal law.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner shall forward to the selective service system in an electronic format the necessary personal information required for registration of an applicant described in paragraph (a). If the applicant is under the age of 18, and the license or card to be issued will expire after the applicant's 18th birthday, then the commissioner shall forward the necessary information to the selective service system when the applicant reaches the age of 18.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.06.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Remote application.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner must establish a process for an eligible individual to apply remotely for a driver's license or Minnesota identification card, whether through a website or other means, or a combination, as provided in this subdivision.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner may issue or reinstate an expired driver's license or Minnesota identification card and may renew a driver's license or Minnesota identification card for an eligible individual who does not apply in-person if:</p>
          <p>(1) the applicant submits documentation to demonstrate eligibility, as prescribed by the commissioner;</p>
          <p>(2) there is not a material change to the applicant's name, date of birth, signature, and driver's license or identification number since the most recent driver's license or Minnesota identification card issuance;</p>
          <p>(3) the application is not for a different type or class of driver's license or Minnesota identification card, as identified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.019#stat.171.019.2">171.019, subdivision 2</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.02">171.02, subdivision </a>2;</p>
          <p>(4) one of the following requirements is met:</p>
          <p>(i) the commissioner has a previous photograph of the applicant on file that was taken within the last five years or in conjunction with the most recent issuance; or</p>
          <p>(ii) for a noncompliant license or identification card, the applicant submits a photograph that meets the requirements of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.07">171.07</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.071">171.071</a>, Minnesota Rules, part <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/7410.1810">7410.1810</a>, subpart 1, and any other technical requirements established by the commissioner, which may include but are not limited to background color, lighting and visibility standards, and electronic file size;</p>
          <p>(5) for a driver's license, the commissioner has a record that the applicant has undergone an examination of the applicant's eyesight within the last two years, or the applicant submits a vision examination certificate that:</p>
          <p>(i) has been completed within the last two years;</p>
          <p>(ii) is signed by a licensed physician or an optometrist, including one who holds a similar license in a jurisdiction outside the United States; and</p>
          <p>(iii) is in a form as prescribed by the commissioner;</p>
          <p>(6) for an expired driver's license or Minnesota identification card:</p>
          <p>(i) expiration was within the past five years;</p>
          <p>(ii) expiration was due to driver's license or identification card issuance by another jurisdiction; and</p>
          <p>(iii) the application includes surrender or invalidation of a valid driver's license or identification card issued by another jurisdiction; and</p>
          <p>(7) the most recent issuance, reinstatement, or renewal was not performed under this subdivision.</p>
          <p>(c) A person who applies for a driver's license or Minnesota identification card under this subdivision is not required to:</p>
          <p>(1) take a knowledge examination;</p>
          <p>(2) take a road examination to demonstrate ability to exercise ordinary and reasonable control in the operation of a motor vehicle; and</p>
          <p>(3) appear in-person for an updated photograph upon return to Minnesota or release from incarceration, as appropriate.</p>
          <p>(d) For purposes of this subdivision, "eligible individual" means:</p>
          <p>(1) a person serving outside Minnesota in active military service, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/190.05#stat.190.05.5">190.05, subdivision 5</a>, in any branch or unit of the armed forces of the United States;</p>
          <p>(2) a person serving outside Minnesota as a volunteer in the Peace Corps;</p>
          <p>(3) a person who is an employee of a federal department or agency who is assigned to foreign service outside of the United States;</p>
          <p>(4) a person residing outside of Minnesota because the person is a spouse, domestic partner, or dependent under age 26 of a person in clause (1), (2), or (3); or</p>
          <p>(5) a person who applies for renewal and is serving a sentence of longer than six months in a Minnesota jail or correctional facility that has no existing agreement on renewals with the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.06.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Noncompliant license or identification card; residence, lawful status.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A person who applies for a noncompliant driver's license or identification card:</p>
          <p>(1) must declare the applicant's residence address in Minnesota; and</p>
          <p>(2) is not required to demonstrate United States citizenship or lawful presence in the United States.</p>
          <p>(b) Minnesota Rules, part <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/7410.0410">7410.0410</a>, or any successor rule, does not apply for a noncompliant driver's license or identification card.</p>
          <p>(c) Nothing in this section allows the commissioner to issue a commercial driver's license to an applicant who does not demonstrate proof of citizenship or immigration status if required by federal law.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.06.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.</h2>
          <p>MS 2023 Supp [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/104/#laws.1.110.0">2024 c 104 art 1 s 110</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.06.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.</h2>
          <p>MS 2023 Supp [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/104/#laws.1.110.0">2024 c 104 art 1 s 110</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.06.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.</h2>
          <p>MS 2023 Supp [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/104/#laws.1.110.0">2024 c 104 art 1 s 110</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.06.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/13">2720-144c</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/401/#laws.0.6.0">1939 c 401 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1949/0/689/#laws.0.1.0">1949 c 689 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/798/#laws.0.1.0">1955 c 798 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/0/564/#laws.0.2.0">1957 c 564 s 2</a>,3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/0/634/#laws.0.1.0">1959 c 634 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1965/0/36/#laws.0.1.0">1965 c 36 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/129/#laws.0.1.0">1971 c 129 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/625/#laws.0.2.0">1971 c 625 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/644/#laws.0.8.0">1971 c 644 s 8</a>,9; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/659/#laws.0.1.0">1971 c 659 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/59/#laws.0.1.0">1973 c 59 s 1</a>;       <p><b>NOTE: </b>(a) The amendments to subdivisions 1, 2, and 3, by Laws 2017, chapter 76, sections 7 to 9, and subdivision 3b, as added by Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 10, were effective May 19, 2017, and apply to driver's licenses and Minnesota identification cards applied for and issued on or after October 1, 2018. Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 29.</p>
      <p>(b) If the Federal REAL ID Act, Public Law 109-13, Division B, is repealed, or if the definition of "official purpose" in Code of Federal Regulations, title 6, part 37, is amended, the amendments to subdivisions 1, 2, and 3, by Laws 2017, chapter 76, sections 7 to 9, and subdivision 3b, as added by Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 10, are repealed on June 30 of the following year. Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 27.</p>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.0605">
        <h1 class="shn">171.0605 EVIDENCE OF IDENTITY AND LAWFUL PRESENCE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.0605.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope and application.</span></h2>
          <p>This section applies only to driver's licenses and Minnesota identification cards that meet all requirements of the REAL ID Act. Except as otherwise provided under this section, the requirements of Minnesota Rules, chapter 7410, or successor rules, apply.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.0605.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Evidence; identity; date of birth.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Only the following is satisfactory evidence of an applicant's identity and date of birth under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.06#stat.171.06.3">171.06, subdivision 3</a>, paragraph (b):</p>
          <p>(1) a driver's license or identification card that:</p>
          <p>(i) complies with all requirements of the REAL ID Act;</p>
          <p>(ii) is not designated as temporary or limited term; and</p>
          <p>(iii) is current or has been expired for five years or less;</p>
          <p>(2) a valid, unexpired United States passport, including a passport booklet or passport card, issued by the United States Department of State;</p>
          <p>(3) a certified copy of a birth certificate issued by a government bureau of vital statistics or equivalent agency in the applicant's state of birth, which must bear the raised or authorized seal of the issuing government entity;</p>
          <p>(4) a consular report of birth abroad, certification of report of birth, or certification of birth abroad, issued by the United States Department of State, Form FS-240, Form DS-1350, or Form FS-545;</p>
          <p>(5) a valid, unexpired permanent resident card issued by the United States Department of Homeland Security or the former Immigration and Naturalization Service of the United States Department of Justice, Form I-551. If the Form I-551 validity period has been automatically extended by the United States Department of Homeland Security, it is deemed unexpired, regardless of the expiration date listed;</p>
          <p>(6) a foreign passport with an unexpired temporary I-551 stamp or a temporary I-551 printed notation on a machine-readable immigrant visa with a United States Department of Homeland Security admission stamp within the validity period;</p>
          <p>(7) a United States Department of Homeland Security Form I-94 or Form I-94A with a photograph and an unexpired temporary I-551 stamp;</p>
          <p>(8) a United States Department of State Form DS-232 with a United States Department of Homeland Security admission stamp and validity period;</p>
          <p>(9) a certificate of naturalization issued by the United States Department of Homeland Security, Form N-550 or Form N-570;</p>
          <p>(10) a certificate of citizenship issued by the United States Department of Homeland Security, Form N-560 or Form N-561;</p>
