          <p>(11) an unexpired employment authorization document issued by the United States Department of Homeland Security, Form I-766 or Form I-688B. If the Form I-766 validity period has been automatically extended by the United States Department of Homeland Security, it is deemed unexpired, regardless of the expiration date listed;</p>
          <p>(12) a valid, unexpired passport issued by a foreign country and a valid, unexpired United States visa accompanied by documentation of the applicant's most recent lawful admittance into the United States;</p>
          <p>(13) a document as designated by the United States Department of Homeland Security under Code of Federal Regulations, title 6, part 37.11 (c)(1)(x);</p>
          <p>(14) a copy of the applicant's certificate of marriage certified by the issuing government jurisdiction;</p>
          <p>(15) a certified copy of a court order that specifies the applicant's name change; or</p>
          <p>(16) a certified copy of a divorce decree or dissolution of marriage that specifies the applicant's name change, issued by a court.</p>
          <p>(b) A document under paragraph (a) must be legible and unaltered.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.0605.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Evidence; lawful status.</span></h2>
          <p>Only a form of documentation identified under subdivision 2, paragraph (a), clauses (2) to (10), or a document issued by a federal agency that demonstrates the applicant's lawful status are satisfactory evidence of an applicant's lawful status under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.06#stat.171.06.3">171.06, subdivision 3</a>, paragraph (b), clause (2).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.0605.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/104/#laws.1.110.0">2024 c 104 art 1 s 110</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.0605.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Evidence; residence in Minnesota.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Submission of two forms of documentation from the following is satisfactory evidence of an applicant's principal residence address in Minnesota under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.06#stat.171.06.3">171.06, subdivision 3</a>, paragraph (b):</p>
          <p>(1) a home utility services bill issued no more than 12 months before the application;</p>
          <p>(2) a home utility services hook-up work order issued no more than 12 months before the application;</p>
          <p>(3) United States bank or financial information issued no more than 12 months before the application, with account numbers redacted, including:</p>
          <p>(i) a bank account statement;</p>
          <p>(ii) a credit card or debit card statement;</p>
          <p>(iii) a brokerage account statement;</p>
          <p>(iv) a money market account statement;</p>
          <p>(v) a Health Savings Account statement; or</p>
          <p>(vi) a retirement account statement;</p>
          <p>(4) a certified transcript from a United States high school, if issued no more than 180 days before the application;</p>
          <p>(5) a certified transcript from a Minnesota college or university, if issued no more than 180 days before the application;</p>
          <p>(6) a student summary report from a United States high school signed by a school principal or designated authority and issued no more than 180 days before the application;</p>
          <p>(7) an employment pay stub issued no more than 12 months before the application that lists the employer's name and address;</p>
          <p>(8) a Minnesota unemployment insurance benefit statement issued no more than 12 months before the application;</p>
          <p>(9) a statement from an assisted living facility licensed under chapter 144G, nursing home licensed under chapter 144A, or a boarding care facility licensed under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.50">144.50 </a>to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.56">144.56</a>, that was issued no more than 12 months before the application;</p>
          <p>(10) a current policy or card for health, automobile, homeowner's, or renter's insurance;</p>
          <p>(11) a federal or state income tax return for the most recent tax filing year;</p>
          <p>(12) a Minnesota property tax statement for the current or prior calendar year or a proposed Minnesota property tax notice for the current year that shows the applicant's principal residential address both on the mailing portion and the portion stating what property is being taxed;</p>
          <p>(13) a Minnesota vehicle certificate of title;</p>
          <p>(14) a filed property deed or title for current residence;</p>
          <p>(15) a Supplemental Security Income award statement issued no more than 12 months before the application;</p>
          <p>(16) mortgage documents for the applicant's principal residence;</p>
          <p>(17) a residential lease agreement for the applicant's principal residence issued no more than 12 months before the application;</p>
          <p>(18) an affidavit of residence for an applicant whose principal residence is a group home, communal living arrangement, cooperative, or a religious order issued no more than 90 days before the application;</p>
          <p>(19) an assisted living or nursing home statement issued no more than 90 days before the application;</p>
          <p>(20) a valid driver's license, including an instruction permit, issued under this chapter;</p>
          <p>(21) a valid Minnesota identification card;</p>
          <p>(22) an unexpired Minnesota professional license;</p>
          <p>(23) an unexpired Selective Service card;</p>
          <p>(24) military orders that are still in effect at the time of application;</p>
          <p>(25) a cellular phone bill issued no more than 12 months before the application; or</p>
          <p>(26) a valid license issued pursuant to the game and fish laws.</p>
          <p>(b) In lieu of one of the two documents required by paragraph (a), an applicant under the age of 18 may use a parent or guardian's proof of principal residence as provided in this paragraph. The parent or guardian of the applicant must provide a document listed under paragraph (a) that includes the parent or guardian's name and the same address as the address on the document provided by the applicant. The parent or guardian must also certify that the applicant is the child of the parent or guardian and lives at that address.</p>
          <p>(c) A document under paragraph (a) must include the applicant's name and principal residence address in Minnesota.</p>
          <p>(d) For purposes of this subdivision, Internet service and cable service are utilities.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.0605.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Exceptions process.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner may grant a variance from the requirements of this section as provided under Minnesota Rules, part <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/7410.0600">7410.0600</a>, or successor rules, for evidence of:</p>
          <p>(1) identity or date of birth under subdivision 2;</p>
          <p>(2) lawful status under subdivision 3, only for demonstration of United States citizenship; and</p>
          <p>(3) residence in Minnesota under subdivision 5.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner must not grant a variance for an applicant having a lawful temporary admission period.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.0605.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/76/#laws.0.11.0">2017 c 76 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/71/#laws.2.9.0">2020 c 71 art 2 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/7/1/#laws.6.25.0">7Sp2020 c 1 art 6 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/68/#laws.5.36.0">2023 c 68 art 5 s 36</a>,37; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/104/#laws.1.60.0">2024 c 104 art 1 s 60</a>-62</p>
      </div>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>(a) This section, as added by Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 11, was effective May 19, 2017, and applies to driver's licenses and Minnesota identification cards applied for and issued on or after October 1, 2018. Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 29.</p>
      <p>(b) If the Federal REAL ID Act, Public Law 109-13, Division B, is repealed, or if the definition of "official purpose" in Code of Federal Regulations, title 6, part 37, is amended, this section, as added by Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 11, is repealed on June 30 of the following year. Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 27.</p>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.061">
        <h1 class="shn">171.061 DRIVER'S LICENSE AGENT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.061.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of this section:</p>
          <p>(1) "applicant" means an individual applying for a driver's license, provisional license, restricted license, duplicate license, instruction permit, Minnesota identification card, or motorized bicycle operator's permit; and</p>
          <p>(2) "application" refers to an application for a driver's license, provisional license, restricted license, duplicate license, instruction permit, Minnesota identification card, or motorized bicycle operator's permit.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.061.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Appointment and discontinuance.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner of public safety may appoint an individual, and for cause discontinue the appointment of an agent, to serve as a driver's license agent.</p>
          <p>(b) A county board may appoint an individual, and for cause discontinue the appointment of an agent, to serve as an agent, pursuant to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/373.32">373.32</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/373.38">373.38</a>, with the approval of the commissioner. If a county board does not discontinue an agent's appointment, although cause for discontinuance exists, the commissioner may discontinue the appointment. If a county board does not appoint an individual, the commissioner may establish an office and appoint an individual to accept applications as the public interest and convenience may require.</p>
          <p>(c) The county board is responsible for the acts of an agent appointed by the board and for forwarding to the department all applications accepted and fees collected by the agent as required by the department.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.061.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Application.</span></h2>
          <p>An applicant may file an application with an agent. The agent shall receive and accept applications in accordance with the laws and rules of the Department of Public Safety for a noncompliant driver's license or identification card; an enhanced driver's license or identification card; a REAL ID compliant driver's license or identification card; restricted license; duplicate license; instruction permit; or motorized bicycle operator's permit. Application records must be maintained at the office of the agent in a manner that complies with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.05#stat.13.05.5">13.05, subdivision 5</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.055">13.055</a>. As an alternative to paper copy storage, an agent may retain records and documents in a secure electronic medium that complies with the security requirements under the United States Federal Bureau of Investigation, Criminal Justice Information Services Division, Policy 5.4 or any successor policy, provided 60 days have elapsed since the transaction and subject to standards established by the commissioner. The agent is responsible for all costs associated with the conversion to electronic records and maintenance of the electronic storage medium, including the destruction of existing paper records after conversion to the electronic format. All queries and responses in the secure electronic medium, and all actions in which data are entered, updated, accessed, or shared or disseminated by the agent must be contained in a data audit trail. Data contained in the audit trail are public to the extent the data are not otherwise classified under this section.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.061.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Fee; equipment.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The agent may charge and retain a filing fee for each application as follows:</p>
          <table class="border_none">
            <col style="width:11.5384615384615%">
            <col style="width:73.0769230769231%">
            <col style="width:6.153846153846154%">
            <col style="width:9.23076923076923%">
            <tr>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: right;">(1) </td>
              <td class="valign_top">New application for a noncompliant, REAL ID-compliant, or enhanced driver's license or identification card</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: right;">$</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: right;">16.00</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: right;">(2)</td>
              <td class="valign_top">Renewal application for a noncompliant, REAL ID-compliant, or enhanced driver's license or identification card </td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: right;">$</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: right;">11.00</td>
            </tr>
          </table>
          <p>Except as provided in paragraph (c), the fee must cover all expenses involved in receiving, accepting, or forwarding to the department the applications and fees required under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.02#stat.171.02.3">171.02, subdivision 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.06">171.06</a>, subdivisions 2 and 2a; and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.07">171.07</a>, subdivisions 3 and 3a.</p>
          <p>(b) The statutory fees and the filing fees imposed under paragraph (a) may be paid by credit card or debit card. The driver's license agent may collect a convenience fee on the statutory fees and filing fees not greater than the cost of processing a credit card or debit card transaction. The convenience fee must be used to pay the cost of processing credit card and debit card transactions. The commissioner must adopt rules to administer this paragraph using the exempt procedures of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.386">14.386</a>, except that section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.386">14.386</a>, paragraph (b), does not apply.</p>
          <p>(c) The department must maintain the photo identification and vision examination equipment for all agents. All photo identification and vision examination equipment must be compatible with standards established by the department.</p>
          <p>(d) A filing fee retained by the agent employed by a county board must be paid into the county treasury and credited to the general revenue fund of the county. An agent who is not an employee of the county must retain the filing fee in lieu of county employment or salary and is considered an independent contractor for pension purposes, coverage under the Minnesota State Retirement System, or membership in the Public Employees Retirement Association.</p>
          <p>(e) Before the end of the first working day following the final day of the reporting period established by the department, the agent must forward to the department all applications and fees collected during the reporting period except as provided in paragraph (d).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.061.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Discontinuance or transfer of appointment.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) An agent shall notify the department no less than 30 days before the discontinuance of service.</p>
          <p>(b) In the event of the notice specified in paragraph (a); death or retirement of the agent; or revocation or discontinuance of the appointment of the agent by the county board or commissioner, the appointment terminates and all equipment provided by the department reverts to the department.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.061.5a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5a.<span class="headnote">Competitive bidding.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Notwithstanding any statute or rule to the contrary, if a driver's license agent appointed under this section permanently stops offering services at the approved office location and permanently closes the approved office location, the commissioner must use a competitive bidding process for the appointment of a replacement driver's license agent. If available, the replacement driver's license agent appointed by the commissioner under this section must continue to offer services at the approved office location. If the existing office location is not available to the replacement driver's license agent, the replacement office location must be at a location that must be approved by the commissioner and must serve a similar service area as the existing office location.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner must not give a preference to a partner, owner, manager, or employee of the driver's license agent that has permanently stopped offering services at the closed office location in a competitive bidding process.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner must adopt rules to administer and enforce a competitive bidding process to select a replacement driver's license agent. If the replacement driver's license agent elects to not offer services at the office location of the prior agent, Minnesota Rules, chapter 7404, governing the selection of a proposed office location of a driver's license agent, applies.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.061.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Rules.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall adopt rules that prescribe:</p>
          <p>(1) criteria, procedures, and requirements for appointing an individual as an agent of the commissioner;</p>
          <p>(2) criteria for establishment, operation, management, location, and movement of a license application office;</p>
          <p>(3) standards for the uniform administration of laws and rules governing the receipt of applications and fees for applications;</p>
          <p>(4) number of applications to be processed;</p>
          <p>(5) standards for submitting applications including valid forms of identification, depositing funds, maintaining records, and holding proper bonds; and</p>
          <p>(6) standards for discontinuing the individual's appointment and for enforcement action.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.061.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/250/#laws.0.10.0">1997 c 250 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/86/#laws.1.41.0">1999 c 86 art 1 s 41</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/238/#laws.2.26.0">1999 c 238 art 2 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/479/#laws.1.18.0">2000 c 479 art 1 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/6/#laws.2.38.0">1Sp2005 c 6 art 2 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/287/#laws.4.34.0">2012 c 287 art 4 s 34</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/117/#laws.3.11.0">2013 c 117 art 3 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/76/#laws.0.12.0">2017 c 76 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/3/#laws.3.75.0">1Sp2017 c 3 art 3 s 75</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/68/#laws.3.16.0">2023 c 68 art 3 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.3.67.0">2024 c 127 art 3 s 67</a></p>
      </div>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>(a) The amendment to subdivision 3 by Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 12, was effective May 19, 2017, and applies to driver's licenses and Minnesota identification cards applied for and issued on or after October 1, 2018. Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 29.</p>
      <p>(b) If the Federal REAL ID Act, Public Law 109-13, Division B, is repealed, or if the definition of "official purpose" in Code of Federal Regulations, title 6, part 37, is amended, the amendment to subdivision 3 by Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 12, is repealed on June 30 of the following year. Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 27.</p>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>Subdivision 5a, as added by Laws 2024, chapter 127, article 3, section 67, is effective October 1, 2025. Laws 2024, chapter 127, article 3, section 67, the effective date.</p>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.062">
        <h1 class="shn">171.062 EVIDENCE OF IDENTITY; NONCOMPLIANT CREDENTIALS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.062.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For purposes of this section, the following terms have the meanings given.</p>
          <p>(b) "Court" includes a foreign court of competent jurisdiction.</p>
          <p>(c) "Foreign" means a jurisdiction that is not, and is not within, the United States, the Commonwealth of the Northern Mariana Islands, the Commonwealth of Puerto Rico, Guam, the United States Virgin Islands, or a territory of the United States.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.062.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Evidence of identity.</span></h2>
          <p>For a noncompliant license or identification card, an applicant must submit:</p>
          <p>(1) a Minnesota driver's license or identification card that is current or has been expired:</p>
          <p>(i) for five years or less with a color photograph or electronically produced or digitized image; or</p>
          <p>(ii) for one year or less without a color photograph or electronically produced or digitized image; or</p>
          <p>(2) if the applicant cannot present a credential under clause (1), either:</p>
          <p>(i) one primary document under subdivision 4 and one secondary document under subdivision 5; or</p>
          <p>(ii) two primary documents under subdivision 4.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.062.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">General requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A document submitted under this section must include the applicant's name and must be:</p>
          <p>(1) issued to or provided for the applicant;</p>
          <p>(2) legible and unaltered;</p>
          <p>(3) an original or a copy certified by the issuing agency or by a court; and</p>
          <p>(4) accompanied by a certified translation or an affidavit of translation into English, if the document is not in English.</p>
          <p>(b) If the applicant's current legal name is different from the name on a document submitted under subdivision 4 or 5, the applicant must submit:</p>
          <p>(1) a certified copy of a court order that specifies the applicant's name change;</p>
          <p>(2) a certified copy of the applicant's certificate of marriage;</p>
          <p>(3) a certified copy of a divorce decree or dissolution of marriage that specifies the applicant's name change, issued by a court; or</p>
          <p>(4) similar documentation of a lawful change of name, as determined by the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(c) A form issued by a federal agency that is specified under this section includes any subsequent form or version.</p>
          <p>(d) The commissioner must establish a process to grant a waiver from the requirements under this section.</p>
          <p>(e) The same document must not be submitted as both a primary document and a secondary document.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.062.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Primary documents.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For purposes of a noncompliant driver's license or identification card, a primary document includes:</p>
          <p>(1) a copy of the applicant's record of birth, or an original certificate of birth that is in the files of the applicable bureau or board under item (iii) and can be readily viewed by the official accepting the application, certified by the issuing state that:</p>
          <p>(i) is not issued by a hospital and is not a baptismal certificate;</p>
          <p>(ii) bears the raised or authorized seal of the issuing government jurisdiction or a protective equivalent; and</p>
          <p>(iii) is issued by:</p>
          <p>(A) a government bureau of vital statistics or community health board;</p>
          <p>(B) the United States Department of State as a Record of Birth Abroad, Form FS-545 or Form DS-1350; or</p>
          <p>(C) a United States embassy as a Consular Report of Birth Abroad, Form FS-240;</p>
          <p>(2) a certified copy of an adoption certificate with the applicant's full name and date of birth from a United States court of competent jurisdiction that bears the raised court seal or other court certification;</p>
          <p>(3) an unexpired identification card issued to the applicant by the United States Department of Defense for active duty, reserve, or retired military personnel, Form DD-2 or Common Access Card;</p>
          <p>(4) a valid, unexpired passport issued to the applicant by the United States Department of State;</p>
          <p>(5) a Canadian birth certificate or Canadian naturalization certificate;</p>
          <p>(6) one of the following documents issued by the United States Department of Justice or the United States Department of Homeland Security or any subsequent form or version of the documents:</p>
          <p>(i) Certificate of Naturalization, Form N-550, Form N-570, or Form N-578;</p>
          <p>(ii) Certificate of Citizenship, Form N-560, Form N-561, or Form N-645;</p>
          <p>(iii) United States Citizen Identification card, Form I-179 or Form I-197;</p>
          <p>(iv) valid, unexpired Permanent Resident or Resident Alien card, Form I-551 or Form I-151;</p>
          <p>(v) Northern Mariana card, Form I-873, with "Northern Mariana" imprinted instead of "Resident Alien";</p>
          <p>(vi) American Indian card, Form I-872, with "American Indian" imprinted instead of "Resident Alien";</p>
          <p>(vii) unexpired employment authorization document with a photograph, Form I-688, Form I-688A, Form I-688B, or Form I-766; or</p>
          <p>(viii) unexpired Re-entry Permit/Refugee Travel Document, Form I-571;</p>
          <p>(7) an unexpired passport or a consular identification document that bears a photograph of the applicant;</p>
          <p>(8) a certified birth certificate issued by a foreign jurisdiction; and</p>
          <p>(9) a certified adoption certificate issued by a foreign jurisdiction that includes the applicant's name and date of birth.</p>
          <p>(b) A document submitted under this subdivision must contain security features that make the document as impervious to alteration as is reasonably practicable in its design and quality of material and technology.</p>
          <p>(c) Submission of more than one primary document is not required under this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.062.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Secondary documents.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For purposes of a noncompliant driver's license or identification card, a secondary document includes:</p>
          <p>(1) a second primary document listed under subdivision 4, paragraph (a);</p>
          <p>(2) a driver's license, identification card, or permit, with a photograph or digitized image, issued by a United States state other than Minnesota or a foreign jurisdiction and that is current or has expired no more than five years before the application;</p>
          <p>(3) a certified copy of a court order or judgment from a United States or Canadian court of competent jurisdiction containing the applicant's full name and date of birth and bearing the raised court seal or other court certification;</p>
          <p>(4) a current United States or Canadian government jurisdiction employee photo identification card;</p>
          <p>(5) a certified copy of a record of birth issued by a government jurisdiction other than one in the United States, the District of Columbia, Guam, Puerto Rico, or the United States Virgin Islands;</p>
          <p>(6) a current identification card or document issued to the applicant by the United States Department of Defense, described as:</p>
          <p>(i) DD Form 1173 series, for dependents of active duty personnel; or</p>
          <p>(ii) DD Form 214, Certificate of Release or Discharge from Active Duty;</p>
          <p>(7) a copy of a marriage certificate certified by the issuing government jurisdiction or the original certificate only if it is in the files of the issuing jurisdiction and can be readily viewed by the official accepting the application;</p>
          <p>(8) an unexpired permit to carry a firearm or concealed weapon bearing a color photo of the applicant issued by a chief of police in an organized, full-time United States police department or by a United States county sheriff;</p>
          <p>(9) a current pilot's license issued by the United States Department of Transportation, Federal Aviation Administration;</p>
          <p>(10) a copy of a transcript containing the applicant's full legal name and date of birth certified by the issuing secondary or postsecondary school;</p>
          <p>(11) a United States nonmetal Social Security card or a Canadian social insurance card;</p>
          <p>(12) a current secondary school student identification card with the student's name, a photograph or electronically produced image of the student, and the student's date of birth or unique student identification number;</p>
          <p>(13) a notice of action on or proof of submission of a completed Application for Asylum and for Withholding of Removal issued by the United States Department of Homeland Security, Form I-589;</p>
          <p>(14) a Certificate of Eligibility for Nonimmigrant Student Status issued by the United States Department of Homeland Security, Form I-20;</p>
          <p>(15) a Certificate of Eligibility for Exchange Visitor (J-1) Status issued by the United States Department of State, Form DS-2019;</p>
          <p>(16) a Deferred Action for Childhood Arrival approval notice issued by the United States Department of Homeland Security;</p>
          <p>(17) an employment authorization document issued by the United States Department of Homeland Security, Form I-688, Form I-688A, Form I-688B, or Form I-766;</p>
          <p>(18) a document issued by the Internal Revenue Service with an individual taxpayer identification number;</p>
          <p>(19) a Social Security card;</p>
          <p>(20) a Supplemental Security Income award statement;</p>
          <p>(21) a Selective Service card;</p>
          <p>(22) military orders that are still in effect at the time of the application with a copy of a DD Form 2058 State of Legal Residence Certificate;</p>
          <p>(23) a Minnesota unemployment insurance benefit statement;</p>
          <p>(24) a valid identification card for health benefits or an assistance or social services program;</p>
          <p>(25) a Minnesota vehicle certificate of title;</p>
          <p>(26) mortgage documents for the applicant's residence;</p>
          <p>(27) a filed property deed or title for the applicant's residence;</p>
          <p>(28) a Minnesota property tax statement or a proposed Minnesota property tax notice;</p>
          <p>(29) a certified copy of a divorce decree or dissolution of marriage issued by a court that specifies the applicant's name or name change;</p>
          <p>(30) a valid Department of Corrections or Federal Bureau of Prisons identification card containing the applicant's full name, date of birth, and photograph; and</p>
          <p>(31) any of the following documents issued by a foreign jurisdiction:</p>
          <p>(i) a driver's license that is current or has been expired for no more than five years before the application;</p>
          <p>(ii) a high school, college, or university student identification card with a certified transcript from the school;</p>
          <p>(iii) an official high school, college, or university transcript that includes the applicant's date of birth and a photograph of the applicant at the age the record was issued;</p>
          <p>(iv) a federal electoral card that contains the applicant's photograph issued on or after January 1, 1991;</p>
          <p>(v) a certified copy of the applicant's certificate of marriage; and</p>
          <p>(vi) a certified copy of a court order or judgment from a court of competent jurisdiction that contains the applicant's name and date of birth.</p>
          <p>(b) Submission of more than one secondary document is not required under this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.062.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Verification.</span></h2>
          <p>The department must be able to verify with the issuing jurisdiction the issuance and authenticity of the primary or secondary documents submitted under this section. Verification is required if:</p>
          <p>(1) the document provided by the applicant is inconsistent with the department record;</p>
          <p>(2) the document provided by the applicant appears to be altered or fraudulent; or</p>
          <p>(3) there is reason to believe the applicant is not who the applicant claims to be.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.062.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/104/#laws.1.63.0">2024 c 104 art 1 s 63</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.063">
        <h1 class="shn">171.063 EVIDENCE OF IDENTITY FOR ENHANCED CREDENTIALS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.063.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Date of birth.</span></h2>
          <p>As satisfactory evidence of date of birth, an applicant for an enhanced driver's license or an enhanced identification card must present one of the following documents:</p>
          <p>(1) original or certified copy of a United States or United States territory birth certificate that bears the raised or authorized seal of the issuing jurisdiction or a protective equivalent;</p>
          <p>(2) United States Department of State Consular Report of Birth Abroad, Form FS-240, Form DS-1350, or Form FS-545;</p>
          <p>(3) valid, unexpired United States passport or United States passport card;</p>
          <p>(4) Certificate of Naturalization, Form N-550 or Form N-570;</p>
          <p>(5) Certificate of Citizenship, Form N-560 or Form N-561;</p>
          <p>(6) American Indian card, Form I-872, or Minnesota tribal identification card that meets the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.072">171.072</a>; or</p>
          <p>(7) United States military photo identification card issued to active duty, reserve, or retired military personnel.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.063.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Full legal name.</span></h2>
          <p>As satisfactory evidence of full legal name, an applicant for an enhanced driver's license or enhanced identification card must present one of the following documents that was not also presented for proof of photographic identity under subdivision 4:</p>
          <p>(1) original or certified copy of a United States or United States territory birth certificate that bears the raised or authorized seal of the issuing jurisdiction or a protective equivalent;</p>
          <p>(2) United States Department of State Consular Report of Birth Abroad, Form FS-240, Form DS-1350, or Form FS-545;</p>
          <p>(3) valid, unexpired United States passport or United States passport card;</p>
          <p>(4) Certificate of Naturalization, Form N-550 or Form N-570;</p>
          <p>(5) Certificate of Citizenship, Form N-560 or Form N-561;</p>
          <p>(6) American Indian card, Form I-872, or Minnesota tribal identification card that meets the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.072">171.072</a>;</p>
          <p>(7) United States military photo identification card issued to active duty, reserve, or retired military personnel;</p>
          <p>(8) federal or Minnesota income tax form W-2;</p>
          <p>(9) federal or Minnesota income tax form SSA-1099;</p>
          <p>(10) non-SSA federal or Minnesota income tax form 1099;</p>
          <p>(11) United States high school identification card with a certified transcript from the same school if issued no more than 180 days before the application;</p>
          <p>(12) United States college or university identification card with a certified transcript from the same college or university if issued no more than 180 days before the application;</p>
          <p>(13) Minnesota unemployment insurance benefit statement issued no more than 90 days before the application;</p>
          <p>(14) life, health, automobile, homeowner's, or renter's insurance policy that is issued no more than 90 days before the application. The commissioner must not accept a proof of insurance card;</p>
          <p>(15) federal or state income tax return or statement for the most recent tax filing year;</p>
          <p>(16) Minnesota property tax statement for the current year that reflects the applicant's principal residential address both on the mailing portion and the portion stating what property is being taxed;</p>
          <p>(17) Minnesota vehicle certificate of title if issued no more than 12 months before the application;</p>
          <p>(18) filed property deed or title for the applicant's current residence if issued no more than 12 months before the application;</p>
          <p>(19) Supplemental Security Income award statement that is issued no more than 12 months before the application;</p>
          <p>(20) valid Minnesota driver's license, valid Minnesota identification card, or valid permit;</p>
          <p>(21) unexpired Minnesota professional license;</p>
          <p>(22) unexpired Selective Service card;</p>
          <p>(23) military orders that are still in effect at the time of the application;</p>
          <p>(24) copy of the applicant's certificate of marriage certified by the issuing government jurisdiction;</p>
          <p>(25) certified copy of a court order specifying a name change; or</p>
          <p>(26) certified copy of a divorce decree or dissolution of marriage granted to the applicant that specifies a name change requested from a court of competent jurisdiction.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.063.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Social Security number.</span></h2>
          <p>As satisfactory evidence of Social Security number, an applicant for an enhanced driver's license or an enhanced identification card must present the applicant's original Social Security card or one of the following:</p>
          <p>(1) federal or Minnesota income tax form W-2;</p>
          <p>(2) federal or Minnesota income tax form SSA-1099;</p>
          <p>(3) non-SSA federal or Minnesota income tax form 1099; or</p>
          <p>(4) United States employment computer-printed pay stub containing the applicant's name, address, and full Social Security number.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.063.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Photographic identity.</span></h2>
          <p>As satisfactory evidence of photographic identity, an applicant for an enhanced driver's license or an enhanced identification card must present one of the following documents:</p>
          <p>(1) valid Minnesota driver's license, identification card, or permit;</p>
          <p>(2) valid driver's license, identification card, or permit issued by another United States state, including the District of Columbia and any United States territory;</p>
          <p>(3) United States military identification card issued to active duty, reserve, or retired military personnel;</p>
          <p>(4) United States military dependent identification card;</p>
          <p>(5) valid, unexpired United States passport or United States passport card;</p>
          <p>(6) American Indian card, Form I-872, or Minnesota tribal identification card that meets the requirements under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.072">171.072</a>;</p>
          <p>(7) valid city, county, state, or federal employee identification card;</p>
          <p>(8) United States high school identification card with a certified transcript from the same school, both issued no more than 180 days before the application;</p>
          <p>(9) United States college or university identification card with a certified transcript from the same college or university, both issued no more than 180 days before the application; or</p>
          <p>(10) veterans universal access identification card.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.063.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">United States citizenship.</span></h2>
          <p>As satisfactory evidence of United States citizenship, an applicant for an enhanced driver's license or enhanced identification card must present one of the following documents:</p>
          <p>(1) original or certified copy of a United States or United States territory birth certificate that bears the raised or authorized seal of the issuing jurisdiction or a protective equivalent;</p>
          <p>(2) United States Department of State Consular Report of Birth Abroad, Form FS-240, Form DS-1350, or Form FS-545;</p>
          <p>(3) valid, unexpired United States passport or United States passport card;</p>
          <p>(4) Certificate of Naturalization, Form N-550 or Form N-570; or</p>
          <p>(5) Certificate of Citizenship, Form N-560 or Form N-561.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.063.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Residency.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) As satisfactory evidence of residency, an applicant for an enhanced driver's license or enhanced identification card must present two different forms of the following documents that list the applicant's name and address:</p>
          <p>(1) United States home utility services bill that is issued no more than 90 days before the application. The commissioner must not accept a United States home utility bill if two unrelated people are listed on the bill;</p>
          <p>(2) United States home utility services hook-up work order that is issued no more than 90 days before the application. The commissioner must not accept a United States home utility services hook-up work order if two unrelated people are listed on the work order;</p>
          <p>(3) United States financial information with account numbers redacted that is issued no more than 90 days before the application, including a:</p>
          <p>(i) bank account statement;</p>
          <p>(ii) canceled check; or</p>
          <p>(iii) credit card statement;</p>
          <p>(4) United States high school identification card with a certified transcript from the same school if issued no more than 180 days before the application;</p>
          <p>(5) United States college or university identification card with a certified transcript from the same college or university if issued no more than 180 days before the application;</p>
          <p>(6) United States employment pay stub that lists the employer's name, address, and telephone number that is issued no more than 90 days before the application;</p>
          <p>(7) Minnesota unemployment insurance benefit statement issued no more than 90 days before the application;</p>
          <p>(8) assisted living or nursing home statement that is issued no more than 90 days before the application;</p>
          <p>(9) life, health, automobile, homeowner's, or renter's insurance policy that is issued no more than 90 days before the application. The commissioner must not accept a proof of insurance card;</p>
          <p>(10) federal or state income tax return or statement for the most recent tax filing year;</p>
          <p>(11) Minnesota property tax statement for the current year that reflects the applicant's principal residential address both on the mailing portion and the portion stating what property is being taxed;</p>
          <p>(12) Minnesota vehicle certificate of title if issued no more than 12 months before the application;</p>
          <p>(13) filed property deed or title for the applicant's current residence if issued no more than 12 months before the application;</p>
          <p>(14) Supplemental Security Income award statement that is issued no more than 12 months before the application;</p>
          <p>(15) mortgage documents for the applicant's principal residence;</p>
          <p>(16) residential lease agreement for the applicant's principal residence that is issued no more than 12 months before the application;</p>
          <p>(17) valid Minnesota driver's license, identification card, or permit;</p>
          <p>(18) unexpired Minnesota professional license;</p>
          <p>(19) unexpired Selective Service card; or</p>
          <p>(20) military orders that are still in effect at the time of the application with a copy of a DD Form 2058 State of Legal Residence Certificate.</p>
          <p>(b) For purposes of this subdivision, Internet service and cable service are utilities.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The commissioner must verify with the United States Postal Service the address information provided under this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.063.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Verification.</span></h2>
          <p>The department must be able to verify with the issuing jurisdiction the issuance and authenticity of the documents submitted under this section. Verification is required if:</p>
          <p>(1) the document provided by the applicant is inconsistent with the department record;</p>
          <p>(2) the document provided by the applicant appears to be altered or fraudulent; or</p>
          <p>(3) there is reason to believe the applicant is not who the applicant claims to be.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.063.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/104/#laws.1.64.0">2024 c 104 art 1 s 64</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.066">
        <h1 class="shn">171.066 ENHANCED DRIVER'S LICENSE FOR EMPLOYMENT.</h1>
        <p>No employer may require an employee to apply for or use an enhanced driver's license as a condition of employment, nor may an employer discharge an employee or otherwise discriminate or retaliate against an employee who refuses to apply for or use an enhanced driver's license.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.066.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/316/#laws.0.4.0">2010 c 316 s 4,18</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.068">
        <h1 class="shn">171.068 ENHANCED IDENTIFICATION CARD FOR EMPLOYMENT.</h1>
        <p>No employer may require an employee to apply for or use an enhanced identification card as a condition of employment, nor may an employer discharge an employee or otherwise discriminate or retaliate against an employee who refuses to apply for or use an enhanced identification card.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.068.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/316/#laws.0.10.0">2010 c 316 s 10,18</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.069">
        <h1 class="shn">171.069 TRANSLATIONS.</h1>
        <p>For any document submitted to the commissioner under this chapter in a language other than English:</p>
        <p>(1) the document must be accompanied by a translation of that document into the English language;</p>
        <p>(2) the translation must be sworn to by the translator as being a true and accurate translation;</p>
        <p>(3) the translator must not be related by blood or marriage to the applicant; and</p>
        <p>(4) the translator must be:</p>
        <p>(i) accredited by the American Translators Association;</p>
        <p>(ii) certified by a court of competent jurisdiction;</p>
        <p>(iii) approved by an embassy or consulate of the United States or diplomatic or consular official of a foreign country assigned or accredited to the United States;</p>
        <p>(iv) affiliated with or approved by the United States Citizenship and Immigration Services or a government jurisdiction within the United States;</p>
        <p>(v) an attorney licensed to practice in the United States or affiliated with that attorney;</p>
        <p>(vi) a vendor listed to provide translation services for the state of Minnesota; or</p>
        <p>(vii) a qualified individual who certifies the individual is competent to translate the document into English.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.069.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/104/#laws.1.65.0">2024 c 104 art 1 s 65</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.07">
        <h1 class="shn">171.07 INFORMATION ON LICENSE AND IDENTIFICATION CARD.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.07.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">License; contents and design.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Upon the payment of the required fee, the department shall issue to every qualifying applicant a license designating the type or class of vehicles the applicant is authorized to drive as applied for. This license must bear: (1) a distinguishing number assigned to the licensee; (2) the licensee's full name and date of birth; (3) either (i) the licensee's residence address, or (ii) the designated address under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/5B.05">5B.05</a>; (4) a description of the licensee in a manner as the commissioner deems necessary; (5) the usual signature of the licensee; and (6) designations and markings as provided in this section. No license is valid unless it bears the usual signature of the licensee. Every license must bear a colored photograph or an electronically produced image of the licensee.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) If the United States Postal Service will not deliver mail to the applicant's residence address as listed on the license, then the applicant shall provide verification from the United States Postal Service that mail will not be delivered to the applicant's residence address and that mail will be delivered to a specified alternate mailing address. When an applicant provides an alternate mailing address under this subdivision, the commissioner shall use the alternate mailing address in lieu of the applicant's residence address for all notices and mailings to the applicant.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) Every license issued to an applicant under the age of 21 must be of a distinguishing color and plainly marked "Under-21."</p>
          <p class="in">(d) A license issued to an applicant age 65 or over must be plainly marked "senior" if requested by the applicant.</p>
          <p>(e) Except for a noncompliant license, a license must bear a distinguishing indicator for compliance with requirements of the REAL ID Act.</p>
          <p>(f) A noncompliant license must:</p>
          <p>(1) be marked "not for federal identification" on the face and in the machine-readable portion;</p>
          <p>(2) have a unique design or color indicator for purposes of the REAL ID Act; and</p>
          <p>(3) bear no indicator or design that relates to the lawful presence or the citizenship of the license holder.</p>
          <p>(g) A REAL ID compliant license issued to a person with temporary lawful status or admission period must be marked "temporary" on the face and in the machine-readable portion.</p>
          <p>(h) A license must display the licensee's full name or no fewer than 39 characters of the name. Any necessary truncation must begin with the last character of the middle name and proceed through the second letter of the middle name, followed by the last character of the first name and proceeding through the second letter of the first name.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.07.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Filing photograph or image; data classification.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The department shall file, or contract to file, all photographs or electronically produced images obtained in the process of issuing drivers' licenses or Minnesota identification cards. The photographs or electronically produced images shall be private data pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.02#stat.13.02.12">13.02, subdivision 12</a>. Notwithstanding section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.04#stat.13.04.3">13.04, subdivision 3</a>, the department shall not be required to provide copies of photographs or electronically produced images to data subjects. The use of the files is restricted:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) to the issuance and control of drivers' licenses;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) to criminal justice agencies, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/299C.46#stat.299C.46.2">299C.46, subdivision 2</a>, for the investigation and prosecution of crimes, service of process, enforcement of no contact orders, location of missing persons, investigation and preparation of cases for criminal, juvenile, and traffic court, location of individuals required to register under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/243.166">243.166 </a>or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/243.167">243.167</a>, and supervision of offenders;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) to public defenders, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/611.272">611.272</a>, for the investigation and preparation of cases for criminal, juvenile, and traffic courts;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) to child support enforcement purposes under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518A.83">518A.83</a>; and</p>
          <p class="in">(5) to a county medical examiner or coroner as required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/390.005">390.005</a> as necessary to fulfill the duties under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/390.11">390.11</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/390.25">390.25</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.07.1b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1b.<span class="headnote">Commercial driver's license.</span></h2>
          <p>Each class C, class B, or class A driver's license must be clearly marked "Minnesota Commercial Driver's License."</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.07.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Limitations on issuing license.</span></h2>
          <p>The department shall not issue a class B or class A license to anyone who does not have or has not qualified for a class D license. A class A license shall not be issued to anyone under 18 years of age.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.07.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Identification card; content and design; fee.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Upon payment of the required fee, the department shall issue to every qualifying applicant a Minnesota identification card. The department may not issue a Minnesota identification card to an individual who has a driver's license, other than a limited license. The department may not issue an enhanced identification card to an individual who is under 16 years of age, not a resident of this state, or not a citizen of the United States of America. The card must bear: (1) a distinguishing number assigned to the applicant; (2) a colored photograph or an electronically produced image of the applicant; (3) the applicant's full name and date of birth; (4) either (i) the licensee's residence address, or (ii) the designated address under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/5B.05">5B.05</a>; (5) a description of the applicant in the manner as the commissioner deems necessary; (6) the usual signature of the applicant; and (7) designations and markings provided under this section.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) If the United States Postal Service will not deliver mail to the applicant's residence address as listed on the Minnesota identification card, then the applicant shall provide verification from the United States Postal Service that mail will not be delivered to the applicant's residence address and that mail will be delivered to a specified alternate mailing address. When an applicant provides an alternate mailing address under this subdivision, the commissioner shall use the alternate mailing address in lieu of the applicant's residence address for all notices and mailings to the applicant.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) Each identification card issued to an applicant under the age of 21 must be of a distinguishing color and plainly marked "Under-21."</p>
          <p class="in">(d) Each Minnesota identification card must be plainly marked "Minnesota identification card - not a driver's license."</p>
          <p>(e) Except for a noncompliant identification card, a Minnesota identification card must bear a distinguishing indicator for compliance with requirements of the REAL ID Act.</p>
          <p>(f) A noncompliant identification card must:</p>
          <p>(1) be marked "not for federal identification" on the face and in the machine-readable portion;</p>
          <p>(2) have a unique design or color indicator for purposes of the REAL ID Act; and</p>
          <p>(3) bear no indicator or design that relates to the lawful presence or the citizenship of the identification card holder.</p>
          <p>(g) A REAL ID compliant Minnesota identification card issued to a person with temporary lawful status or admission period must be marked "temporary" on the face and in the machine-readable portion.</p>
          <p>(h) A Minnesota identification card must display the cardholder's full name or no fewer than 39 characters of the name. Any necessary truncation must begin with the last character of the middle name and proceed through the second letter of the middle name, followed by the last character of the first name and proceeding through the second letter of the first name.</p>
          <p class="in">(i) The fee for a Minnesota identification card is 50 cents when issued to a person who is developmentally disabled, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/252A.02#stat.252A.02.2">252A.02, subdivision 2</a>; a physically disabled person, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.345#stat.169.345.2">169.345, subdivision 2</a>; or, a person with mental illness, as described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.462#stat.245.462.20">245.462, subdivision 20</a>, paragraph (c).</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.07.3a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.<span class="headnote">Identification cards for seniors.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A Minnesota identification card issued to an applicant 65 years of age or over shall be of a distinguishing color and plainly marked "senior." The fee for the card issued to an applicant 65 years of age or over shall be one-half the required fee for a class D driver's license rounded down to the nearest quarter dollar. A Minnesota identification card or a Minnesota driver's license issued to a person 65 years of age or over shall be valid identification for the purpose of qualifying for reduced rates, free licenses or services provided by any board, commission, agency or institution that is wholly or partially funded by state appropriations. This subdivision does not apply to an enhanced identification card issued to an applicant age 65 or older.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.07.3b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3b.<span class="headnote">Identification card for homeless youth.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A homeless youth, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256K.45#stat.256K.45.1a">256K.45, subdivision 1a</a>, who meets the requirements of this subdivision may obtain a noncompliant identification card, notwithstanding section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.06#stat.171.06.3">171.06, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) An applicant under this subdivision must:</p>
          <p>(1) provide the applicant's full name, date of birth, and sex;</p>
          <p>(2) provide the applicant's height in feet and inches, weight in pounds, and eye color;</p>
          <p>(3) submit a certified copy of a birth certificate issued by a government bureau of vital statistics or equivalent agency in the applicant's state of birth, which must bear the raised or authorized seal of the issuing government entity; and</p>
          <p>(4) submit a statement verifying that the applicant is a homeless youth who resides in Minnesota that is signed by:</p>
          <p>(i) an employee of a human services agency receiving public funding to provide services to homeless youth, runaway youth, youth with mental illness, or youth with substance use disorders; or</p>
          <p>(ii) staff at a school who provide services to homeless youth or a school social worker.</p>
          <p>(c) For a noncompliant identification card under this subdivision:</p>
          <p>(1) the commissioner must not impose a fee, surcharge, or filing fee under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.06">171.06</a>, subdivision 2; and</p>
          <p>(2) a driver's license agent must not impose a filing fee under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.061">171.061, subdivision </a>4.</p>
          <p>(d) Minnesota Rules, parts <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/7410.0400">7410.0400</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/7410.0410">7410.0410</a>, or successor rules, do not apply for an identification card under this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.07.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Identification card expiration.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Except as otherwise provided in this subdivision, the expiration date of a Minnesota identification card is the birthday of the applicant in the fourth year following the date of issuance of the card.</p>
          <p>(b) For an applicant age 65 or older:</p>
          <p>(1) the expiration date of a Minnesota identification card is the birthday of the applicant in the eighth year following the date of issuance of the card; or</p>
          <p>(2) a noncompliant identification card is valid for the lifetime of the applicant.</p>
          <p>(c) For the purposes of paragraph (b), "Minnesota identification card" does not include an enhanced identification card issued to an applicant age 65 or older.</p>
          <p>(d) The expiration date for an Under-21 identification card is the cardholder's 21st birthday. The commissioner shall issue an identification card to a holder of an Under-21 identification card who applies for the card, pays the required fee, and presents proof of identity and age, unless the commissioner determines that the applicant is not qualified for the identification card.</p>
          <p>(e) Notwithstanding paragraphs (a) to (d), the expiration date for an identification card issued to a person with temporary lawful status is the last day of the person's legal stay in the United States, or one year after issuance if the last day of the person's legal stay is not identified.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.07.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Anatomical gift; donor document.</span></h2>
          <p>The department shall offer a donor document to each person making application for a driver's license or a Minnesota identification card who indicates a desire not to make a decision about making an anatomical gift at the time the application is made. The commissioner of public safety shall prescribe the form of the donor document and the application for a driver's license or a Minnesota identification card. The forms must be designed so that execution by the applicant of the donor document or application will make an anatomical gift under the Darlene Luther Revised Uniform Anatomical Gift Act, chapter 525A. If the donor is 18 years of age or older, the donor document or application must be signed by the donor. If the donor cannot sign, the donor document or application may be signed for the donor at the donor's direction, in the donor's presence, and in the presence of two witnesses who must sign the donor document or application in the donor's presence. If the donor is a minor, the donor document or application must be signed by the minor donor, and one of the minor donor's parents, a legal guardian, or a parent having legal custody. If the minor cannot sign, the donor document or application may not be signed for the minor. The department shall identify donors of anatomical gifts by the designation "donor" on the front side of the donor's driver's license or Minnesota identification card. The issuance of a driver's license or Minnesota identification card identifying the person as a "donor" completes the donation process and the license or identification card constitutes the final donor record. The department is not required to keep the physical record of the donor card or application after issuing the driver's license or identification card for the donation to be valid. The department shall maintain a computer record of donors. Revocation, suspension, expiration, or cancellation of the license does not invalidate the anatomical gift. The designation "donor" constitutes sufficient legal authority for the removal of all body organs or parts upon death of the donor for the purpose of transplantation. The donor designation may be removed only upon written notice to the department. Delivery of the license or Minnesota identification card during the donor's lifetime is not necessary to make the gift valid.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.07.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Medical alert identifier.</span></h2>
          <p>Upon the written request of the applicant, the department shall issue a driver's license or Minnesota identification card bearing a graphic or written medical alert identifier. The applicant must request the medical alert identifier at the time the photograph or electronically produced image is taken. No specific medical information will be contained on the driver's license or Minnesota identification card.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.07.6a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6a.<span class="headnote">Autism spectrum identifier.</span></h2>
          <p>Upon the written request of the applicant, the commissioner must issue a driver's license or Minnesota identification card bearing a graphic or written identifier for an autism spectrum disorder, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62A.3094#stat.62A.3094.1">62A.3094, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (b). The applicant must submit the written request for the identifier at the time the photograph or electronically produced image is taken. The commissioner must not include any specific medical information on the driver's license or Minnesota identification card.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.07.6b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6b.<span class="headnote">Mental health identifier.</span></h2>
          <p>Upon the written request of the applicant, the commissioner must issue a driver's license or Minnesota identification card bearing a graphic or written identifier for a mental health condition. The applicant must submit the written request for the identifier at the time the photograph or electronically produced image is taken. The commissioner must not include any specific medical information on the driver's license or Minnesota identification card.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.07.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Living Will/Health Care Directive designation.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) At the written request of the applicant and on payment of the required fee, the department shall issue, renew, or reissue a driver's license or Minnesota identification card bearing the graphic or written designation of a Living Will/Health Care Directive. The designation does not constitute delivery of a health care declaration under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/145B.05">145B.05</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) On payment of the required fee, the department shall issue a replacement or renewal license or identification card without the designation if requested by the applicant.</p>
          <p>(c) This subdivision does not impose any additional duty on a health care provider, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/145B.02#stat.145B.02.6">145B.02, subdivision 6</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/145C.01#stat.145C.01.6">145C.01, subdivision 6</a>, beyond the duties imposed in chapter 145B or 145C.</p>
          <p>(d) For the purposes of this subdivision:</p>
          <p>(1) "living will" means a declaration made under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/145B.03">145B.03</a>; and</p>
          <p>(2) "health care directive" means a durable power of attorney for health care under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/145C.02">145C.02</a>, or any other written advance health care directive of the applicant that is authorized by statute or not prohibited by law.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.07.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Certification; school bus safety laws.</span></h2>
          <p>Before a driver's license may be issued or renewed, an applicant for a driver's license or renewal shall certify by signature that the applicant is aware of the duties and responsibilities required of drivers under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.444">169.444</a> to guard against jeopardizing the safety of school children around school buses and the penalties for violating that section. A failure to make this certification does not bar a prosecution for violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.444">169.444</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.07.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Improved security.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall develop new drivers' licenses and identification cards, to be issued beginning January 1, 1994, that must be as impervious to alteration as is reasonably practicable in their design and quality of material and technology. The driver's license security laminate shall be made from materials not readily available to the general public. The design and technology employed must enable the driver's license and identification card to be subject to two or more methods of visual verification capable of clearly indicating the presence of tampering or counterfeiting. The driver's license and identification card must not be susceptible to reproduction by photocopying or simulation and must be highly resistant to data or photograph substitution and other tampering.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.07.9a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9a.<span class="headnote">Security features.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A driver's license or identification card must include reasonable security measures to: prevent forgery, alteration, reproduction, and counterfeiting; facilitate detection of fraud; prohibit the ability to superimpose a photograph or electronically produced image; and protect against unauthorized disclosure of personal information contained in the driver's license or identification card. The driver's license must include the best available anticounterfeit laminate technology.</p>
          <p>(b) An enhanced driver's license or enhanced identification card may include radio frequency identification technology that is limited to a randomly assigned number, which must be encrypted if agreed to by the United States Department of Homeland Security and does not include biometric data or any information other than the citizenship status of the license holder or cardholder. The commissioner shall ensure that the radio frequency identification technology is secure from unauthorized data access. An applicant must sign an acknowledgment of understanding of the radio frequency identification technology and its use for the sole purpose of verifying United States citizenship before being issued an enhanced driver's license or an enhanced identification card.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.07.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Agreement with other agency.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner of public safety is authorized to enter into agreements with other agencies to issue cards to clients of those agencies for use in their programs. The cards may be issued to persons who do not qualify for a Minnesota driver's license or do not provide evidence of name and identity as required by rule for a Minnesota identification card. Persons issued cards under this subdivision will meet the identification verification requirements of the contracting agency.</p>
          <p>(b) The interagency agreement may include provisions for the payment of the county fee provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.061#stat.171.061.4">171.061, subdivision 4</a>, and the actual cost to manufacture the card.</p>
          <p>(c) Cards issued under this subdivision are not Minnesota identification cards for the purposes defined in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/48.512">48.512</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/201.061">201.061</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/201.161">201.161</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/340A.503">340A.503</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/604.113">604.113</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.07.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Standby or temporary custodian.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Upon the written request of the applicant and upon payment of an additional fee of $4.25, the department shall issue a driver's license or Minnesota identification card bearing a symbol or other appropriate identifier indicating that the license holder has appointed an individual to serve as a standby or temporary custodian under chapter 257B.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The request must be accompanied by a copy of the designation executed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/257B.04">257B.04</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The department shall maintain a computerized records system of all individuals listed as standby or temporary custodians by driver's license and identification card applicants. This data must be released to appropriate law enforcement agencies under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.69">13.69</a>. Upon a parent's request and payment of a fee of $4.25, the department shall revise its list of standby or temporary custodians to reflect a change in the appointment.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) At the request of the license or cardholder, the department shall cancel the standby or temporary custodian indication without additional charge. However, this paragraph does not prohibit a fee that may be applicable for a duplicate or replacement license or card, renewal of a license, or other service applicable to a driver's license or identification card.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) Notwithstanding sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.08#stat.13.08.1">13.08, subdivision 1</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.69">13.69</a>, the department and department employees are conclusively presumed to be acting in good faith when employees rely on statements made, in person or by telephone, by persons purporting to be law enforcement and subsequently release information described in paragraph (b). When acting in good faith, the department and department personnel are immune from civil liability and not subject to suit for damages resulting from the release of this information.</p>
          <p class="in">(f) The department and its employees:</p>
