      <p><b>NOTE: </b>(a) Subdivision 1a, as added by Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 19, was effective October 1, 2018. Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 19, the effective date.</p>
      <p>(b) Subdivisions 3c, 7b, and 7c, as added by Laws 2017, chapter 76, sections 20 to 22, were effective May 19, 2017, and apply to driver's licenses and Minnesota identification cards applied for and issued on or after October 1, 2018. Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 29.</p>
      <p>(c) If the Federal REAL ID Act, Public Law 109-13, Division B, is repealed, or if the definition of "official purpose" in Code of Federal Regulations, title 6, part 37, is amended, subdivisions 1a, 3c, 7b, and 7c, as added by Laws 2017, chapter 76, sections 19 to 22, are repealed on June 30 of the following year. Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 27.</p>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.1205">
        <h1 class="shn">171.1205 RESTRICTIONS ON USE.</h1>
        <p>A state agency or a political subdivision must not use the possession of a noncompliant driver's license or identification card as (1) evidence of a person's citizenship or lawful presence in the United States, or (2) the primary basis for investigation, detention, or arrest.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.1205.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/13/#laws.1.10.0">2023 c 13 art 1 s 10</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.121">
        <h1 class="shn">171.121 USE OF ANOTHER'S RESIDENCE ADDRESS.</h1>
        <p>A person may notify the commissioner in writing to the effect that the person (1) is the owner of a residence, and (2) does not consent to have that residence address identified on any driver's license or identification card or driving record of the department as the residence address or permanent mailing address of any person named in the notice. The notice may not name a spouse of the notifying person. Upon receiving the notice the commissioner shall not issue any license or identification card under this chapter, or accept an application for a license or identification card under this chapter, that lists the residence address identified in the notice as the residence address or permanent mailing address of any person named in the notice.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.121.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/356/#laws.0.1.0">1996 c 356 s 1</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.122">
        <h1 class="shn">171.122 ENHANCED DRIVERS' LICENSES AND IDENTIFICATION CARDS; LIMITATION.</h1>
        <p>Provisions of this chapter that relate expressly to enhanced drivers' licenses or enhanced identification cards apply only to drivers' licenses within the meaning of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.01#stat.171.01.31a">171.01, subdivision 31a</a>, and identification cards within the meaning of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.01">171.01</a>, subdivision 31b, unless otherwise expressly provided in statute.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.122.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/316/#laws.0.15.0">2010 c 316 s 15,18</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.13">
        <h1 class="shn">171.13 EXAMINATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.13.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Examination subjects and locations; provisions for color blindness, disabled veterans.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Except as otherwise provided in this section, the commissioner must examine each applicant for a driver's license by such agency as the commissioner directs. This examination must include:</p>
          <p>(1) a test of the applicant's eyesight, provided that this requirement is met by submission of a vision examination certificate under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.06#stat.171.06.7">171.06, subdivision 7</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) a test of the applicant's ability to read and understand highway signs regulating, warning, and directing traffic;</p>
          <p>(3) a test of the applicant's knowledge of (i) traffic laws; (ii) the effects of alcohol and drugs on a driver's ability to operate a motor vehicle safely and legally, and of the legal penalties and financial consequences resulting from violations of laws prohibiting the operation of a motor vehicle while under the influence of alcohol or drugs; (iii) railroad grade crossing safety; (iv) slow-moving vehicle safety; (v) laws relating to pupil transportation safety, including the significance of school bus lights, signals, stop arm, and passing a school bus; (vi) traffic laws related to bicycles; and (vii) the circumstances and dangers of carbon monoxide poisoning;</p>
          <p>(4) an actual demonstration of ability to exercise ordinary and reasonable control in the operation of a motor vehicle; and</p>
          <p>(5) other physical and mental examinations as the commissioner finds necessary to determine the applicant's fitness to operate a motor vehicle safely upon the highways.</p>
          <p>(b) Notwithstanding paragraph (a), the commissioner must not deny an application for a driver's license based on the exclusive grounds that the applicant's eyesight is deficient in color perception or that the applicant has been diagnosed with diabetes mellitus. War veterans operating motor vehicles especially equipped for disabled persons, if otherwise entitled to a license, must be granted such license.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner must ensure that an applicant may take an exam either in the county where the applicant resides or in an adjacent county at a reasonably convenient location. The schedule for each exam station must be posted on the department's website.</p>
          <p>(d) The commissioner shall ensure that an applicant is able to obtain an appointment for an examination to demonstrate ability under paragraph (a), clause (4), within 14 days of the applicant's request if, under the applicable statutes and rules of the commissioner, the applicant is eligible to take the examination.</p>
          <p>(e) The commissioner must provide real-time information on the department's website about the availability and location of exam appointments. The website must show the next available exam dates and times for each exam station. The website must also provide an option for a person to enter an address to see the date and time of the next available exam at each exam station sorted by distance from the address provided.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.13.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Waiver when license issued by another jurisdiction.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If the commissioner determines that an applicant for a driver's license is 21 years of age or older and possesses a valid driver's license issued by another state or jurisdiction that requires a comparable examination to obtain a driver's license, the commissioner must waive the requirements that the applicant pass a knowledge examination and demonstrate ability to exercise ordinary and reasonable control in the operation of a motor vehicle.</p>
          <p>(b) If the commissioner determines that an applicant for a motorcycle endorsement is 21 years of age or older and possesses a valid driver's license with a motorcycle endorsement issued by another state or jurisdiction that requires a comparable examination to obtain an endorsement, the commissioner must waive the requirements with respect to the endorsement that the applicant pass a knowledge examination and demonstrate the ability to exercise ordinary and reasonable control in the operation of a motor vehicle.</p>
          <p>(c) For purposes of this subdivision, "jurisdiction" includes, but is not limited to, both the active and reserve components of any branch or unit of the United States armed forces, and "valid driver's license" includes any driver's license that is recognized by that branch or unit as currently being valid, or as having been valid at the time of the applicant's separation or discharge from the military within a period of time deemed reasonable and fair by the commissioner, up to and including one year past the date of the applicant's separation or discharge.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.13.1b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1b.</h2>
          <p>MS 2010 [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.0705#stat.171.0705.1">171.0705, subdivision 1</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.13.1c">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1c.</h2>
          <p>MS 2010 [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.0705">171.0705, subd 6</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.13.1d">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1d.</h2>
          <p>MS 2010 [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.0705">171.0705, subd 9</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.13.1e">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1e.<span class="headnote">Slow-moving vehicle.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall include, in each examination under subdivision 1, an examination of the applicant's knowledge of highway safety with respect to approaching, following, and passing slow-moving vehicles and the significance of the slow-moving vehicle emblem.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.13.1f">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1f.</h2>
          <p>MS 2010 [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.0705">171.0705, subd 2</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.13.1g">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1g.</h2>
          <p>MS 2010 [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.0705">171.0705, subd 4</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.13.1h">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1h.</h2>
          <p>MS 2010 [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.0705">171.0705, subd 8</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.13.1i">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1i.</h2>
          <p>MS 2010 [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.0705">171.0705, subd 10</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.13.1j">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1j.</h2>
          <p>MS 2010 [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.0705">171.0705, subd 7</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.13.1k">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1k.</h2>
          <p>MS 2010 [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.0705">171.0705, subd 5</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.13.1l">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1l.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.0705">171.0705, subd 3</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.13.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Examination upon renewal.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The department shall issue a driver's license upon renewal:</p>
          <p>(1) when the applicant has passed an examination consisting of a screening of the applicant's eyesight since the last previous license renewal or issuance;</p>
          <p>(2) if applicable, when an applicant has passed a written examination since the last previous license renewal or issuance and after receiving a warning letter or attending a preliminary hearing as a habitual violator, within the meaning of rules adopted by the commissioner; and</p>
          <p>(3) if applicable, when an applicant has passed a road examination since the last previous license renewal or issuance and after having had driving privileges suspended as a habitual violator, within the meaning of rules adopted by the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(b) A screening of eyesight required by this subdivision does not constitute the practice of optometry as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148.56">148.56</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner may adopt rules to administer this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.13.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Examination of licensed driver.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may require an examination by such agency as the commissioner directs of any licensed driver, to determine incompetency, physical or mental disability or disease, or any other condition which might affect the driver from exercising reasonable and ordinary control over a motor vehicle, but no examination shall be required only for the reason that any licensed driver has attained a certain age. If as a result of the examination the commissioner believes that the driver is an unsafe person to operate a motor vehicle upon the public highways, the commissioner may cancel the driver's license of the person. The commissioner shall give the person written notice of the cancellation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.13.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Cancellation for failure to submit to examination.</span></h2>
          <p>If a licensee does not submit to any examination required under the provisions of subdivision 3, the commissioner may cancel the driver's license of the licensee. If such license is canceled, the licensee shall immediately surrender to the department all driver's license certificates in possession.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.13.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Examination fee for vehicle endorsement.</span></h2>
          <p>Any person applying to secure a school bus, tank vehicle, passenger, double-trailer or triple-trailer, or hazardous materials vehicle endorsement on the person's driver's license shall pay a $2.50 examination fee at the place of application.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.13.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Motorcycle endorsement examination fee.</span></h2>
          <p>A person applying for an initial motorcycle endorsement on a driver's license must pay at the place of examination a $2.50 examination fee, an endorsement fee as prescribed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.06#stat.171.06.2a">171.06, subdivision 2a</a>, and the appropriate driver's license fee as prescribed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.06#stat.171.06.2">171.06, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.13.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote"> Examination fees.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A fee of $10 must be paid by an individual to take a third and any subsequent knowledge test administered by the department if the individual has failed two previous consecutive knowledge tests on the subject.</p>
          <p>(b) A fee of $20 must be paid by an individual to take a third and any subsequent skills or road test administered by the department if the individual has previously failed two consecutive skill or road tests in a specified class of motor vehicle.</p>
          <p>(c) A fee of $20 must be paid by an individual who fails to appear for a scheduled skills or road test or who cancels a skills or road test within 24 hours of the appointment time.</p>
          <p>(d) All fees received under this subdivision must be paid into the state treasury and credited to the driver and vehicle services operating account under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/299A.705">299A.705</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.13.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Test scheduling.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner must not schedule or reserve recurring time with a public, private, or commercial driver education program for purposes of administering skills or road tests to a class D or commercial driver's license applicant.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.13.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Online driver's license knowledge testing authorization.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner must implement online knowledge testing as provided in this subdivision. The commissioner must not charge a fee to a driver education program or an authorized entity for access to the online knowledge testing system or for administering the online knowledge test.</p>
          <p>(b) Upon written request from a driver education program licensed by the department, the commissioner must grant access to the department's web-based knowledge testing system to the driver education program. Once granted access to the online knowledge testing system, a driver education program may administer the online knowledge test to a student of the program.</p>
          <p>(c) An entity other than a driver education program may apply to the commissioner for authority to administer online knowledge tests. The commissioner may approve or disapprove an application for administering the online knowledge tests under this paragraph. Upon approving an application of an entity, the commissioner must grant access to the department's web-based knowledge testing system to that authorized entity. Once granted access to the online knowledge testing system, the authorized entity may administer the online knowledge test.</p>
          <p>(d) A driver education program or authorized entity:</p>
          <p>(1) must provide all computers and equipment for persons that take the online knowledge test;</p>
          <p>(2) must provide appropriate proctors to monitor persons taking the online knowledge test; and</p>
          <p>(3) may charge a fee of no more than $10 for administering the online knowledge test.</p>
          <p>(e) For purposes of paragraph (d), clause (2), a proctor must be:</p>
          <p>(1) an employee of the driver education program, authorized entity, or a state or local government;</p>
          <p>(2) a driver's license agent; or</p>
          <p>(3) a classroom teacher, school administrator, or paraprofessional at a public or private school, excluding a home school.</p>
          <p class="bl">The proctor must be physically present at the location where the test is being administered. A proctor must not be a relative of the person taking the test. For purposes of this paragraph, a relative is a spouse, fiancee, fiance, grandparent, parent, child, sibling, or legal guardian, including adoptive, half, step, and in-law relationships.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.13.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Written examination plain language requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For purposes of this subdivision, the following terms have the meanings given:</p>
          <p>(1) "committee" means the Department of Public Safety, Division of Driver and Vehicle Services Test Maintenance Committee responsible for monitoring, reviewing, and editing the written portion of the driver's knowledge examination on behalf of the Division of Driver and Vehicle Services;</p>
          <p>(2) "plain language standards" means the requirements specified in paragraph (b) and the requirements of the Plain Writing Act, Public Law 111-274, and governor's Executive Order 14-07; and</p>
          <p>(3) "written examination" means the written portion of the driver's knowledge examination in English and administered under this section.</p>
          <p>(b) By February 1, 2025, the commissioner must implement plain language standards and create a new written portion of the knowledge examination to ensure the written examination is a fair assessment of the state's traffic and driving laws. The new written portion of the knowledge examination, to the extent practicable, must be organized to serve the reader's needs and written using clear, simplified language with the following grammatical standards:</p>
          <p>(1) "you" and other pronouns address the test-taker directly;</p>
          <p>(2) utilize the active voice;</p>
          <p>(3) use short, simple sentences and straightforward questions;</p>
          <p>(4) avoid complex and compound sentence structures;</p>
          <p>(5) utilize commonly used and familiar words;</p>
          <p>(6) avoid turning verbs into nouns through nominalization;</p>
          <p>(7) minimize the use of abbreviations;</p>
          <p>(8) place words carefully to avoid large gaps between the subject, verb, and object in a sentence;</p>
          <p>(9) avoid confusing terms such as "either, or" and "neither, nor";</p>
          <p>(10) utilize simple verb tenses, including the simple present verb tense where practicable, to explain confusing or complex concepts;</p>
          <p>(11) omit double negatives and terms like "except for," "unless," and "indicated otherwise";</p>
          <p>(12) avoid hidden terms and use concrete, familiar words to describe confusing or complex concepts or items;</p>
          <p>(13) omit excess and unnecessary words;</p>
          <p>(14) use the word "must" to express requirements;</p>
          <p>(15) put exceptions at the end of a question;</p>
          <p>(16) place modifying terms in correct locations; and</p>
          <p>(17) any other recommended strategies and techniques designed to offer clear communication to test-takers.</p>
          <p>(c) Any revisions or additions to the subjects tested on a knowledge examination must be written utilizing the plain language standards specified in paragraph (b). Revisions made to the written examination must assess whether the applicant understands the traffic laws of Minnesota and test knowledge of the requirements specified in subdivision 1, paragraph (a), clauses (2) and (3).</p>
          <p>(d) The committee must convene its first meeting by August 1, 2024. Before publication of the new written examination under plain language requirements, the committee must meet at least four times before January 1, 2025. The commissioner of public safety may enter into an agreement with a third party to propose, draft, and revise the written examination under the requirements of this subdivision.</p>
          <p>(e) The commissioner may adopt plain language standards into other Division of Driver and Vehicle Services materials, including the driver's manual under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.0705">171.0705</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.13.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Exam scheduling reporting.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For purposes of this subdivision, the following terms have the meanings given:</p>
          <p>(1) "delay" or "delayed" means a road test examination under this section between 15 and 30 days after an eligible applicant's request for a road test; and</p>
          <p>(2) "reporting period" means a calendar year or a fiscal year as identified for each report under paragraph (b).</p>
          <p>(b) By March 1 and September 1 of each year, the commissioner must submit a report on road test examination scheduling to the chairs and ranking minority members of the legislative committees with jurisdiction over transportation policy and finance. The report due by September 1 must include information for the most recently ended fiscal year. The report due by March 1 must include information for the most recently ended calendar year.</p>
          <p>(c) At a minimum, the report must:</p>
          <p>(1) identify each performance measure or metric established by the commissioner related to scheduling availability and passage of road tests;</p>
          <p>(2) for the reporting period, identify the results for the performance measures or metrics under clause (1); and</p>
          <p>(3) for the reporting period, identify the rate at which applicants are able to obtain an appointment for a road test in the time period provided under subdivision 1, paragraph (d), compared to the number of individuals who experienced a delay in booking a road test appointment.</p>
          <p>(d) For the reporting period, if a goal is not met in a performance measure or metric under paragraph (c), clause (1), or if the requirements specified under subdivision 1, paragraph (d), are not fully met, the report must also:</p>
          <p>(1) include the number of administered road tests for Class D and commercial drivers' licenses per month for the previous five fiscal years;</p>
          <p>(2) provide information about factors that impact road test examination appointment availability, including information on staffing and the use of overtime at exam stations, budgetary resources, the number of potential applicants seeking a road test, and any other analysis based on the department's experience necessary to identify and project what may cause delays in the next five fiscal years;</p>
          <p>(3) analyze the rate and frequency of which an applicant was administered a road test for either a Class D or commercial driver's license at an exam station outside the applicant's county or exam station region to identify the rate at which an applicant must travel to obtain a road test appointment;</p>
          <p>(4) for the analysis required in clause (3), provide a breakout by county and exam station region; and</p>
          <p>(5) conduct the analysis required in clauses (3) and (4) for retakes of Class D or commercial driver's license road tests.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <p><b>NOTE: </b>The amendments to subdivision 9 by Laws 2024, chapter 127, article 3, section 70, are effective August 1, 2025. Laws 2024, chapter 127, article 3, section 70, the effective date.</p>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.131">
        <h1 class="shn">171.131 PHYSICIAN REPORTING TO COMMISSIONER.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.131.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Voluntary reporting by physicians.</span></h2>
          <p>Any physician who diagnoses a physical or mental condition which in the physician's judgment will significantly impair the person's ability to operate safely a motor vehicle may voluntarily report the person's name and other information relevant to the condition to the commissioner. The commissioner, upon receiving the report, shall require the person so reported to be examined as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.13#stat.171.13.3">171.13, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.131.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Physicians immune from liability.</span></h2>
          <p>Any physician reporting in good faith and exercising due care shall have immunity from any liability, civil or criminal, that otherwise might result by reason of actions pursuant to this section. No cause of action may be brought against any physician for not making a report pursuant to this section.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.131.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/479/#laws.0.1.0">1982 c 479 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/216/#laws.1.32.0">1983 c 216 art 1 s 32</a>; 1986 c 444</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.14">
        <h1 class="shn">171.14 CANCELLATION.</h1>
        <p class="in">(a) The commissioner may cancel any driver's license upon determination that (1) the licensee was not entitled to the issuance of the license, (2) the licensee failed to give the required or correct information in the application, (3) the licensee committed any fraud or deceit in making the application, or (4) the person, at the time of the cancellation, would not have been entitled to receive a license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.04">171.04</a>.</p>
        <p class="in">(b) The commissioner shall cancel the driver's license of a person described in paragraph (a), clause (3), for 60 days or until the required or correct information has been provided, whichever is longer.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.14.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/13">2720-144k</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/401/#laws.0.14.0">1939 c 401 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/0/506/#laws.0.1.0">1959 c 506 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/307/#laws.0.26.0">1989 c 307 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/287/#laws.1.65.0">2008 c 287 art 1 s 65</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.15">
        <h1 class="shn">171.15 NONRESIDENT'S DRIVING PRIVILEGE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.15.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Suspension or revocation.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The privilege of driving a motor vehicle on the highways of this state given to a nonresident hereunder shall be subject to the suspension or revocation by the commissioner in like manner and for like cause as a driver's license issued hereunder may be suspended or revoked.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner is further authorized, upon receiving a record of conviction in this state of a nonresident driver of a motor vehicle of any offense under the motor vehicle laws of this state, to forward a certified copy of such record to the motor vehicle administrator in the state wherein the person so convicted is a resident.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.15.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/454/#laws.0.37.0">1986 c 454 s 37</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.15.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/13">2720-145</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/401/#laws.0.15.0">1939 c 401 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/783/#laws.0.6.0">1978 c 783 s 6</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.16">
        <h1 class="shn">171.16 COURT MAY RECOMMEND SUSPENSION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.16.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Court to report to commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p>Every court having jurisdiction over offenses committed under any law of this state or ordinance of a political subdivision regulating the operation of motor vehicles, shall forward to the department, within ten days, a record of the conviction of any person in the court for a violation of any laws or ordinances, except parking violations and defective vehicle equipment or vehicle size or weight violations.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.16.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Suspension on conviction.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The court may recommend the suspension of the driver's license of the person so convicted, and, subject to the limitations in this section, the commissioner shall suspend such license as recommended by the court, without a hearing as provided herein.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner is prohibited from suspending a person's driver's license if the person was convicted only under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.24#stat.171.24.1">171.24, subdivision 1</a> or 2.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.16.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote"> Failure to pay fine.</span></h2>
          <p> The commissioner is prohibited from suspending a person's driver's license based solely on the fact that a person:</p>
          <p>(1) has been convicted of:</p>
          <p>(i) violating a law of this state or an ordinance of a political subdivision which regulates the operation or parking of motor vehicles;</p>
          <p>(ii) a violation under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.06#stat.169.06.10">169.06, subdivision 10</a>; or</p>
          <p>(iii) a violation under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.14#stat.169.14.13">169.14, subdivision 13</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) has been sentenced to the payment of a fine or had a surcharge levied against that person, or sentenced to a fine upon which a surcharge was levied; and</p>
          <p>(3) has refused or failed to comply with that sentence or to pay the surcharge.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.16.3a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.<span class="headnote">Failure to appear.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Upon receiving a report from the court, or from the driver licensing authority of a state, district, territory, or possession of the United States or a province of a foreign country which has an agreement in effect with this state pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.91">169.91</a>, that a resident of this state or a person licensed as a driver in this state did not appear in court in compliance with the terms of a citation, the commissioner of public safety shall notify the driver that the driver's license will be suspended unless the commissioner receives notice within 30 days that the driver has appeared in the appropriate court. If the commissioner does not receive notice of the appearance in the appropriate court or payment of the fine within 30 days of the date of the commissioner's notice to the driver, the commissioner may suspend the driver's license, subject to the notice requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.18#stat.171.18.2">171.18, subdivision 2</a>. Notwithstanding the requirements in this subdivision, the commissioner is prohibited from suspending the driver's license of a person based solely on the fact that the person did not appear in court (1) in compliance with the terms of a citation for a petty misdemeanor, or (2) for a violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.24#stat.171.24.1">171.24, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) The order of suspension shall indicate the reason for the order and shall notify the driver that the driver's license remains suspended until the driver has furnished evidence, satisfactory to the commissioner, of compliance with any order entered by the court.</p>
          <p>(c) Suspension shall be ordered under this subdivision only when the report clearly identifies the person arrested; describes the violation, specifying the section of the traffic law, ordinance or rule violated; indicates the location and date of the offense; and describes the vehicle involved and its registration number.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.16.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/698/#laws.0.8.0">1973 c 698 s 8</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.16.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Juvenile court.</span></h2>
          <p>When any judge of a juvenile court, or any of its duly authorized agents, shall determine formally or informally that any person under the age of 18 years has violated any of the provisions of any law of this state, or ordinances of political subdivisions thereof, regulating the operation of motor vehicles on streets and highways, except parking violations, and except traffic offenses involving a violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.20">169A.20</a> that must be reported under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.17">171.17</a>, the judge, or duly authorized agent, shall immediately report the determination to the department and may recommend the suspension of the person's license, and the commissioner is hereby authorized to suspend the license, without a hearing.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.16.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Notice of suspension.</span></h2>
          <p>A suspension under this section is subject to the notice requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.18#stat.171.18.2">171.18, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.16.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Suspension under reciprocal agreement.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For purposes of this subdivision:</p>
          <p>(1) "issuing jurisdiction" means a state, district, territory, or possession of the United States or a province of a foreign country which has an agreement in effect with this state pursuant to the nonresident violator compact; and</p>
          <p>(2) "traffic violation" means a violation of a traffic regulation relating to the operation of a motor vehicle and excludes a parking, vehicle equipment, or vehicle weight limit violation.</p>
          <p>(b) Notwithstanding subdivisions 3 and 3a, the commissioner may suspend the driver's license of a person licensed in this state upon receiving a report from an issuing jurisdiction that the person:</p>
          <p>(1) did not appear in court in compliance with the terms of a citation for a traffic violation that, if committed in this state, is a petty misdemeanor or a violation under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.24">171.24</a>, subdivision 1; or</p>
          <p>(2) is convicted of a traffic violation, is subject to a fine or surcharge, and has failed to pay the fine or surcharge.</p>
          <p>(c) A suspension is authorized under this subdivision only as necessary to conform with the requirements of the nonresident violator compact.</p>
          <p>(d) A suspension under this subdivision is subject to the notice requirements under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.18#stat.171.18.2">171.18, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.16.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/13">2720-145a</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/401/#laws.0.16.0">1939 c 401 s 16</a>, except par (c); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/946/#laws.0.2.0">1969 c 946 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/1/27/#laws.0.24.0">Ex1971 c 27 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/421/#laws.0.4.0">1973 c 421 s 4</a>,5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/341/#laws.0.3.0">1981 c 341 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/359/#laws.0.18.0">1983 c 359 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/622/#laws.0.16.0">1984 c 622 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/123/#laws.0.2.0">1987 c 123 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/307/#laws.0.27.0">1989 c 307 s 27</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/346/#laws.0.5.0">1996 c 346 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/367/#laws.8.3.0">1998 c 367 art 8 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/388/#laws.0.20.0">1998 c 388 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/478/#laws.2.7.0">2000 c 478 art 2 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/5/#laws.4.67.0">1Sp2021 c 5 art 4 s 67</a>,77-79,149; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.3.71.0">2024 c 127 art 3 s 71</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.161">
        <h1 class="shn">171.161 COMMERCIAL DRIVER'S LICENSE; FEDERAL CONFORMITY.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.161.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Conformity with federal law.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of public safety shall ensure the programs and policies related to commercial drivers' licensure and the operation of commercial motor vehicles in Minnesota conform with the requirements of Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, part 383.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.161.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Conflicts.</span></h2>
          <p>To the extent a requirement of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.162">171.162</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.169">171.169</a>, or any other state or local law, conflicts with a provision of Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, part 383, the federal provision prevails.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.161.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/312/#laws.11.22.0">2014 c 312 art 11 s 22</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.162">
        <h1 class="shn">171.162 COMMERCIAL DRIVER'S LICENSE, REQUIRED RECORDS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.162.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Driving record.</span></h2>
          <p>As required by Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, section 383.73, before issuing a class A, class B, or class C commercial driver's license, the department shall request the applicant's complete driving record from all states where the applicant was previously licensed over the last ten years to operate any type of motor vehicle.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.162.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Applicant self-certification.</span></h2>
          <p>As required by Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, section 383.71, an applicant for a class A, class B, or class C commercial driver's license shall certify, on a form prescribed by the department, whether:</p>
          <p>(1) the applicant operates or expects to operate a class A, class B, or class C motor vehicle in interstate or foreign commerce or is otherwise subject to the medical examination requirements of Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, part 391;</p>
          <p>(2) the applicant operates or expects to operate a class A, class B, or class C motor vehicle in interstate or foreign commerce but is exempt from the medical examination requirements of Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, part 391;</p>
          <p>(3) the applicant operates or expects to operate a class A, class B, or class C motor vehicle entirely in intrastate commerce, is not subject to the medical examination requirements of Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, part 391, but is subject to the medical examination requirements of chapter 221; or</p>
          <p>(4) the applicant operates or expects to operate a class A, class B, or class C motor vehicle entirely in intrastate commerce, is not subject to the medical examination requirements of Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, part 391, and is exempt from the medical examination requirements of chapter 221.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.162.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Medical examiner's certificate; applicants.</span></h2>
          <p>An applicant for a class A, class B, or class C commercial driver's license who is subject to the medical examination requirements of chapter 221 or Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, part 391, shall submit a valid medical examiner's certificate or a current medical waiver to the department.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.162.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Medical examiner's certificate; timely submission.</span></h2>
          <p>The holder of a class A, class B, or class C commercial driver's license who is subject to the medical examination requirements of chapter 221 or Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, part 391, shall submit a valid medical examiner's certificate or a current medical waiver to the department prior to the expiration of the certificate or waiver previously submitted by the person.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.162.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Medical examiner's certificate; existing licensees.</span></h2>
          <p>By November 30, 2013, a person who holds a class A, class B, or class C commercial driver's license as of August 1, 2013, shall submit to the department the self-certification described in subdivision 2. If the licensee certifies that the licensee is subject to the medical examination requirements of chapter 221 or Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, part 391, the licensee shall also submit to the department a valid medical examiner's certificate or a current medical waiver. If the licensee fails to submit the self-certification or, if required, a valid medical examiner's certificate or current medical waiver, the department shall follow the notice and license downgrade procedures of subdivision 6.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.162.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Downgrade of license; notice.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If a licensee has previously certified to the department that the licensee is subject to the medical examination requirements of chapter 221 or Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, part 391, the department shall send written notice if the licensee's medical examiner's certificate or medical waiver has expired or been removed, rescinded, or revoked. The notice must state that the commercial driver's license will be downgraded to a class D driver's license unless, within 30 days of the date of the notice, the licensee submits to the department:</p>
          <p>(1) a valid medical examiner's certificate or a current medical waiver; or</p>
          <p>(2) a new self-certification, as described in subdivision 2, in which the licensee certifies that the licensee is subject to a different set of medical examination requirements or exemptions than indicated on the licensee's previous self-certification, accompanied by a valid medical examiner's certificate or a current medical waiver, if applicable.</p>
          <p>(b) The department shall send the notice described in paragraph (a):</p>
          <p>(1) by electronic mail to the electronic mail address provided by the licensee to the department for that purpose;</p>
          <p>(2) by facsimile transmission to the telephone number provided by the licensee to the department for that purpose; or</p>
          <p>(3) by mail, addressed to the licensee's last known address with postage prepaid.</p>
          <p>(c) As applied to licensees who have previously certified they operate or expect to operate motor vehicles in interstate or foreign commerce, the notice described in paragraph (a) must conform to the requirements of Code of Regulations, title 49, section 383.73, paragraph (j).</p>
          <p>(d) If the licensee does not submit a new self-certification but submits a valid medical examiner's certificate or a current medical waiver within the 30-day period described in paragraph (a), the commissioner shall take no action. If the licensee submits a new self-certification as described in paragraph (a), along with a valid medical examiner's certificate or a current medical waiver, as applicable, the commissioner shall restrict the licensee's operating privileges to the extent required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.09#stat.171.09.1">171.09, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (c) or (d). If the licensee does not submit a new self-certification, a valid medical examiner's certificate, or a current medical waiver within the 30-day period described in paragraph (a), the commissioner shall remove the licensee's privilege to operate a class A, class B, or class C motor vehicle.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.162.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Rulemaking authority.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall adopt rules to implement and administer this section and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.09#stat.171.09.1">171.09, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.162.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/6/#laws.3.71.0">1Sp2005 c 6 art 3 s 71</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/242/#laws.0.10.0">2010 c 242 s 10</a>,11</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.163">
        <h1 class="shn">171.163 COMMERCIAL DRIVER'S LICENSE RECORD KEEPING.</h1>
        <p>An agency, court, or public official in Minnesota shall not mask, defer imposition of judgment for, or allow an individual to enter into a diversion program that would prevent a conviction for a violation of a state or local traffic control law, except a parking violation, from appearing on the driving record of a holder of a commercial driver's license, when the violation is committed in any type of motor vehicle, or on the driving record of an individual who committed the violation in a commercial motor vehicle.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.163.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/36/#laws.3.11.0">2009 c 36 art 3 s 11</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.165">
        <h1 class="shn">171.165 COMMERCIAL DRIVER'S LICENSE, DISQUALIFICATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.165.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Federal standards.</span></h2>
          <p>Subject to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.166">171.166</a>, the commissioner shall disqualify a person from operating commercial motor vehicles in accordance with the driver disqualifications and penalties in Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, part 383, subpart D and Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, section 384.219.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.165.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Implied consent revocation.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The commissioner shall disqualify a person from operating commercial motor vehicles for a revocation under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.52">169A.52</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.177">171.177</a> or a statute or ordinance from another state or jurisdiction in conformity with it, for a period that is equivalent in duration under the driver disqualifications and penalties in Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, part 383, subpart D, that pertain to a conviction of being under the influence of alcohol or refusal to be tested.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.165.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/6/#laws.3.108.0">1Sp2005 c 6 art 3 s 108</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.165.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/6/#laws.3.108.0">1Sp2005 c 6 art 3 s 108</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.165.4a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/6/#laws.3.108.0">1Sp2005 c 6 art 3 s 108</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.165.4b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4b.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/6/#laws.3.108.0">1Sp2005 c 6 art 3 s 108</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.165.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Rules.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall adopt rules to administer this section. The rules must include procedures for issuing class D licenses to persons who have been disqualified from operating commercial motor vehicles but whose drivers' licenses have not otherwise been revoked, suspended, canceled, or denied.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.165.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Exemptions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A disqualification shall not be imposed under this section on a recreational vehicle operator, farmer, or authorized emergency vehicle operator operating a commercial motor vehicle within the scope of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.02#stat.171.02.2">171.02, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (c).</p>
          <p>(b) A conviction for a violation that occurred before August 1, 2005, while operating a vehicle that is not a commercial motor vehicle shall not be counted as a first or subsequent violation for purposes of determining the period for which a driver must be disqualified under this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.165.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Scope.</span></h2>
          <p>This section applies to offenses committed, and revocations imposed for incidents occurring, on or after January 1, 1990.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.165.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/307/#laws.0.28.0">1989 c 307 s 28</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/333/#laws.0.29.0">1991 c 333 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/501/#laws.0.7.0">1994 c 501 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/275/#laws.0.10.0">1996 c 275 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/93/#laws.0.1.0">1999 c 93 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/478/#laws.2.7.0">2000 c 478 art 2 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/371/#laws.1.60.0">2002 c 371 art 1 s 60</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/6/#laws.3.72.0">1Sp2005 c 6 art 3 s 72</a>-74; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/350/#laws.1.61.0">2008 c 350 art 1 s 61</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/83/#laws.3.18.0">2017 c 83 art 3 s 18</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.166">
        <h1 class="shn">171.166 REVIEW OF DISQUALIFICATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.166.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Review of convictions.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall review court records of convictions subject to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.165">171.165</a>, other than a violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.20">169A.20</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.31">169A.31</a>, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.50">169A.50</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.53">169A.53</a>, or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.177">171.177</a>, if the commissioner has reasonable cause to believe the information is pertinent to the disqualification of an individual.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.166.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Notification to commercial driver's license holder or applicant.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall notify the applicant or license holder and the individual who is the subject of a review, in writing, of the results of the review. The commissioner shall notify the individual reviewed if the information contained in the review could cause license disqualification.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.166.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Reconsideration of license disqualification.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Within 30 days after receiving notice of possible disqualification under subdivision 2, the individual who is the subject of the review may request reconsideration of the notice of possible disqualification. The individual must submit the request for reconsideration to the commissioner in writing. The individual must present information to show that the information the commissioner relied upon is incorrect or not applicable for disqualification of the individual being reviewed.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner may set aside the disqualification if the commissioner finds that the information the commissioner relied upon is incorrect or not applicable for disqualification of the individual being reviewed.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner shall notify the applicant or license holder in writing of the reconsideration decision within 15 working days after receiving the request for reconsideration. The disqualification takes effect 20 days after the person receives the reconsideration decision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.166.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/56/#laws.0.2.0">1995 c 56 s 2</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.166.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/307/#laws.0.29.0">1989 c 307 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/56/#laws.0.1.0">1995 c 56 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/478/#laws.2.7.0">2000 c 478 art 2 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/83/#laws.3.18.0">2017 c 83 art 3 s 18</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.167">
        <h1 class="shn">171.167 NOTICE TO COMMERCIAL DRIVER'S LICENSE INFORMATION SYSTEM.</h1>
        <p>The department shall participate fully in the commercial driver's license information system established under the Commercial Motor Vehicle Safety Act of 1986 at United States Code, title 49, section 31309.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.167.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/6/#laws.3.75.0">1Sp2005 c 6 art 3 s 75</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.168">
        <h1 class="shn">171.168 NOTICE OF VIOLATION BY COMMERCIAL DRIVER.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.168.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Department notice.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Each person who operates a commercial motor vehicle, who has a commercial driver's license issued by this state, and who is convicted of a criminal offense; of a serious traffic violation, as defined in Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, part 383; or of violating any other state or local law relating to motor vehicle traffic control, other than a parking violation, in any type of motor vehicle in another state or jurisdiction, shall notify the department's Division of Driver and Vehicle Services of the conviction. The person shall notify the division within 30 days after the date that the person was convicted.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.168.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Employer notice.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Each person who operates a commercial motor vehicle, who has a commercial driver's license issued by this state, and who is convicted of violating, in any type of motor vehicle, a Minnesota state or local law relating to motor vehicle traffic control, other than a parking violation, shall notify the person's employer of the conviction. The person shall notify the person's employer within 30 days after the date that the person was convicted. If the person is not currently employed, the person shall notify the division according to subdivision 1.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.168.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Department notice information.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Notification to the division must be made in writing and contain the following information:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the driver's full name;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the driver's license number;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the date of conviction;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) the specific criminal or other offense; serious traffic violation, as defined in Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, part 383; and any other violation of state or local law relating to motor vehicle traffic control, for which the person was convicted and any suspension, revocation, or cancellation of certain driving privileges that resulted from the conviction;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) an indication whether the violation was in a commercial motor vehicle;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) the location of the offense; and</p>
          <p class="in">(7) the driver's signature.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.168.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/350/#laws.1.62.0">2008 c 350 art 1 s 62</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/55/#laws.1.91.0">2022 c 55 art 1 s 91</a>,92</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.169">
        <h1 class="shn">171.169 NOTICE OF COMMERCIAL LICENSE SUSPENSION.</h1>
        <p class="in">Each employee, as defined in Code of Federal Regulations, title 49, section 383.5, who has a Minnesota-issued driver's license suspended, revoked, or canceled by this state or another state or jurisdiction, who loses the right to operate a commercial motor vehicle in this state or another state or jurisdiction for any period, or who is disqualified from operating a commercial motor vehicle for any period, shall notify the person's employer of the suspension, revocation, cancellation, lost privilege, or disqualification. The employee shall notify the employer before the end of the business day following the day the employee received notice of the suspension, revocation, cancellation, lost privilege, or disqualification.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.169.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/350/#laws.1.63.0">2008 c 350 art 1 s 63</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.17">
        <h1 class="shn">171.17 REVOCATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.17.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Offenses.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The department shall immediately revoke the license of a driver upon receiving a record of the driver's conviction of:</p>
          <p>(1) manslaughter resulting from the operation of a motor vehicle or criminal vehicular homicide or injury under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2112">609.2112</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2113">609.2113</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2114">609.2114</a>, or Minnesota Statutes 2012, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.21">609.21</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) a violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.20">169A.20</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.487">609.487</a>;</p>
          <p>(3) a felony in the commission of which a motor vehicle was used;</p>
          <p>(4) failure to stop and disclose identity and render aid, as required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.09">169.09</a>, in the event of a motor vehicle accident, resulting in the death or personal injury of another;</p>
          <p>(5) perjury or the making of a false affidavit or statement to the department under any law relating to the application, ownership, or operation of a motor vehicle, including on the certification required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.05#stat.171.05.2">171.05, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (a), clause (1), item (ii), subitem (C), to issue an instruction permit to a homeschool student;</p>
          <p>(6) except as this section otherwise provides, three charges of violating within a period of 12 months any of the provisions of chapter 169 or of the rules or municipal ordinances enacted in conformance with chapter 169, for which the accused may be punished upon conviction by imprisonment;</p>
          <p>(7) two or more violations, within five years, of the misdemeanor offense described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.444#stat.169.444.2">169.444, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (a);</p>
          <p>(8) the gross misdemeanor offense described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.444#stat.169.444.2">169.444, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (b);</p>
          <p>(9) an offense in another state that, if committed in this state, would be grounds for revoking the driver's license; or</p>
          <p>(10) a violation of an applicable speed limit by a person driving in excess of 100 miles per hour. The person's license must be revoked for six months for a violation of this clause, or for a longer minimum period of time applicable under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.53">169A.53</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.54">169A.54</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.174">171.174</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) The department shall immediately revoke the school bus endorsement of a driver upon receiving a record of the driver's conviction of the misdemeanor offense described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.443#stat.169.443.7">169.443, subdivision 7</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.17.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Offenses by juveniles.</span></h2>
          <p>When a juvenile court judge or duly authorized agent determines under a proceeding held under chapter 260 that a person under the age of 18 years has committed an offense defined in this section, the judge or authorized agent shall immediately report this determination to the department, and the commissioner shall immediately revoke the person's license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.17.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Notice.</span></h2>
          <p>Upon revoking a license under this chapter, the department shall immediately notify the licensee, in writing, by depositing in the United States post office a notice addressed to the licensee at the licensee's last known address, with postage prepaid.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.17.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Criminal vehicular operation; revocation periods.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) As used in this subdivision, "qualified prior impaired driving incident" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.03#stat.169A.03.22">169A.03, subdivision 22</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) Upon receiving a record of a conviction for a violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2112">609.2112</a>, subdivision 1, paragraph (a), clause (2), (3), (4), (5), or (6); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2113#stat.609.2113.1">609.2113, subdivision 1</a>, clause (2), (3), (4), (5), or (6); subdivision 2, clause (2), (3), (4), (5), or (6); or subdivision 3, clause (2), (3), (4), (5), or (6); or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2114#stat.609.2114.1">609.2114, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a), clause (2), (3), (4), (5), or (6), or subdivision 2, clause (2), (3), (4), (5), or (6); the commissioner shall revoke the driver's license or driving privileges of a person as follows:</p>
          <p>(1) not less than ten years if the violation resulted in great bodily harm or death to another and the person has two or more qualified prior impaired driving incidents within the past ten years or three or more qualified prior impaired driving incidents, and with denial under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.04#stat.171.04.1">171.04, subdivision 1</a>, clause (10), until rehabilitation is established according to standards established by the commissioner;</p>
          <p>(2) not less than eight years if the violation resulted in great bodily harm or death to another and the person has a qualified prior impaired driving incident within the past ten years;</p>
          <p>(3) not less than six years if the violation resulted in great bodily harm or death to another;</p>
          <p>(4) not less than six years if the violation resulted in bodily harm or substantial bodily harm to another and the person has two or more qualified prior impaired driving incidents within the past ten years or three or more qualified prior impaired driving incidents, and with denial under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.04#stat.171.04.1">171.04, subdivision 1</a>, clause (10), until rehabilitation is established according to standards established by the commissioner;</p>
          <p>(5) not less than four years if the violation resulted in bodily harm or substantial bodily harm to another and the person has a qualified prior impaired driving incident within the past ten years; or</p>
          <p>(6) not less than two years if the violation resulted in bodily harm or substantial bodily harm to another.</p>
          <p>(c) Section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.09">169A.09</a> applies when determining the number of qualified prior impaired driving incidents under this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.17.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/13">2720-145b</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/401/#laws.0.17.0">1939 c 401 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1951/0/483/#laws.0.1.0">1951 c 483 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1961/0/32/#laws.0.1.0">1961 c 32 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/1/27/#laws.0.25.0">Ex1971 c 27 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/363/#laws.0.39.0">1981 c 363 s 39</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/0/248/#laws.0.70.0">1985 c 248 s 70</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/0/291/#laws.0.21.0">1985 c 291 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/123/#laws.0.3.0">1987 c 123 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/270/#laws.0.2.0">1991 c 270 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/277/#laws.0.14.0">1991 c 277 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/78/#laws.0.5.0">1993 c 78 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/388/#laws.0.21.0">1998 c 388 s 21</a>,22; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/478/#laws.2.7.0">2000 c 478 art 2 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/6/#laws.3.76.0">1Sp2005 c 6 art 3 s 76</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/11/#laws.1.29.0">1Sp2011 c 11 art 1 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/117/#laws.3.12.0">2013 c 117 art 3 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/180/#laws.0.9.0">2014 c 180 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/83/#laws.1.60.0">2020 c 83 art 1 s 60</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/85/#laws.0.44.0">2024 c 85 s 44</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.171">
        <h1 class="shn">171.171 SUSPENSION; ILLEGAL PURCHASE OF ALCOHOL OR TOBACCO.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner shall suspend for a period of 90 days the license of a person who:</p>
        <p>(1) is under the age of 21 years and is convicted of purchasing or attempting to purchase an alcoholic beverage in violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/340A.503">340A.503</a> if the person used a license, Minnesota identification card, or any type of false identification to purchase or attempt to purchase the alcoholic beverage;</p>
        <p>(2) is convicted under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.22#stat.171.22.1">171.22, subdivision 1</a>, clause (2), or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/340A.503#stat.340A.503.2">340A.503, subdivision 2</a>, clause (3), of lending or knowingly permitting a person under the age of 21 years to use the person's license, Minnesota identification card, or other type of identification to purchase or attempt to purchase an alcoholic beverage; or</p>
        <p>(3) is convicted under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.22#stat.171.22.1">171.22, subdivision 1</a>, clause (2), of lending or knowingly permitting a person under the age of 21 years to use the person's license, Minnesota identification card, or other type of identification to purchase or attempt to purchase tobacco, a tobacco-related device, or an electronic delivery device, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.685#stat.609.685.1">609.685, subdivision 1</a>; or a nicotine or lobelia delivery product, as described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.6855#stat.609.6855.1">609.6855, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.171.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/0/305/#laws.1.1.0">1985 c 305 art 1 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/301/#laws.0.9.0">1989 c 301 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/227/#laws.0.1.0">1997 c 227 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/388/#laws.0.23.0">1998 c 388 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/472/#laws.0.1.0">2000 c 472 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/88/#laws.0.3.0">2020 c 88 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.172">
        <h1 class="shn">171.172 REVOCATION; CONTROLLED SUBSTANCE OFFENSE.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner of public safety shall revoke the license of any person convicted of or any juvenile adjudicated for a controlled substance offense if the court has notified the commissioner of a determination made under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.0271">152.0271</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260B.198#stat.260B.198.1">260B.198, subdivision 1</a>. The period of revocation shall be for the applicable time period specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.0271">152.0271</a>. If the person does not have a license or if the person's license is suspended or revoked at the time of the conviction or adjudication, the commissioner shall, upon the person's application for license issuance or reinstatement, delay the issuance or reinstatement of the person's license for the applicable time period specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.0271">152.0271</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.172.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/347/#laws.0.14.0">1993 c 347 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/388/#laws.0.24.0">1998 c 388 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/139/#laws.4.2.0">1999 c 139 art 4 s 2</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.173">
        <h1 class="shn">171.173 SUSPENSION; UNDERAGE DRINKING OFFENSE.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner of public safety shall suspend the license of any person convicted of or any juvenile adjudicated for an offense under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/340A.503#stat.340A.503.1">340A.503, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a), clause (2), if the court has notified the commissioner of a determination made under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.33#stat.169A.33.4">169A.33, subdivision 4</a>. The period of suspension shall be for the applicable period specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.33">169A.33</a>. If the person does not have a license or if the person's license is suspended or revoked at the time of the conviction or adjudication, the commissioner shall, upon the person's application for license issuance or reinstatement, delay the issuance or reinstatement of the person's license for the applicable time period specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.33">169A.33</a>. Upon receipt of the court's order, the commissioner is authorized to take the licensing action without a hearing.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.173.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/347/#laws.0.15.0">1993 c 347 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/388/#laws.0.25.0">1998 c 388 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/0/7/#laws.0.44.0">2001 c 7 s 44</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.174">
        <h1 class="shn">171.174 REVOCATION; FLEEING PEACE OFFICER OFFENSE.</h1>
