        <p>The commissioner of public safety shall revoke the license of a person upon receipt of a certificate of conviction showing that the person has in a motor vehicle violated section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.487#stat.609.487.3">609.487, subdivision 3</a> or 4, or an ordinance in conformity with those subdivisions. The commissioner shall revoke the license as follows:</p>
        <p>(1) for the first offense under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.487#stat.609.487.3">609.487, subdivision 3</a>, for not less than one year;</p>
        <p>(2) for the second offense or subsequent offenses under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.487#stat.609.487.3">609.487, subdivision 3</a>, for not less than three years;</p>
        <p>(3) for an offense under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.487#stat.609.487.4">609.487, subdivision 4</a>, clause (a), for not less than ten years;</p>
        <p>(4) for an offense under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.487#stat.609.487.4">609.487, subdivision 4</a>, clause (b), for not less than seven years; and</p>
        <p>(5) for an offense under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.487#stat.609.487.4">609.487, subdivision 4</a>, clause (c), for not less than five years.</p>
        <p class="bl">A limited license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.30">171.30</a> may not be issued for one-half of the revocation period specified in clauses (1) to (5) and after that period is over only upon and as recommended by the adjudicating court.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.174.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/408/#laws.3.10.0">1996 c 408 art 3 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/388/#laws.0.26.0">1998 c 388 s 26</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.175">
        <h1 class="shn">171.175 SUSPENSION; THEFT OF MOTOR FUEL OFFENSE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.175.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Theft of motor fuel.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of public safety shall suspend for 30 days the license of any person convicted or juvenile adjudicated delinquent for theft of motor fuel under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.52#stat.609.52.2">609.52, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (a), clause (1) or (18).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.175.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Definition.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purposes of this section, "motor fuel" has the meaning given it in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/604.15#stat.604.15.1">604.15, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.175.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/136/#laws.17.3.0">2005 c 136 art 17 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/173/#laws.0.1.0">2012 c 173 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/83/#laws.1.61.0">2020 c 83 art 1 s 61</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.176">
        <h1 class="shn">171.176 REVOCATION OR SUSPENSION; SCHOOL ATTENDANCE.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner of public safety may not link or condition issuing, suspending, or revoking a person's license to operate a motor vehicle to attendance at a secondary school.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.176.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/5/#laws.2.79.0">1Sp2005 c 5 art 2 s 79</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.177">
        <h1 class="shn">171.177 REVOCATION; PURSUANT TO SEARCH WARRANT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.177.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Search warrant-required testing advisory.</span></h2>
          <p>At the time a blood or urine test is directed pursuant to a search warrant, the person must be informed that refusal to submit to a blood or urine test is a crime.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.177.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Type of test.</span></h2>
          <p>The peace officer who directs a test pursuant to a search warrant shall direct a blood or urine test as provided in the warrant. If the warrant authorizes either a blood or urine test, the officer may direct whether the test is of blood or urine. If the person to whom the test is directed objects to the test, the officer shall offer the person an alternative test of either blood or urine. Action may be taken against a person who refuses to take a blood test only if a urine test was offered and action may be taken against a person who refuses to take a urine test only if a blood test was offered.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.177.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">License revocation pursuant to search warrant.</span></h2>
          <p>After executing a search warrant for the collection of a blood or urine sample based upon probable cause of a violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.20">169A.20</a>, the peace officer acting under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.13">626.13</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.17">626.17</a> shall certify to the commissioner of public safety:</p>
          <p>(1) when a person refuses to comply with the execution of the search warrant; or</p>
          <p>(2) if a person submits to the test and the test results indicate:</p>
          <p>(i) an alcohol concentration of 0.08 or more;</p>
          <p>(ii) an alcohol concentration of 0.04 or more, if the person was driving, operating, or in physical control of a commercial motor vehicle at the time of the violation; or</p>
          <p>(iii) the presence of a controlled substance listed in Schedule I or II or its metabolite, other than marijuana or tetrahydrocannabinols.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.177.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Test refusal; license revocation.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Upon certification under subdivision 3 that there existed probable cause to believe the person had been driving, operating, or in physical control of a motor vehicle in violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.20">169A.20</a>, and that the person refused to comply with the execution of the search warrant, the commissioner shall revoke the person's license or permit to drive or nonresident operating privilege. The commissioner shall revoke the license, permit, or nonresident operating privilege:</p>
          <p>(1) for a person with no qualified prior impaired driving incidents within the past ten years, for a period of not less than one year;</p>
          <p>(2) for a person under the age of 21 years and with no qualified prior impaired driving incidents within the past ten years, for a period of not less than one year;</p>
          <p>(3) for a person with one qualified prior impaired driving incident within the past ten years or two qualified prior impaired driving incidents, for a period of not less than two years;</p>
          <p>(4) for a person with two qualified prior impaired driving incidents within the past ten years or three qualified prior impaired driving incidents, for a period of not less than three years;</p>
          <p>(5) for a person with three qualified prior impaired driving incidents within the past ten years, for a period of not less than four years; or</p>
          <p>(6) for a person with four or more qualified prior impaired driving incidents, for a period of not less than six years.</p>
          <p>(b) When a person who had been driving, operating, or in physical control of a commercial motor vehicle refuses to comply with the search warrant and permit testing, the commissioner shall disqualify the person from operating a commercial motor vehicle and shall revoke the person's license or permit to drive or nonresident operating privilege according to the federal regulations adopted by reference in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.165">171.165, subdivision </a>2.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.177.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Test failure; license revocation.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Upon certification under subdivision 3 pursuant to a search warrant, that there existed probable cause to believe the person had been driving, operating, or in physical control of a motor vehicle in violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.20">169A.20</a>, and that the person submitted to a test and the test results indicate an alcohol concentration of 0.08 or more or the presence of a controlled substance listed in Schedule I or II or its metabolite, other than marijuana or tetrahydrocannabinols, the commissioner shall revoke the person's license or permit to drive or nonresident operating privilege:</p>
          <p>(1) for a period of 90 days or, if the test results indicate an alcohol concentration of twice the legal limit or more, not less than one year;</p>
          <p>(2) if the person is under the age of 21 years, for a period of not less than 180 days or, if the test results indicate an alcohol concentration of twice the legal limit or more, not less than one year;</p>
          <p>(3) for a person with one qualified prior impaired driving incident within the past ten years or two qualified prior impaired driving incidents, for a period of not less than one year or, if the test results indicate an alcohol concentration of twice the legal limit or more, not less than two years;</p>
          <p>(4) for a person with two qualified prior impaired driving incidents within the past ten years or three qualified prior impaired driving incidents, for a period of not less than three years;</p>
          <p>(5) for a person with three qualified prior impaired driving incidents within the past ten years, for a period of not less than four years; or</p>
          <p>(6) for a person with four or more qualified prior impaired driving incidents, for a period of not less than six years.</p>
          <p>(b) On certification by the peace officer that there existed probable cause to believe the person had been driving, operating, or in physical control of a commercial motor vehicle with any presence of alcohol and that the person submitted to a test and the test results indicated an alcohol concentration of 0.04 or more, the commissioner shall disqualify the person from operating a commercial motor vehicle under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.165">171.165</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) If the test is of a person's blood or urine by a laboratory operated by the Bureau of Criminal Apprehension or authorized by the bureau to conduct the analysis of a blood or urine sample, the laboratory may directly certify to the commissioner the test results, and the peace officer shall certify to the commissioner that there existed probable cause to believe the person had been driving, operating, or in physical control of a motor vehicle in violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.20">169A.20</a>, and that the person submitted to a test. Upon receipt of both certifications, the commissioner shall undertake the license actions described in paragraphs (a) and (b).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.177.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Unlicensed drivers; license issuance denial.</span></h2>
          <p>If the person is a resident without a license or permit to operate a motor vehicle in this state, the commissioner shall deny to the person the issuance of a license or permit after the date of the alleged violation for the same period as provided in this section for revocation, subject to review as provided in subdivisions 10 and 11.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.177.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Notice of revocation or disqualification; review.</span></h2>
          <p>A revocation under this section, or a disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.165">171.165</a>, becomes effective at the time the commissioner or a peace officer acting on behalf of the commissioner notifies the person of the intention to revoke, disqualify, or both, and of revocation or disqualification. The notice must advise the person of the right to obtain administrative and judicial review as provided in subdivisions 10 and 11. If mailed, the notice and order of revocation or disqualification is deemed received three days after mailing to the last known address of the person.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.177.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Test refusal; driving privilege lost.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) On behalf of the commissioner, a peace officer requiring a test or directing the administration of a chemical test pursuant to a search warrant shall serve immediate notice of intention to revoke and of revocation on a person who refuses to permit a test or on a person who submits to a test, the results of which indicate an alcohol concentration of 0.08 or more.</p>
          <p>(b) On behalf of the commissioner, a peace officer requiring a test or directing the administration of a chemical test of a person driving, operating, or in physical control of a commercial motor vehicle pursuant to a search warrant shall serve immediate notice of intention to disqualify and of disqualification on a person who refuses to permit a test or on a person who submits to a test, the results of which indicate an alcohol concentration of 0.04 or more.</p>
          <p>(c) The officer shall:</p>
          <p>(1) invalidate the person's driver's license or permit card by clipping the upper corner of the card in such a way that no identifying information including the photo is destroyed, and immediately return the card to the person;</p>
          <p>(2) issue the person a temporary license effective for only seven days; and</p>
          <p>(3) send the notification of this action to the commissioner along with the certificate required by subdivision 4 or 5.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.177.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Notice of action to other states.</span></h2>
          <p>When a nonresident's privilege to operate a motor vehicle in this state has been revoked or denied, the commissioner shall give information of the action taken in writing to the official in charge of traffic control or public safety of the state of the person's residence and of any state in which the person has a license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.177.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Administrative review.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) At any time during a period of revocation imposed under this section, or a period of disqualification imposed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.165">171.165</a>, a person may request in writing a review of the order of revocation or disqualification by the commissioner, unless the person is entitled to review under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.166">171.166</a>. Upon receiving a request, the commissioner or the commissioner's designee shall review the order, the evidence upon which the order was based, and any other material information brought to the attention of the commissioner and determine whether sufficient cause exists to sustain the order. Within 15 days of receiving the request, the commissioner shall report in writing the results of the review. The review provided in this subdivision is not subject to the contested case provisions of the Administrative Procedure Act in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.001">14.001</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.69">14.69</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) The availability of administrative review for an order of revocation or disqualification has no effect upon the availability of judicial review under this section.</p>
          <p>(c) Review under this subdivision must take place, if possible, at the same time as any administrative review of the person's impoundment order under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.60">169A.60, subdivision </a>9.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.177.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Petition for judicial review.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Within 60 days following receipt of a notice and order of revocation pursuant to this section, a person may petition the court for review. The petition must be filed with the district court administrator in the county where the alleged offense occurred, together with proof of service of a copy on the commissioner, and accompanied by the standard filing fee for civil actions. Responsive pleading is not required of the commissioner, and court fees must not be charged for the appearance of the commissioner in the matter.</p>
          <p>(b) The petition must:</p>
          <p>(1) be captioned in the full name of the person making the petition as petitioner and the commissioner as respondent;</p>
          <p>(2) include the petitioner's date of birth and driver's license number, and the date of the offense; and</p>
          <p>(3) state with specificity the grounds upon which the petitioner seeks rescission of the order of revocation, disqualification, or denial.</p>
          <p>(c) The filing of the petition does not stay the revocation, disqualification, or denial. The reviewing court may order a stay of the balance of the revocation or disqualification if the hearing has not been conducted within 60 days after filing the petition upon terms the court deems proper.</p>
          <p>(d) Judicial reviews must be conducted according to the Rules of Civil Procedure, except that prehearing discovery is mandatory and is limited to:</p>
          <p>(1) the notice of revocation;</p>
          <p>(2) the certificate of analysis of the blood or urine test;</p>
          <p>(3) the peace officer's certificate and any accompanying documentation submitted by the arresting officer to the commissioner; and</p>
          <p>(4) disclosure of potential witnesses, including experts, and the basis of their testimony.</p>
          <p class="bl">Other types of discovery are available only upon order of the court.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.177.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Judicial hearing; issues, order, appeal.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A judicial review hearing under this section must be before a district judge in any county in the judicial district where the alleged offense occurred. The hearing is to the court and may be conducted at the same time and in the same manner as hearings upon pretrial motions in the criminal prosecution under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.20">169A.20</a>, if any. The hearing must be recorded. The commissioner shall appear and be represented by the attorney general or through the prosecuting authority for the jurisdiction involved. The hearing must be held at the earliest practicable date, and in any event no later than 60 days following the filing of the petition for review. The judicial district administrator shall establish procedures to ensure efficient compliance with this subdivision. To accomplish this, the administrator may, whenever possible, consolidate and transfer review hearings among the locations within the judicial district where terms of district court are held.</p>
          <p>(b) The scope of the hearing is limited to the issues in clauses (1) to (13):</p>
          <p>(1) Did the peace officer have probable cause to believe the person was driving, operating, or in physical control of a motor vehicle or commercial motor vehicle in violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.20">169A.20</a>?</p>
          <p>(2) Was the person lawfully placed under arrest for violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.20">169A.20</a>?</p>
          <p>(3) Was the person involved in a motor vehicle accident or collision resulting in property damage, personal injury, or death?</p>
          <p>(4) Did a licensed peace officer apply for a search warrant in accordance with the requirements set forth in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.04">626.04</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.18">626.18</a> or conforming statutes in an adjacent state?</p>
          <p>(5) Did a neutral magistrate review the application for a search warrant and determine there was probable cause to believe that the person was driving, operating, or in physical control of a motor vehicle or commercial motor vehicle in violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.20">169A.20</a>?</p>
          <p>(6) Was the search warrant and the process by which it was obtained valid?</p>
          <p>(7) At the time of directing the person to take the test, did the peace officer inform the person that refusing the test was a crime as required by subdivision 1?</p>
          <p>(8) Did the person refuse to permit the test?</p>
          <p>(9) If a test was taken by a person driving, operating, or in physical control of a motor vehicle, did the test results indicate at the time of testing:</p>
          <p>(i) an alcohol concentration of 0.08 or more; or</p>
          <p>(ii) the presence of a controlled substance listed in Schedule I or II or its metabolite, other than marijuana or tetrahydrocannabinols?</p>
          <p>(10) If a test was taken by a person driving, operating, or in physical control of a commercial motor vehicle, did the test results indicate an alcohol concentration of 0.04 or more at the time of testing?</p>
          <p>(11) Was the testing method used valid and reliable and were the test results accurately evaluated?</p>
          <p>(12) Did the person prove the defense of necessity?</p>
          <p>(13) Did the person prove the defense of controlled substance use in accordance with a prescription?</p>
          <p>(c) Certified or otherwise authenticated copies of laboratory or medical personnel reports, records, documents, licenses, and certificates are admissible as substantive evidence.</p>
          <p>(d) The court shall order that the revocation or disqualification be either rescinded or sustained and forward the order to the commissioner. The court shall file its order within 14 days following the hearing. If the revocation or disqualification is sustained, the court shall also forward the person's driver's license or permit to the commissioner for further action by the commissioner if the license or permit is not already in the commissioner's possession.</p>
          <p>(e) Any party aggrieved by the decision of the reviewing court may appeal the decision as provided in the Rules of Appellate Procedure.</p>
          <p>(f) The civil hearing under this section shall not give rise to an estoppel on any issues arising from the same set of circumstances in any criminal prosecution.</p>
          <p>(g) It is an affirmative defense for the petitioner to prove a necessity.</p>
          <p>(h) It is an affirmative defense to the presence of a Schedule I or II controlled substance that the person used the controlled substance according to the terms of a prescription issued for the person according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.11">152.11</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.12">152.12</a>, unless the court finds by a preponderance of the evidence that the use of the controlled substance impaired the person's ability to operate a motor vehicle.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.177.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Test refusal; no test given.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If a person refuses to permit a blood or urine test as required by a search warrant and the provisions of this section, then a test must not be given. However, the applicable provisions of this section, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.52">169A.52, subdivision </a>1, and other law apply.</p>
          <p>(b) Notwithstanding paragraph (a), if a peace officer has probable cause to believe that a person has violated section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2112">609.2112</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2113">609.2113</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2114">609.2114</a>, or Minnesota Statutes 2012, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.21">609.21</a>, a test may be required and obtained despite the person's refusal. A refusal to submit to a test does not constitute a violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.50">609.50</a>, unless the refusal was accompanied by force or violence or the threat of force or violence.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.177.14">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>The definitions in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.03">169A.03</a> apply to this section.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.177.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/83/#laws.2.10.0">2017 c 83 art 2 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/55/#laws.1.93.0">2022 c 55 art 1 s 93</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/123/#laws.5.4.0">2024 c 123 art 5 s 4</a>-9</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.18">
        <h1 class="shn">171.18 SUSPENSION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.18.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Offenses.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Subject to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.16">171.16</a>, the commissioner may suspend the license of a driver without preliminary hearing upon a showing by department records or other sufficient evidence that the licensee:</p>
          <p>(1) has committed an offense for which mandatory revocation of license is required upon conviction;</p>
          <p>(2) has been convicted by a court for violating a provision of chapter 169 or an ordinance regulating traffic, other than a conviction for a petty misdemeanor, and department records show that the violation contributed in causing an accident resulting in the death or personal injury of another, or serious property damage;</p>
          <p>(3) is an habitually reckless or negligent driver of a motor vehicle;</p>
          <p>(4) is an habitual violator of the traffic laws;</p>
          <p>(5) is incompetent to drive a motor vehicle as determined in a judicial proceeding;</p>
          <p>(6) has permitted an unlawful or fraudulent use of the license;</p>
          <p>(7) has committed an offense in another state that, if committed in this state, would be grounds for suspension;</p>
          <p>(8) has committed a violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.444#stat.169.444.2">169.444, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (a), within five years of a prior conviction under that section;</p>
          <p>(9) has committed a violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.22">171.22</a>, except that the commissioner may not suspend a person's driver's license based solely on the fact that the person possessed a fictitious or fraudulently altered Minnesota identification card;</p>
          <p>(10) has failed to appear in court as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.16#stat.171.16.3a">171.16, subdivision 3a</a>;</p>
          <p>(11) has failed to report a medical condition that, if reported, would have resulted in cancellation of driving privileges;</p>
          <p>(12) has been found to have committed an offense under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.33">169A.33</a>; or</p>
          <p>(13) has paid or attempted to pay a fee required under this chapter for a license or permit by means of a dishonored check issued to the state or a driver's license agent, which must be continued until the registrar determines or is informed by the agent that the dishonored check has been paid in full.</p>
          <p class="bl">However, an action taken by the commissioner under clause (2) or (5) must conform to the recommendation of the court when made in connection with the prosecution of the licensee.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner is prohibited from suspending the driver's license of an individual who meets any of the conditions described in paragraph (a) due to a conviction for a violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.24">171.24</a>, subdivision 1 or 2.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.18.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Notice.</span></h2>
          <p>The department may not suspend a driver's license until 14 days after the date on which the department mails to the licensee a written notice of the department's intent to suspend the license, addressed to the licensee at the licensee's last known address, with postage prepaid. Suspension of a driver's license shall take effect immediately upon a finding by the commissioner, based on department records or other sufficient evidence, that a delay in the effectiveness of the suspension poses a threat to public safety. The department shall immediately notify the licensee of the suspension by mail, addressed to the licensee's last known address, with postage prepaid.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.18.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Hearing.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The licensee may request, in writing, a hearing. The department shall afford the requesting licensee an opportunity for a hearing within 20 days after receipt of the request in the county where the licensee resides, unless the department and the licensee agree that the hearing may be held in some other county.</p>
          <p>(b) For the hearing, the commissioner may administer oaths and issue subpoenas for the attendance of witnesses and the production of relevant books and papers, and may require a reexamination of the licensee.</p>
          <p>(c) Following the hearing, the department shall either rescind its order of suspension or, for good cause shown, may extend the suspension of the license or revoke the license.</p>
          <p>(d) The department shall not suspend a license for a period of more than one year.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.18.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/13">2720-145c</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/401/#laws.0.18.0">1939 c 401 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/713/#laws.0.1.0">1955 c 713 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/698/#laws.0.6.0">1973 c 698 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/783/#laws.0.7.0">1978 c 783 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/307/#laws.0.30.0">1989 c 307 s 30</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/510/#laws.0.8.0">1990 c 510 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/277/#laws.0.15.0">1991 c 277 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/636/#laws.2.4.0">1994 c 636 art 2 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.2.41.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 2 s 41</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/346/#laws.0.6.0">1996 c 346 s 6</a>,7; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/442/#laws.0.21.0">1996 c 442 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/478/#laws.2.7.0">2000 c 478 art 2 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/6/#laws.3.77.0">1Sp2005 c 6 art 3 s 77</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/260/#laws.2.17.0">2006 c 260 art 2 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/5/#laws.4.80.0">1Sp2021 c 5 art 4 s 80</a>,149</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.181">
        <h1 class="shn">171.181 RESIDENT DRIVING PRIVILEGE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.181.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Foreign state conviction.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) On revoking or suspending the driver's license of a Minnesota resident as a result of a foreign state conviction, the commissioner shall notify that foreign state when the driver's license is reinstated or a new license issued.</p>
          <p>(b) For the purposes of this section, "foreign state" means a state as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.01#stat.171.01.47">171.01, subdivision 47</a>, excluding the state of Minnesota.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.181.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/127/#laws.0.2.0">1983 c 127 s 2</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.181.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/783/#laws.0.8.0">1978 c 783 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/10/#laws.1.32.0">2005 c 10 art 1 s 32</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.182">
        <h1 class="shn">171.182 SUSPENSION; UNINSURED VEHICLE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.182.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definition.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purposes of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.182">171.182</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.184">171.184</a>, the term "judgment" means a judgment which has become final by expiration without appeal within the time which an appeal might have been perfected, or by final affirmation on appeal, rendered by a court of competent jurisdiction, including county, municipal, or conciliation court, of any state of the United States, upon a claim for relief arising out of ownership, maintenance, or use of a motor vehicle, for damages, including damages for care and loss of services, because of bodily injury to or death of a person, or for damages because of injury to or destruction of property, including the loss of use thereof, or upon a claim for relief on an agreement of settlement for the damages.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.182.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Copy of judgment to commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p>If a person fails within 30 days to satisfy a judgment, the court administrator, upon affidavit of the judgment creditor that the judgment has not been satisfied, shall immediately notify the commissioner that the judgment has not been satisfied.</p>
          <p>If the judgment debtor named in a judgment reported to the commissioner is a nonresident, the commissioner shall notify the official in charge of the issuance of drivers' licenses of the state of which the judgment debtor is a resident.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.182.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Conditions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner, upon notification of a judgment, shall suspend the license or the nonresident's operating privilege of the person against whom judgment was rendered if:</p>
          <p>(1) at the time of the accident the person did not maintain the reparation security required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/65B.48">65B.48</a>; and</p>
          <p>(2) the judgment has not been satisfied.</p>
          <p>(b) Suspensions under this section are subject to the notice requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.18#stat.171.18.2">171.18, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.182.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Duration.</span></h2>
          <p>A license or nonresident's operating privilege shall remain suspended and shall not be renewed, nor shall a license be thereafter issued to the person until every judgment is satisfied in full, or has expired, or to the extent hereinafter provided.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.182.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Affidavit of insurance coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>A person whose license or nonresident's operating privileges has been suspended or has become subject to suspension because of an unsatisfied judgment may be relieved of the suspension by filing with the commissioner an affidavit stating that at the time of the accident giving rise to the judgment the person was insured, that the insurer is liable for the judgment, and the reason, if known, why the judgment has not been paid. The affidavit shall be accompanied by a copy of the insurance policy and other documents the commissioner requires to show that the loss, injury or damage giving rise to the judgment was covered by the policy. If satisfied that the insurer was authorized to issue the policy and is liable for the judgment, at least to the extent and for the amounts required in this chapter, the commissioner shall not suspend the license, or reinstate the license if already suspended.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.182.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/598/#laws.0.1.0">1982 c 598 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/1/3/#laws.1.82.0">1Sp1986 c 3 art 1 s 82</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/346/#laws.0.8.0">1996 c 346 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/123/#laws.13.2.0">2024 c 123 art 13 s 2</a>,3</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.183">
        <h1 class="shn">171.183 SATISFACTION OF JUDGMENT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.183.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purposes of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.182">171.182</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.184">171.184</a>, a judgment is satisfied if:</p>
          <p>(1) $30,000 has been credited upon any judgment or judgments rendered in excess of that amount because of bodily injury to or death of one person as the result of any one accident;</p>
          <p>(2) subject to the limit of $30,000 because of bodily injury to or death of one person, the sum of $60,000 has been credited upon any judgment or judgments rendered in excess of that amount because of bodily injury to or death of two or more persons as the result of any one accident; or</p>
          <p>(3) $10,000 has been credited upon any judgment or judgments rendered in excess of that amount because of damage to or destruction of property of others as a result of any one accident.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.183.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Other payments credited.</span></h2>
          <p>Payments made in settlement of claims because of bodily injury, death or property damage arising from a motor vehicle accident shall be credited in reduction of the amounts provided for in this section.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.183.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/598/#laws.0.2.0">1982 c 598 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/8/#laws.2.47.0">1Sp2001 c 8 art 2 s 47</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.184">
        <h1 class="shn">171.184 INSTALLMENT PAYMENTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.184.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Authorization.</span></h2>
          <p>A judgment debtor upon due notice to the judgment creditor may apply to the court in which the judgment was rendered for the privilege of paying the judgment in installments. The court, in its discretion and without prejudice to any other legal remedies which the judgment creditor may have, may so order and fix the amounts and times of payments of the installments.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.184.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Stay of suspension.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall not suspend a license or a nonresident's operating privilege if the judgment debtor gives proof of maintaining the reparation security required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/65B.48">65B.48</a>, obtains an order or enters into a written agreement with the judgment creditor permitting the payment of the judgment in installments, and does not default on the payment of any installment.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.184.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Termination of stay.</span></h2>
          <p>If the judgment debtor fails to pay any installment as specified by an order or agreement, then upon notice of default, the commissioner shall forthwith suspend the license, or nonresident's operating privilege, of the judgment debtor until the judgment is satisfied.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.184.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/598/#laws.0.3.0">1982 c 598 s 3</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.171.185"><b>171.185</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/6/#laws.2.48.0">1Sp2005 c 6 art 2 s 48</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.186">
        <h1 class="shn">171.186 SUSPENSION FOR NONPAYMENT OF SUPPORT; LIMITED LICENSE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.186.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Suspension.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall suspend a person's driver's license or operating privileges without a hearing upon receipt of a court order or notice from a public authority responsible for child support enforcement that states that the driver is in arrears in court-ordered child support or maintenance payments, or both, in an amount equal to or greater than three times the obligor's total monthly support and maintenance payments, and is not in compliance with a written payment agreement pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518A.69">518A.69</a> that is approved by a court, a child support magistrate, or the public authority responsible for child support enforcement, in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518A.65">518A.65</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.186.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Notice.</span></h2>
          <p>Upon suspending a driver's license or operating privileges under this section, the department shall immediately notify the licensee, in writing, by mailing a notice addressed to the licensee at the licensee's last known address.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.186.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Duration.</span></h2>
          <p>A license or operating privilege must remain suspended and may not be reinstated, nor may a license be subsequently issued to the person, until the commissioner receives notice from the court, a child support magistrate, or public authority responsible for child support enforcement that the person is in compliance with all current orders of support or written payment agreements pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518A.69">518A.69</a>. A fee may not be assessed for reinstatement of a license under this section unless the person whose license was suspended under this section has obtained a limited license during the period of suspension.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.186.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Limited license.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Notwithstanding subdivision 3, the commissioner may issue a limited license to a person whose license has been suspended under this section if the person qualifies for a limited license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.30">171.30</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) A limited license issued to a person under this subdivision must expire 90 days after the date it is issued.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.186.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/257/#laws.1.11.0">1995 c 257 art 1 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/196/#laws.2.3.0">1999 c 196 art 2 s 3</a>,4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/344/#laws.0.1.0">2002 c 344 s 1</a>-3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/164/#laws.0.29.0">2005 c 164 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/7/#laws.0.28.0">1Sp2005 c 7 s 28</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.187">
        <h1 class="shn">171.187 SUSPENSION; CRIMINAL VEHICULAR OPERATION AND MANSLAUGHTER.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.187.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Suspension required.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall suspend the driver's license of a person:</p>
          <p>(1) for whom a peace officer has made the certification described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/629.344">629.344 </a>that probable cause exists to believe that the person violated section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2112">609.2112</a>, subdivision 1, paragraph (a), clause (2), (3), (4), (5), or (6); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2113#stat.609.2113.1">609.2113, subdivision 1</a>, clause (2), (3), (4), (5), or (6); subdivision 2, clause (2), (3), (4), (5), or (6); or subdivision 3, clause (2), (3), (4), (5), or (6); or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2114#stat.609.2114.1">609.2114, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a), clause (2), (3), (4), (5), or (6), or subdivision 2, clause (2), (3), (4), (5), or (6); or</p>
          <p>(2) who has been formally charged with a violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.20">609.20</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.205">609.205</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2112">609.2112</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2113">609.2113</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2114">609.2114</a>, resulting from the operation of a motor vehicle.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.187.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Suspension period.</span></h2>
          <p>A suspension under this section continues until:</p>
          <p>(1) the conviction, acquittal, or dismissal of the underlying crime that resulted in the suspension; or</p>
          <p>(2) the commissioner, acting under subdivision 4, orders the termination of the suspension.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.187.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Credit.</span></h2>
          <p>If a person whose driver's license was suspended under subdivision 1 is later convicted of the underlying offense that resulted in the suspension and the commissioner revokes the person's license, the commissioner shall credit the time accrued under the suspension period toward the revocation period imposed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.17">171.17</a>, subdivision 4, or for violations of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.20">609.20</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.205">609.205</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2112">609.2112</a>, subdivision 1, paragraph (a), clause (1), (7), or (8); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2113#stat.609.2113.1">609.2113, subdivision 1</a>, clause (1), (7), or (8); subdivision 2, clause (1), (7), or (8); or subdivision 3, clause (1), (7), or (8); or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2114#stat.609.2114.1">609.2114, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a), clause (1), (7), or (8), or subdivision 2, clause (1), (7), or (8).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.187.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Administrative review of license suspension.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) At any time during which a person's driver's license is suspended under this section, the person may request in writing a review of the suspension by the commissioner. Upon receiving a request, the commissioner or the commissioner's designee shall review the order of suspension, the evidence upon which the order was based, and any other material information brought to the attention of the commissioner, and determine whether sufficient cause exists to sustain the order. Within 15 days of receiving the request, the commissioner shall report in writing the results of the review. The review provided in this subdivision is not subject to the contested case provisions in chapter 14.</p>
          <p>(b) In addition to any other reason provided for in this subdivision, a person may request a review of the suspension by the commissioner if the suspension has been in place for at least three months and the person has not been indicted or formally charged with the underlying crime that resulted in the license suspension.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.187.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/117/#laws.3.13.0">2013 c 117 art 3 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/180/#laws.0.9.0">2014 c 180 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/83/#laws.1.62.0">2020 c 83 art 1 s 62</a>,63</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.188">
        <h1 class="shn">171.188 DRIVING WHILE IMPAIRED REVOCATION AND PROHIBITION; OFF-ROAD RECREATIONAL VEHICLES AND MOTORBOATS.</h1>
        <p>(a) The provisions of this chapter for revoking or canceling a driver's license or nonresident driving privilege for alcohol, controlled substance, or intoxicating substance violations also apply to the operating privileges for off-road recreational vehicles and motorboats.</p>
        <p>(b) Upon conviction, the commissioner must notify a person that the person is prohibited from operating off-road recreational vehicles and motorboats for the same period that the person's driver's license or driving privilege is revoked or canceled for the alcohol, controlled substance, or intoxicating substance conviction.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.188.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/6/#laws.3.10.0">1Sp2021 c 6 art 3 s 10</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.19">
        <h1 class="shn">171.19 PETITION FOR COURT HEARING ON LICENSE REINSTATEMENT.</h1>
        <p>Any person whose driver's license has been refused, revoked, suspended, canceled, or disqualified by the commissioner, except where the license is revoked or disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.52">169A.52</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.177">171.177</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.186">171.186</a>, may file a petition for a hearing in the matter in the district court in the county wherein such person shall reside and, in the case of a nonresident, in the district court in any county, and such court is hereby vested with jurisdiction, and it shall be its duty, to set the matter for hearing upon 15 days' written notice to the commissioner, and thereupon to take testimony and examine into the facts of the case to determine whether the petitioner is entitled to a license or is subject to revocation, suspension, cancellation, disqualification, or refusal of license, and shall render judgment accordingly. The petition for hearing must either be filed within 180 days of the effective date of the order of revocation, suspension, cancellation, disqualification, or refusal to license or be filed before expiration of the withdrawal period, whichever occurs first. The petition shall be heard by the court without a jury and may be heard in or out of term. The commissioner may appear in person, or by agents or representatives, and may present evidence upon the hearing by affidavit personally, by agents, or by representatives. The petitioner may present evidence by affidavit, except that the petitioner must be present in person at such hearing for the purpose of cross-examination. In the event the department shall be sustained in these proceedings, the petitioner shall have no further right to make further petition to any court for the purpose of obtaining a driver's license until after the expiration of one year after the date of such hearing.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.19.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/13">2720-145d</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/401/#laws.0.19.0">1939 c 401 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/423/#laws.0.11.0">1982 c 423 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/571/#laws.14.10.0">1992 c 571 art 14 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/245/#laws.1.2.0">1997 c 245 art 1 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/1/2/#laws.0.54.0">1Sp1997 c 2 s 54</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/405/#laws.0.6.0">1998 c 405 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/478/#laws.2.7.0">2000 c 478 art 2 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/83/#laws.3.18.0">2017 c 83 art 3 s 18</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.20">
        <h1 class="shn">171.20 MINNESOTA LICENSE REQUIRED; CONDITIONS, FEES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.20.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/266/#laws.0.34.0">1993 c 266 s 34</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.20.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Operation after revocation, suspension, cancellation, or disqualification.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A resident or nonresident whose driver's license or right or privilege to operate a motor vehicle in this state has been suspended, revoked, or canceled, shall not operate a motor vehicle in this state under license, permit, or registration certificate issued by any other jurisdiction or otherwise during the suspension, or after the revocation until Minnesota driving privileges are reinstated.</p>
          <p>(b) A resident or nonresident who has been disqualified from holding a commercial driver's license or been denied the privilege to operate a commercial motor vehicle in this state shall not operate a commercial motor vehicle in this state under license, permit, or registration certificate issued by any other jurisdiction or otherwise during the disqualification period until Minnesota commercial driving privileges are reinstated.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.20.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Driver improvement clinic.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may require, before reissuing a license which has been revoked or suspended, that the licensee complete a course of study at an approved driver improvement clinic or, in the case of a licensee who is age 18 or younger, a youth-oriented driver improvement clinic. The commissioner may not require the licensee to complete such a course unless an approved driver improvement clinic or youth-oriented driver improvement clinic is located within 35 miles of the licensee's residence. For purposes of this section "an approved driver improvement clinic" means a clinic whose curriculum and mode of instruction conform to standards promulgated by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.20.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Reinstatement fee.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Before the license is reinstated, a single $20 reinstatement fee is imposed for:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) an individual whose driver's license has been suspended under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.16">171.16</a>, subdivision 2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.175">171.175</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.18">171.18</a>; or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.182">171.182</a>, or who has been disqualified from holding a commercial driver's license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.165">171.165</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) an individual whose driver's license has been suspended under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.186">171.186</a> and who is not exempt from such a fee; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) an individual whose license has been suspended under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.791">169.791</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.798">169.798</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) An individual whose driver's license is subject to more than one suspension and who is otherwise eligible for reinstatement must pay a single reinstatement fee and a single filing fee. An individual whose driver's license has been suspended and revoked and who is otherwise eligible for reinstatement must pay a reinstatement fee as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.29">171.29</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) When fees are collected by a driver's license agent appointed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.061">171.061</a>, a filing fee is imposed in the amount specified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.061#stat.171.061.4">171.061, subdivision 4</a>. The reinstatement fee and filing fee must be deposited in an approved state depository as directed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.061#stat.171.061.4">171.061, subdivision 4</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) Reinstatement fees collected under paragraph (a) for suspensions under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.18#stat.171.18.1">171.18, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a), clause (10), must be deposited in the general fund.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) A suspension may be rescinded without fee for good cause.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.20.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/13">2720-145e</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/401/#laws.0.20.0">1939 c 401 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1961/0/44/#laws.0.1.0">1961 c 44 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1965/0/711/#laws.0.6.0">1965 c 711 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/9/#laws.0.32.0">1969 c 9 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/118/#laws.0.2.0">1969 c 118 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/520/#laws.0.3.0">1980 c 520 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/127/#laws.0.1.0">1983 c 127 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/0/291/#laws.0.22.0">1985 c 291 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/228/#laws.0.3.0">1989 c 228 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/307/#laws.0.31.0">1989 c 307 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/265/#laws.2.20.0">1995 c 265 art 2 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/1/2/#laws.0.55.0">1Sp1997 c 2 s 55</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/388/#laws.0.27.0">1998 c 388 s 27</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/405/#laws.0.7.0">1998 c 405 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/426/#laws.0.28.0">2000 c 426 s 28</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/344/#laws.0.4.0">2002 c 344 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/19/#laws.2.39.0">1Sp2003 c 19 art 2 s 39</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/295/#laws.1.13.0">2004 c 295 art 1 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/136/#laws.18.12.0">2005 c 136 art 18 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/6/#laws.2.42.0">1Sp2005 c 6 art 2 s 42</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/143/#laws.3.7.0">2007 c 143 art 3 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/5/#laws.2.4.0">1Sp2019 c 5 art 2 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/5/#laws.4.81.0">1Sp2021 c 5 art 4 s 81</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/55/#laws.1.94.0">2022 c 55 art 1 s 94</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.21">
        <h1 class="shn">171.21 COPY OF RECORD AS EVIDENCE.</h1>
        <p>Copies of any of the files or records of the department certified by the commissioner as being true copies shall be received in evidence in any court in this state with the same force and effect as the originals.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.21.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/13">2720-145f</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/401/#laws.0.21.0">1939 c 401 s 21</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.22">
        <h1 class="shn">171.22 UNLAWFUL ACTS RELATING TO DRIVER'S LICENSE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.22.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Violations.</span></h2>
          <p>With regard to any driver's license, including a commercial driver's license, it shall be unlawful for any person:</p>
          <p>(1) to display, cause or permit to be displayed, or have in possession, any fictitious or fraudulently altered driver's license or Minnesota identification card;</p>
          <p>(2) to lend the person's driver's license or Minnesota identification card to any other person or knowingly permit the use thereof by another;</p>
          <p>(3) to display or represent as one's own any driver's license or Minnesota identification card not issued to that person;</p>
          <p>(4) to use a fictitious name or date of birth to any police officer or in any application for a driver's license or Minnesota identification card, or to knowingly make a false statement, or to knowingly conceal a material fact, or otherwise commit a fraud in any such application;</p>
          <p>(5) to alter any driver's license or Minnesota identification card;</p>
          <p>(6) to take any part of the driver's license examination for another or to permit another to take the examination for that person;</p>
          <p>(7) to make a counterfeit driver's license or Minnesota identification card;</p>
          <p>(8) to use the name and date of birth of another person to any police officer for the purpose of falsely identifying oneself to the police officer;</p>
          <p>(9) to display as a valid driver's license any canceled, revoked, or suspended driver's license. A person whose driving privileges have been withdrawn may display a driver's license only for identification purposes; or</p>
          <p>(10) to submit a false affidavit or statement to the department on the certification required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.05#stat.171.05.2">171.05, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (a), clause (1), item (ii), subitem (C), to issue an instruction permit to a homeschool student.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.22.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Penalties.</span></h2>
          <p>Any person who violates subdivision 1, clause (7) or (8), is guilty of a gross misdemeanor. Any person who violates any other provision of subdivision 1 is guilty of a misdemeanor.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.22.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/13">2720-145g</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/401/#laws.0.22.0">1939 c 401 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/0/564/#laws.0.5.0">1957 c 564 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1961/0/64/#laws.0.1.0">1961 c 64 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/9/#laws.0.33.0">1969 c 9 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/163/#laws.0.1.0">1971 c 163 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/681/#laws.0.16.0">1988 c 681 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/301/#laws.0.10.0">1989 c 301 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/307/#laws.0.32.0">1989 c 307 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/266/#laws.0.26.0">1993 c 266 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/501/#laws.0.8.0">1994 c 501 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/636/#laws.2.5.0">1994 c 636 art 2 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/11/#laws.1.30.0">1Sp2011 c 11 art 1 s 30</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/85/#laws.0.45.0">2024 c 85 s 45</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.23">
        <h1 class="shn">171.23 RENTING VEHICLE TO UNLICENSED DRIVER.</h1>
        <p>No person shall rent or lease a motor vehicle to any other person unless the latter person is then duly licensed hereunder, or, in the case of a nonresident, then duly licensed under the law of the state or country of residence, except a nonresident whose home state or country does not require that a driver be licensed.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.23.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/13">2720-145i</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/401/#laws.0.24.0">1939 c 401 s 24</a>; 1986 c 444</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.24">
        <h1 class="shn">171.24 VIOLATIONS; DRIVING WITHOUT VALID LICENSE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.24.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Driving after suspension; misdemeanor.</span></h2>
          <p>Except as otherwise provided in subdivision 5, a person is guilty of a misdemeanor if:</p>
          <p>(1) the person's driver's license or driving privilege has been suspended;</p>
          <p>(2) the person has been given notice of or reasonably should know of the suspension; and</p>
          <p>(3) the person disobeys the order by operating in this state any motor vehicle, the operation of which requires a driver's license, while the person's license or privilege is suspended.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.24.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Driving after revocation; misdemeanor.</span></h2>
          <p>A person is guilty of a misdemeanor if:</p>
          <p>(1) the person's driver's license or driving privilege has been revoked;</p>
          <p>(2) the person has been given notice of or reasonably should know of the revocation; and</p>
          <p>(3) the person disobeys the order by operating in this state any motor vehicle, the operation of which requires a driver's license, while the person's license or privilege is revoked.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.24.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Driving after cancellation; misdemeanor.</span></h2>
          <p>A person is guilty of a misdemeanor if:</p>
          <p>(1) the person's driver's license or driving privilege has been canceled;</p>
          <p>(2) the person has been given notice of or reasonably should know of the cancellation; and</p>
          <p>(3) the person disobeys the order by operating in this state any motor vehicle, the operation of which requires a driver's license, while the person's license or privilege is canceled.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.24.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Driving after disqualification; misdemeanor.</span></h2>
          <p>A person is guilty of a misdemeanor if the person:</p>
          <p>(1) has been disqualified from holding a commercial driver's license or been denied the privilege to operate a commercial motor vehicle;</p>
          <p>(2) has been given notice of or reasonably should know of the disqualification; and</p>
          <p>(3) disobeys the order by operating in this state a commercial motor vehicle while the person is disqualified to hold the license or privilege.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.24.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Gross misdemeanor.</span></h2>
          <p>A person is guilty of a gross misdemeanor if:</p>
          <p>(1) the person's driver's license or driving privilege has been canceled or denied under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.04#stat.171.04.1">171.04, subdivision 1</a>, clause (10);</p>
          <p>(2) the person has been given notice of or reasonably should know of the cancellation or denial; and</p>
          <p>(3) the person disobeys the order by operating in this state any motor vehicle, the operation of which requires a driver's license, while the person's license or privilege is canceled or denied.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.24.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Responsibility for prosecution.</span></h2>
          <p>The attorney in the jurisdiction in which the violation occurred who is responsible for prosecution of misdemeanor violations of this section is also responsible for prosecution of gross misdemeanor violations of this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.24.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Sufficiency of notice.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Notice of revocation, suspension, cancellation, or disqualification is sufficient if personally served, or if mailed by first class mail to the person's last known address or to the address listed on the person's driver's license. Notice is also sufficient if the person was informed that revocation, suspension, cancellation, or disqualification would be imposed upon a condition occurring or failing to occur, and where the condition has in fact occurred or failed to occur.</p>
          <p>(b) It is not a defense that a person failed to file a change of address with the post office, or failed to notify the Department of Public Safety of a change of name or address as required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.11">171.11</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.24.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/13">2720-144h</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/13">2720-145j</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/401/#laws.0.23.0">1939 c 401 s 23</a>,25; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1943/0/331/#laws.0.3.0">1943 c 331 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1947/0/479/#laws.0.2.0">1947 c 479 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/1/27/#laws.0.26.0">Ex1971 c 27 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/520/#laws.0.4.0">1980 c 520 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/622/#laws.0.17.0">1984 c 622 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/307/#laws.0.33.0">1989 c 307 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/347/#laws.0.16.0">1993 c 347 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/615/#laws.0.20.0">1994 c 615 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/636/#laws.2.6.0">1994 c 636 art 2 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/12/#laws.3.8.0">1997 c 12 art 3 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/238/#laws.2.91.0">1999 c 238 art 2 s 91</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.2405">
        <h1 class="shn">171.2405 LICENSE REINSTATEMENT DIVERSION PROGRAM.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.2405.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Establishment.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A city or county may establish a license reinstatement diversion program for holders of class D drivers' licenses who have been charged with violating section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.24#stat.171.24.1">171.24, subdivision 1</a> or 2. An individual charged with driving after revocation under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.24#stat.171.24.2">171.24, subdivision 2</a>, is eligible for diversion only if the revocation was due to a violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.791">169.791</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.797">169.797</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.52">169A.52</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.54">169A.54</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.17">171.17, subdivision </a>1, paragraph (a), clause (6); or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.177">171.177</a>. An individual who is a holder of a commercial driver's license or who has committed an offense in a commercial motor vehicle is not eligible to participate in the diversion program. Nothing in this section authorizes the issuance of a driver's license to a diversion program participant during the underlying suspension or revocation period at issue in the violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.24#stat.171.24.1">171.24, subdivision 1</a> or 2.</p>
          <p>(b) Notwithstanding any law or ordinance to the contrary, a city or county may contract with a third party to create and administer the diversion program under this section. Any participating city or county, at its own expense, may request an audit of the administrator.</p>
          <p>(c) For purposes of this section, "administrator" means the city, county, or administrator of the program.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.2405.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Diversion of an individual.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A prosecutor for a participating city or county may determine whether to accept an individual for diversion. When making the determination, the prosecutor must consider:</p>
          <p>(1) whether the individual has a record of driving without a valid license or other criminal record, or has previously participated in a diversion program;</p>
          <p>(2) the strength of the evidence against the individual, along with any mitigating factors; and</p>
          <p>(3) the apparent ability and willingness of the individual to participate in the diversion program and comply with program requirements.</p>
          <p>(b) A city or county attorney may request that an individual be reviewed for a diversion program without a formal city or county diversion program being established. The city or county attorney must follow the requirements of this subdivision and subdivision 1 and may submit the individual's application to an administrator for processing in collaboration with DVS to determine if an individual is eligible for approval into the diversion program. The participant must meet the requirements in subdivision 4.</p>
          <p>(c) A judge may submit a request for an individual to apply for entry into a diversion program under this subdivision and subdivision 1. The participant must meet the requirements in subdivision 4.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.2405.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Diversion driver's license.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Notwithstanding any law to the contrary, the commissioner may issue a diversion driver's license to a person who is a participant in a diversion program, after receiving an application and payment of:</p>
