          <p>(1) the reinstatement fee under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.20#stat.171.20.4">171.20, subdivision 4</a>, by a participant whose driver's license has been suspended;</p>
          <p>(2) the reinstatement fee under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.29#stat.171.29.2">171.29, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (a), by a participant whose driver's license has been revoked under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.791">169.791</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.797">169.797</a>; or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.17#stat.171.17.1">171.17, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a), clause (6); or</p>
          <p>(3) the reinstatement fee under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.29#stat.171.29.2">171.29, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (a), by a participant whose driver's license has been revoked under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.52">169A.52</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.54">169A.54</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.177">171.177</a>. The reinstatement fee and surcharge under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.29#stat.171.29.2">171.29, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (b), must also be paid during the course of and as a condition of the diversion program.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner may impose restrictions on a diversion driver's license that are suitable to the licensee's driving ability or applicable to the licensee as the commissioner deems appropriate to ensure the safe operation of a motor vehicle by the licensee. The participant must follow all requirements of this section, the requirements set out by DVS and court restrictions.</p>
          <p>(c) Payments made by participants in the diversion program of the reinstatement fee and surcharge under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.29#stat.171.29.2">171.29, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (b), must be applied first toward payment of the reinstatement fee and, after the reinstatement fee has been fully paid, toward payment of the surcharge. Each payment that is applied toward the reinstatement fee must be credited as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.29#stat.171.29.2">171.29, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (b), and each payment that is applied toward the surcharge must be credited as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.29">171.29</a>, subdivision 2, paragraphs (c) and (d). After the reinstatement fee and surcharge are satisfied, the participant must pay the program participation fee.</p>
          <p>(d) Notwithstanding any law to the contrary, a diversion driver's license issued to a participant in the program must not be revoked or suspended for convictions entered due to payments made under subdivision 4.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.2405.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Program components.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) At a minimum, the diversion program must require individuals to:</p>
          <p>(1) successfully attend and complete, at the individual's expense, educational classes that provide, among other things, information on driver's licensure;</p>
          <p>(2) pay to the administrator, under a schedule approved by the prosecutor, all required related fees, fines, and charges, including applicable statutory license reinstatement fees and costs of participation in the program;</p>
          <p>(3) comply with all traffic laws; and</p>
          <p>(4) demonstrate compliance with motor vehicle insurance requirements.</p>
          <p>(b) Individuals whose underlying citations cost less than $250 shall receive a 60 percent discount on the diversion program fee. Individuals whose underlying citations cost $250 to $500 shall receive a 40 percent discount on the diversion program fee.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.2405.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Termination of participation; reinstatement of driver's license.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) An individual's participation in the diversion program must be terminated if:</p>
          <p>(1) the individual is found guilty of a moving traffic violation;</p>
          <p>(2) the individual fails to provide proof of vehicle insurance; or</p>
          <p>(3) the administrator of the diversion program informs the commissioner that the individual is no longer satisfying the conditions of the diversion program.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner must cancel an individual's diversion driver's license upon receiving notice from the administrator that the individual is not complying with the requirements of the program.</p>
          <p>(c) The original charge against the individual of a violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.24">171.24</a> may be reinstated against an individual whose participation in the diversion program terminates under paragraph (a), clause (1) or (2).</p>
          <p>(d) If an individual satisfies all requirements of the diversion program, including, at a minimum, satisfactory fulfillment of the components under subdivision 4, the administrator must inform the court, the prosecutor, and the commissioner of the individual's satisfactory completion of the diversion program.</p>
          <p>(e) Upon receiving notice under paragraph (d), the commissioner must reinstate the individual's driver's license.</p>
          <p>(f) Upon receiving notice under paragraph (d), the court must dismiss the charge or the prosecutor must decline to prosecute the individual.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.2405.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Fees held on termination of participant.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Upon termination of the participant in the program under subdivision 5, where there are any held funds and only after the administrator has made payouts on citations and fees, the third-party administrator shall hold remaining participant fees for 12 months from the date of termination under subdivision 5, paragraph (a), clause (1) or (2).</p>
          <p>(b) A participant who meets DVS requirements to re-enter the diversion program may use held funds to pay fees to be reinstated into the program.</p>
          <p>(c) After 12 months, the administrator shall retain the funds for the work performed during the participant's enrollment period, prior to the participant's termination date in the diversion program.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.2405.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Biennial report.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) By February 1 of each even-numbered year, the administrator must report on each city and county that participated in the diversion program and provide a report to each participating city and county, the commissioner, and the legislative committees with jurisdiction over transportation and the judiciary concerning the results of the program. The report must be made available electronically and, upon request, in print. The report must include, without limitation, the effect of the program on:</p>
          <p>(1) recidivism rates for participants in the diversion program;</p>
          <p>(2) the number of participants who successfully completed the program;</p>
          <p>(3) the amount charged to individuals for program fees;</p>
          <p>(4) payment of the fees and fines collected in the diversion program to cities, counties, and the state;</p>
          <p>(5) the total amount of money collected from participants in the program;</p>
          <p>(6) the total amount of money, by category, paid or applied to reinstatement;</p>
          <p>(7) educational support provided to participants in the diversion program;</p>
          <p>(8) the total number of participants in the diversion program;</p>
          <p>(9) the total number of participants terminated from the program under subdivision 5, paragraph (a), clauses (1) to (3);</p>
          <p>(10) the reimbursement policy for all payments listed under clause (4); and</p>
          <p>(11) the amount of all payments listed under clause (4) retained from participants who were terminated from the program.</p>
          <p>(b) The report must include all recommendations made by cities or counties regarding the future of the program and any necessary or suggested legislative changes.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.2405.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/5/#laws.6.5.0">1Sp2019 c 5 art 6 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/55/#laws.1.95.0">2022 c 55 art 1 s 95</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.241">
        <h1 class="shn">171.241 CHAPTER VIOLATIONS; MISDEMEANORS.</h1>
        <p>It is a misdemeanor for any person to willfully violate any of the provisions of this chapter unless the violation is declared by any law to be a felony or gross misdemeanor, or the violation is declared by a section of this chapter to be a misdemeanor.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.241.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/622/#laws.0.18.0">1984 c 622 s 18</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.171.245"><b>171.245</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/727/#laws.0.11.0">1978 c 727 s 11</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.25">
        <h1 class="shn">171.25 ENFORCEMENT; DELEGATION OF AUTHORITY.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner shall be charged with the responsibility for the administration and execution of this chapter.</p>
        <p>Any duties required of or powers conferred on the commissioner under the provisions of this chapter may be done and performed or exercised by any of duly authorized agents.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.25.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/13">2720-145k</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/13">2720-146</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/401/#laws.0.26.0">1939 c 401 s 26</a>,27; 1986 c 444</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.26">
        <h1 class="shn">171.26 MONEY CREDITED TO FUNDS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.26.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Driver and vehicle services operating account.</span></h2>
          <p>Unless otherwise specified, all money received under this chapter must be deposited in the driver and vehicle services operating account under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/299A.705">299A.705</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.26.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>MS 2008 [Expired, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/6/#laws.2.43.0">1Sp2005 c 6 art 2 s 43</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.26.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/13">2720-146a</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/401/#laws.0.28.0">1939 c 401 s 28</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1943/0/610/#laws.0.4.0">1943 c 610 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1947/0/607/#laws.0.1.0">1947 c 607 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/644/#laws.0.14.0">1971 c 644 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/163/#laws.0.37.0">1976 c 163 s 37</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/293/#laws.0.70.0">1983 c 293 s 70</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/233/#laws.0.61.0">1991 c 233 s 61</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/266/#laws.0.27.0">1993 c 266 s 27</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/455/#laws.6.3.0">1996 c 455 art 6 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/136/#laws.18.13.0">2005 c 136 art 18 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/6/#laws.2.43.0">1Sp2005 c 6 art 2 s 43</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/55/#laws.1.96.0">2022 c 55 art 1 s 96</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/25/#laws.0.97.0">2023 c 25 s 97</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/68/#laws.4.60.0">2023 c 68 art 4 s 60</a></p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">MISCELLANEOUS</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.27">
        <h1 class="shn">171.27 LICENSE EXPIRATION AND RENEWAL; EXCEPTIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.27.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Expiration.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Except as otherwise provided in this section, the expiration date for each driver's license is the birthday of the driver in the fourth year following the date of issuance of the license. The birthday of the driver shall be as indicated on the application for a driver's license. A license may be renewed on or before expiration or within one year after expiration upon application, payment of the required fee, and passing the examination required of all drivers for renewal. Driving privileges shall be extended or renewed on or preceding the expiration date of an existing driver's license unless the commissioner believes that the licensee is no longer qualified as a driver.</p>
          <p>(b) The expiration date for each under-21 license shall be the 21st birthday of the licensee. Upon the licensee attaining the age of 21 and upon the application, payment of the required fee, and passing the examination required of all drivers for renewal, a driver's license shall be issued unless the commissioner determines that the licensee is no longer qualified as a driver.</p>
          <p>(c) The expiration date for each provisional license is two years after the date of application for the provisional license.</p>
          <p>(d) Notwithstanding paragraphs (a) to (c), the expiration date for a license issued to a person with temporary lawful status is the last day of the person's legal stay in the United States, or one year after issuance if the last day of the person's legal stay is not identified.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.27.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Extension of expiration.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For purposes of this subdivision, "eligible individual" means:</p>
          <p>(1) a person then or subsequently serving outside Minnesota in active military service, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/190.05#stat.190.05.5">190.05, subdivision 5</a>, in any branch or unit of the armed forces of the United States;</p>
          <p>(2) a person then or subsequently serving outside Minnesota as a volunteer in the Peace Corps;</p>
          <p>(3) a person who is an employee of a federal department or agency and is assigned to foreign service outside of the United States; or</p>
          <p>(4) a person residing outside of Minnesota because the person is a spouse, domestic partner, or dependent under age 26 of a person in clause (1), (2), or (3).</p>
          <p>(b) A valid Minnesota driver's license issued to an eligible individual continues in full force and effect without requirement for renewal until the date one year following the person's separation or discharge from active military or volunteer service, or following the conclusion of assignment to foreign service outside the United States, and until the license holder's birthday in the fourth full year following the person's most recent license renewal or, in the case of a provisional license, until the person's birthday in the third full year following the renewal.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.27.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/13">2720-146b</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/401/#laws.0.29.0">1939 c 401 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1943/0/610/#laws.0.1.0">1943 c 610 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1947/0/479/#laws.0.3.0">1947 c 479 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1951/0/29/#laws.0.1.0">1951 c 29 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/0/564/#laws.0.6.0">1957 c 564 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/846/#laws.0.2.0">1971 c 846 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/698/#laws.0.7.0">1973 c 698 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/725/#laws.0.24.0">1973 c 725 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/0/145/#laws.0.1.0">1985 c 145 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/454/#laws.0.25.0">1986 c 454 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/301/#laws.0.11.0">1989 c 301 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/388/#laws.0.28.0">1998 c 388 s 28</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/0/54/#laws.0.1.0">2011 c 54 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/76/#laws.0.23.0">2017 c 76 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/5/#laws.4.82.0">1Sp2021 c 5 art 4 s 82</a></p>
      </div>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>(a) The amendment to this section by Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 23, was effective May 19, 2017, and applies to driver's licenses and Minnesota identification cards applied for and issued on or after October 1, 2018. Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 29.</p>
      <p>(b) If the Federal REAL ID Act, Public Law 109-13, Division B, is repealed, or if the definition of "official purpose" in Code of Federal Regulations, title 6, part 37, is amended, the amendment to this section by Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 23, is repealed on June 30 of the following year. Laws 2017, chapter 76, section 27.</p>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.171.28"><b>171.28</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/255/#laws.0.21.0">2014 c 255 s 21</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.29">
        <h1 class="shn">171.29 REVOKED LICENSE; CONDITIONS FOR REINSTATEMENT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.29.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Examination required.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) No person whose driver's license has been revoked by reason of conviction, plea of guilty, or forfeiture of bail not vacated, under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.791">169.791</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.797">169.797</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.17">171.17</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.172">171.172</a>, or revoked under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.792">169.792</a> shall be issued another license unless and until that person shall have successfully passed an examination as required by the commissioner of public safety. This subdivision does not apply to an applicant for early reinstatement under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.792#stat.169.792.7a">169.792, subdivision 7a</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) The requirement to successfully pass the examination described in paragraph (a) does not apply to a person whose driver's license has been revoked because of an impaired driving offense.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.29.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Reinstatement fees and surcharges allocated and appropriated.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) An individual whose driver's license has been revoked by reason of one or more convictions, pleas of guilty, forfeitures of bail not vacated, or mandatory revocations under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.791">169.791</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.792">169.792</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.797">169.797</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.17">171.17</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.172">171.172</a>, and who is otherwise eligible for reinstatement must pay a single $30 fee before the driver's license is reinstated. An individual whose driver's license has been revoked under provisions specified in both this paragraph and paragraph (b) must pay the reinstatement fee as provided in paragraph (b).</p>
          <p class="in">(b) A person whose driver's license has been revoked under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.52">169A.52</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.54">169A.54</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.177">171.177</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2112">609.2112</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2113">609.2113</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2114">609.2114</a>, or Minnesota Statutes 2012, section 609.21, must pay a $250 fee plus a $430 surcharge for each instance of revocation before the driver's license is reinstated, except as provided in paragraph (f). The $250 fee must be credited as follows:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) 20 percent to the driver and vehicle services operating account under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/299A.705">299A.705</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) 67 percent to the general fund;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) eight percent to a separate account to be known as the Bureau of Criminal Apprehension account. Money in this account is annually appropriated to the commissioner of public safety and the appropriated amount must be apportioned 80 percent for laboratory costs and 20 percent for carrying out the provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/299C.065">299C.065</a>; and</p>
          <p class="in">(4) five percent to a separate account to be known as the vehicle forfeiture account, which is created in the special revenue fund. The money in the account is annually appropriated to the commissioner for costs of handling vehicle forfeitures.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The revenue from $50 of the surcharge must be credited to a separate account to be known as the traumatic brain injury and spinal cord injury account. The revenue from $50 of the surcharge on a reinstatement under paragraph (f) is credited from the first installment payment to the traumatic brain injury and spinal cord injury account. The money in the account is annually appropriated to the commissioner of health to be used as follows: 83 percent for contracts with a qualified community-based organization to provide information, resources, and support to assist persons with traumatic brain injury and their families to access services, and 17 percent to maintain the traumatic brain injury and spinal cord injury registry created in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.662">144.662</a>. For the purposes of this paragraph, a "qualified community-based organization" is a private, not-for-profit organization of consumers of traumatic brain injury services and their family members. The organization must be registered with the United States Internal Revenue Service under section 501(c)(3) as a tax-exempt organization and must have as its purposes:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the promotion of public, family, survivor, and professional awareness of the incidence and consequences of traumatic brain injury;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the provision of a network of support for persons with traumatic brain injury, their families, and friends;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the development and support of programs and services to prevent traumatic brain injury;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) the establishment of education programs for persons with traumatic brain injury; and</p>
          <p class="in">(5) the empowerment of persons with traumatic brain injury through participation in its governance.</p>
          <p class="bl">A patient's name, identifying information, or identifiable medical data must not be disclosed to the organization without the informed voluntary written consent of the patient or patient's guardian or, if the patient is a minor, of the parent or guardian of the patient.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) The remainder of the surcharge must be credited to a separate account to be known as the remote electronic alcohol-monitoring program account. The commissioner shall transfer the balance of this account to the commissioner of management and budget on a monthly basis for deposit in the general fund.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) When these fees are collected by a driver's license agent, appointed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.061">171.061</a>, a filing fee is imposed in the amount specified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.061#stat.171.061.4">171.061, subdivision 4</a>. The reinstatement fees, surcharge, and filing fee must be deposited in an approved depository as directed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.061#stat.171.061.4">171.061, subdivision 4</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(f) A person whose driver's license has been revoked as provided in subdivision 1 under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.52">169A.52</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.54">169A.54</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.177">171.177</a> may choose to pay 50 percent and an additional $25 of the total amount of the surcharge and 50 percent of the fee required under paragraph (b) to reinstate the person's driver's license, provided the person meets all other requirements of reinstatement. If a person chooses to pay 50 percent of the total and an additional $25, the driver's license must expire after two years. The person must pay an additional 50 percent less $25 of the total to extend the license for an additional two years, provided the person is otherwise still eligible for the license. After this final payment of the surcharge and fee, the license may be renewed on a standard schedule, as provided under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.27">171.27</a>. A filing fee may be imposed for each installment payment. Revenue from the filing fee is credited to the driver and vehicle services operating account under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/299A.705">299A.705</a> and is appropriated to the commissioner.</p>
          <p class="in">(g) Any person making installment payments under paragraph (f), whose driver's license subsequently expires, or is canceled, revoked, or suspended before payment of 100 percent of the surcharge and fee, must pay the outstanding balance due for the initial reinstatement before the driver's license is subsequently reinstated. Upon payment of the outstanding balance due for the initial reinstatement, the person may pay any new surcharge and fee imposed under paragraph (b) in installment payments as provided under paragraph (f).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.29.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Compliance with impoundment laws.</span></h2>
          <p>A person whose license has been revoked under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.52">169A.52</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.54">169A.54</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.177">171.177</a> may not be issued another license at the end of the revocation period unless the person has complied with all applicable registration plate impoundment provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/168.041">168.041</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.60">169A.60</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.29.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/0/515/#laws.0.1.0">1959 c 515 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/363/#laws.0.40.0">1981 c 363 s 40</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/293/#laws.0.71.0">1983 c 293 s 71</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/483/#laws.0.1.0">1994 c 483 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/501/#laws.0.9.0">1994 c 501 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/226/#laws.5.2.0">1995 c 226 art 5 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/8/#laws.2.48.0">1Sp2001 c 8 art 2 s 48</a>; art 11 s 12; art 12 s 15; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/9/#laws.19.13.0">1Sp2001 c 9 art 19 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/83/#laws.3.18.0">2017 c 83 art 3 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/5/#laws.4.83.0">1Sp2021 c 5 art 4 s 83</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/11/#laws.2.7.0">1Sp2021 c 11 art 2 s 7</a>;       <div class="section" id="stat.171.30">
        <h1 class="shn">171.30 LIMITED LICENSE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.30.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Conditions of issuance.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner may issue a limited license to the driver under the conditions in paragraph (b) in any case where a person's license has been:</p>
          <p>(1) suspended under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.18">171.18</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.173">171.173</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.186">171.186</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.187">171.187</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) revoked, canceled, or denied under section:</p>
          <p>(i) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.792">169.792</a>;</p>
          <p>(ii) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.797">169.797</a>;</p>
          <p>(iii) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.52">169A.52</a>:</p>
          <p>(A) subdivision 3, paragraph (a), clause (1) or (2); or</p>
          <p>(B) subdivision 4, paragraph (a), clause (1) or (2), if the test results indicate an alcohol concentration of less than twice the legal limit;</p>
          <p>(iv) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.17">171.17</a>; or</p>
          <p>(v) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.172">171.172</a>;</p>
          <p>(3) revoked, canceled, or denied under section 169A.54:</p>
          <p>(i) subdivision 1, clause (1), if the test results indicate an alcohol concentration of less than twice the legal limit;</p>
          <p>(ii) subdivision 1, clause (2); or</p>
          <p>(iii) subdivision 2, if the person does not have a qualified prior impaired driving incident as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.03#stat.169A.03.22">169A.03, subdivision 22</a>, on the person's record, and the test results indicate an alcohol concentration of less than twice the legal limit; or</p>
          <p>(4) revoked, canceled, or denied under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.177">171.177</a>:</p>
          <p>(i) subdivision 4, paragraph (a), clause (1) or (2); or</p>
          <p>(ii) subdivision 5, paragraph (a), clause (1) or (2), if the test results indicate an alcohol concentration of less than twice the legal limit.</p>
          <p>(b) The following conditions for a limited license under paragraph (a) include:</p>
          <p>(1) if the driver's livelihood or attendance at a substance use disorder treatment or counseling program depends upon the use of the driver's license;</p>
          <p>(2) if the use of a driver's license by a homemaker is necessary to prevent the substantial disruption of the education, medical, or nutritional needs of the family of the homemaker; or</p>
          <p>(3) if attendance at a postsecondary institution of education by an enrolled student of that institution depends upon the use of the driver's license.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner in issuing a limited license may impose such conditions and limitations as in the commissioner's judgment are necessary to the interests of the public safety and welfare including reexamination as to the driver's qualifications. The license may be limited to the operation of particular vehicles, to particular classes and times of operation, and to particular conditions of traffic. The commissioner may require that an applicant for a limited license affirmatively demonstrate that use of public transportation or carpooling as an alternative to a limited license would be a significant hardship.</p>
          <p>(d) For purposes of this subdivision:</p>
          <p>(1) "homemaker" refers to the person primarily performing the domestic tasks in a household of residents consisting of at least the person and the person's dependent child or other dependents; and</p>
          <p>(2) "twice the legal limit" means an alcohol concentration of two times the limit specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.20#stat.169A.20.1">169A.20, subdivision 1</a>, clause (5).</p>
          <p>(e) The limited license issued by the commissioner shall clearly indicate the limitations imposed and the driver operating under the limited license shall have the license in possession at all times when operating as a driver.</p>
          <p>(f) In determining whether to issue a limited license, the commissioner shall consider the number and the seriousness of prior convictions and the entire driving record of the driver and shall consider the number of miles driven by the driver annually.</p>
          <p>(g) If the person's driver's license or permit to drive has been revoked under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.792">169.792</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.797">169.797</a>, the commissioner may only issue a limited license to the person after the person has presented an insurance identification card, policy, or written statement indicating that the driver or owner has insurance coverage satisfactory to the commissioner of public safety. The commissioner of public safety may require the insurance identification card provided to satisfy this subdivision be certified by the insurance company to be noncancelable for a period not to exceed 12 months.</p>
          <p>(h) The limited license issued by the commissioner to a person under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.186#stat.171.186.4">171.186, subdivision 4</a>, must expire 90 days after the date it is issued. The commissioner must not issue a limited license to a person who previously has been issued a limited license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.186#stat.171.186.4">171.186, subdivision 4</a>.</p>
          <p>(i) The commissioner shall not issue a limited driver's license to any person described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.04#stat.171.04.1">171.04, subdivision 1</a>, clause (6), (7), (8), (11), or (14).</p>
          <p>(j) The commissioner shall not issue a class A, class B, or class C limited license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.30.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">60-day waiting period.</span></h2>
          <p>A limited license shall not be issued for a period of 60 days to an individual whose license or privilege has been revoked or suspended for commission of the following offenses:</p>
          <p>(1) any felony in the commission of which a motor vehicle was used; or</p>
          <p>(2) failure to stop and disclose identity as required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.09">169.09</a>, in the event of a motor vehicle accident resulting in the death or personal injury of another.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.30.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Other waiting periods.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding subdivision 2, a limited license must not be issued for a period of:</p>
          <p>(1) 15 days, to a person whose license or privilege has been revoked or suspended for a first violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.20">169A.20</a>, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.50">169A.50</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.53">169A.53</a>, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.177">171.177</a>, or a statute or ordinance from another state in conformity with either of those sections; or</p>
          <p>(2) one year, to a person whose license or privilege has been revoked or suspended for:</p>
          <p>(i) committing manslaughter resulting from the operation of a motor vehicle; or</p>
          <p>(ii) committing criminal vehicular operation under section:</p>
          <p>(A) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2113#stat.609.2113.1">609.2113, subdivision 1</a>, 2, or 3; or</p>
          <p>(B) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2114#stat.609.2114.2">609.2114, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.30.2b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2b.<span class="headnote">Waiting period for youth under 18.</span></h2>
          <p>If a person whose driver's license was suspended or revoked for a violation listed under subdivision 2 or 2a is under the age of 18 years at the time of that violation, the commissioner shall not issue a limited license to the person for a period of time that is the longest of: (1) 90 days; or (2) twice the length of the period specified for that violation in subdivision 2 or 2a.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.30.2c">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2c.</h2>
          <p>MS 2010 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/366/#laws.0.17.0">2010 c 366 s 17</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.30.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/371/#laws.1.64.0">2002 c 371 art 1 s 64</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.30.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Penalty.</span></h2>
          <p>A person who violates a condition or limitation of a limited license issued under subdivision 1 or fails to have the license in immediate possession at all times when operating a motor vehicle is guilty of a misdemeanor. In addition, except as otherwise provided in the ignition interlock program under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.306">171.306</a>, a person who violates a condition or limitation of a limited license may not operate a motor vehicle for the remainder of the period of suspension or revocation, or 30 days, whichever is longer.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.30.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Exception; criminal vehicular homicide.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding subdivision 1, the commissioner may not issue a limited license to a person whose driver's license has been suspended or revoked due to:</p>
          <p>(1) a violation under section:</p>
          <p>(i) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2112#stat.609.2112.1">609.2112, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a);</p>
          <p>(ii) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2114#stat.609.2114.1">609.2114, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a); or</p>
          <p>(iii) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.13#stat.169.13.1">169.13, subdivision 1</a>, that contributed to causing death to another; or</p>
          <p>(2) a statute or ordinance from another state in conformity with the offenses under clause (1).</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="section" id="stat.171.301">
        <h1 class="shn">171.301 REINTEGRATION LICENSE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.301.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Conditions of issuance.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner may issue a reintegration driver's license to any person:</p>
          <p>(1) who is 18 years of age or older;</p>
          <p>(2) who has been released from a period of at least 180 consecutive days of confinement or incarceration in:</p>
          <p>(i) an adult correctional facility under the control of the commissioner of corrections or licensed by the commissioner of corrections under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/241.021">241.021</a>;</p>
          <p>(ii) a federal correctional facility for adults; or</p>
          <p>(iii) an adult correctional facility operated under the control or supervision of any other state; and</p>
          <p>(3) whose license has been suspended or revoked under the circumstances listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.30#stat.171.30.1">171.30, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a), clauses (1) to (4), for a violation that occurred before the individual was incarcerated for the period described in clause (2).</p>
          <p>(b) If the person's driver's license or permit to drive has been revoked under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.792">169.792</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169.797">169.797</a>, the commissioner may only issue a reintegration driver's license to the person after the person has presented an insurance identification card, policy, or written statement indicating that the driver or owner has insurance coverage satisfactory to the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(c) If the person's driver's license or permit to drive has been suspended under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.186">171.186</a>, the commissioner may only issue a reintegration driver's license to the person after the commissioner receives notice of a court order provided pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518A.65">518A.65</a>, paragraph (e), showing that the person's driver's license or operating privileges should no longer be suspended.</p>
          <p>(d) If the person's driver's license has been revoked under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.17">171.17, subdivision </a>1, paragraph (a), clause (1), the commissioner may only issue a reintegration driver's license to the person after the person has completed the applicable revocation period.</p>
          <p>(e) The commissioner must not issue a reintegration driver's license:</p>
          <p>(1) to any person described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.04#stat.171.04.1">171.04, subdivision 1</a>, clause (7), (8), (10), or (11);</p>
          <p>(2) to any person described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.55#stat.169A.55.5">169A.55, subdivision 5</a>;</p>
          <p>(3) if the person has committed a violation after the person was released from custody that results in the suspension, revocation, or cancellation of a driver's license, including suspension for nonpayment of child support or maintenance payments as described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.186#stat.171.186.1">171.186, subdivision 1</a>; or</p>
          <p>(4) if the issuance would conflict with the requirements of the nonresident violator compact.</p>
          <p>(f) The commissioner must not issue a class A, class B, or class C reintegration driver's license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.301.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Application.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Application for a reintegration driver's license must be made in the form and manner approved by the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(b) A person seeking a reintegration driver's license who was released from confinement or incarceration on or after April 1, 2024, must apply for the license within one year of release. A person seeking a reintegration driver's license who was released from confinement or incarceration before April 1, 2024, must apply for the license by April 1, 2025.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.301.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Fees prohibited.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For a reintegration driver's license under this section:</p>
          <p>(1) the commissioner must not impose:</p>
          <p>(i) a fee, surcharge, or filing fee under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.06#stat.171.06.2">171.06, subdivision 2</a>;</p>
          <p>(ii) a reinstatement fee under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.20#stat.171.20.4">171.20, subdivision 4</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.29#stat.171.29.2">171.29, subdivision 2</a>; or</p>
          <p>(iii) an endorsement fee under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.06#stat.171.06.2a">171.06, subdivision 2a</a>; and</p>
          <p>(2) a driver's license agent must not impose a filing fee under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.061">171.061, subdivision </a>4.</p>
          <p>(b) Issuance of a reintegration driver's license does not forgive or otherwise discharge any unpaid fees or fines.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.301.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Cancellation of license.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner must cancel the reintegration driver's license of any person who commits a violation that would result in the suspension, revocation, or cancellation of a driver's license, including suspension for nonpayment of child support or maintenance payments as described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.186#stat.171.186.1">171.186, subdivision 1</a>. The commissioner must not cancel a reintegration driver's license for payment of a fine or resolution of a criminal charge if the underlying incident occurred before the reintegration driver's license was issued, unless the conviction would have made the person ineligible to receive a reintegration driver' s license. Except as described in paragraph (b), a person whose reintegration driver's license is canceled under this subdivision may not be issued another reintegration driver's license and may not operate a motor vehicle for the remainder of the period of suspension or revocation or 30 days, whichever is longer.</p>
          <p>(b) A person whose reintegration driver's license is canceled under paragraph (a) may apply for a new reintegration driver's license if the person is incarcerated or confined for a period of at least 180 consecutive days after the cancellation and the person meets the conditions described in subdivision 1.</p>
          <p>(c) Nothing in this section prohibits cancellation and reinstatement of a reintegration driver's license for any other reason described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.14">171.14</a> provided any factor making the person not eligible for a driver's license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.04">171.04</a> occurred or became known to the commissioner after issuance of the reintegration driver's license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.301.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Expiration.</span></h2>
          <p>A reintegration driver's license expires 15 months from the date of issuance of the license. A reintegration driver's license may not be renewed.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.171.301.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Issuance of regular driver's license.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Notwithstanding any statute or rule to the contrary, the commissioner must issue a REAL ID-compliant or noncompliant license to a person who possesses a reintegration driver's license if:</p>
          <p>(1) the person has possessed the reintegration driver's license for at least one full year;</p>
          <p>(2) the reintegration driver's license has not been canceled under subdivision 4 and has not expired under subdivision 5;</p>
          <p>(3) the person meets the application requirements under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.06">171.06</a>, including payment of the applicable fees, surcharge, and filing fee under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.06">171.06, subdivisions 2</a> and 2a, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.061#stat.171.061.4">171.061, subdivision 4</a>; and</p>
          <p>(4) issuance of the license does not conflict with the requirements of the nonresident violator compact.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner must forgive any outstanding balance due on a reinstatement fee or surcharge under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.20#stat.171.20.4">171.20, subdivision 4</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.29#stat.171.29.2">171.29, subdivision 2</a>, for a person who is eligible and applies for a license under paragraph (a).</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.301.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/68/#laws.4.61.0">2023 c 68 art 4 s 61</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.3.72.0">2024 c 127 art 3 s 72</a>,73</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.171.302">
        <h1 class="shn">171.302 LICENSE VIOLATIONS; CONSECUTIVE SENTENCING.</h1>
        <p>When sentencing an offender for violating section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.20#stat.171.20.2">171.20, subdivision 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.24">171.24</a>; or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.30">171.30</a>, the court may impose consecutive sentences for offenses arising out of a single course of conduct as permitted in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.035#stat.609.035.2">609.035, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.171.302.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/442/#laws.0.23.0">1996 c 442 s 23</a>
        </p>
      </div>
